=== FILE: README.md ===
# HiBlue · versiunea 0.0.13

HiBlue este o aplicație Android hibridă: Android/Kotlin ca host, interfață HTML/CSS/JavaScript în WebView, GamePlatform pentru jocuri și Supabase pentru serviciile online.

## Jocuri active
- **Flyby** — arcade, runtime web.
- **Flip Nova** — arcade, runtime web.

În această versiune au fost eliminate complet cele trei prototipuri abandonate, împreună cu resursele și înregistrările specifice acestora. Infrastructura generală GamePlatform și adaptorul generic pentru jocuri native au fost păstrate.

## Build APK
Workflow-ul GitHub Actions **Build APK** reconstruiește proiectul pornind de la `hiblue.txt` și aplică update-urile în ordine. Pentru versiunea 0.0.13, build-ul produce APK-ul și noul artefact `hiblue-txt-0.0.12`.

## Arhitectură și extensii
Documentația tehnică se află în `docs/`. Pentru un joc web nou, urmează `docs/ADDING_WEB_GAME.md` și `docs/GAME_SDK.md`. Pentru integrarea unui joc Android nativ, vezi `docs/NATIVE_GAMES.md`.

## Notă privind datele externe
Acest update elimină codul jocurilor din proiectul Android. Nu șterge automat rânduri, scoruri sau ratinguri din proiectul Supabase deja configurat și nu șterge datele istorice din Git.


## Social și Chat (0.0.13)

Integrarea reunește Feed, postări, Stories, profiluri, căutare și compunere, plus conversații directe și bula plutitoare Android. Pentru backend, rulează în Supabase SQL Editor, în ordine, `supabase/update0.0.10.sql` și apoi `supabase/update0.0.11.sql` (o singură dată fiecare). Migrațiile nu sunt executate automat de build. Mesajele nu folosesc criptare end-to-end.
=== FILE: build-yml.txt ===
name: Build APK
on: [push, workflow_dispatch]
jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with: {distribution: temurin, java-version: 17}
      - uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v3
        with: {gradle-version: 8.7}
      - run: gradle assembleDebug
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with: {name: HiBlue-apk, path: app/build/outputs/apk/debug/*.apk}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/ro/lingurar/hiblue/MainActivity.kt ===
package ro.lingurar.hiblue

import android.Manifest
import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
import android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.graphics.Color
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.view.WindowManager
import android.webkit.*
import android.widget.FrameLayout
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.view.ViewCompat
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
import androidx.core.view.WindowInsetsCompat
import androidx.core.view.WindowInsetsControllerCompat
import org.json.JSONObject
import ro.lingurar.hiblue.bridge.HostActions
import ro.lingurar.hiblue.bubble.BubbleService
import ro.lingurar.hiblue.bridge.HostBridge
import ro.lingurar.hiblue.host.DeviceServices
import ro.lingurar.hiblue.nativegames.NativeGames

/**
 * Android Host: ciclul de viață, WebView, inset-uri, Back, permisiuni și puntea către frontend.
 * Interfața (UI) rămâne în aplicația web din assets; aici NU se construiește UI.
 *
 * Comunicare:
 *   JS -> Kotlin : window.HBNative.invoke(json)   (vezi bridge/HostBridge.kt)
 *   Kotlin -> JS : NativeBridge._emit(tip, date)  (lifecycle, permission, nativeGame)
 */
class MainActivity : Activity() {
    private companion object {
        const val ASSET_PREFIX = "file:///android_asset/"
        const val START_URL = "file:///android_asset/index.html"
        const val REQ_NOTIF = 7
        const val REQ_NATIVE_GAME = 9
        const val REQ_FILE = 11
    }

    private lateinit var wv: WebView
    private lateinit var device: DeviceServices
    private var lastBack = 0L
    private var insTop = 0
    private var insBottom = 0
    @Volatile private var pendingNativeId: String? = null
    private var fileCallback: ValueCallback<Array<Uri>>? = null
    @Volatile private var pendingChat: String? = null

    /** Setat doar din firul UI (onPageStarted); citit din firul JavaBridge. */
    @Volatile private var pageTrusted = false

    private val actions = object : HostActions {
        override fun trustedPage(): Boolean = pageTrusted

        override fun vibrate(kind: Int) {
            runOnUiThread { device.vibrate(kind) }
        }

        override fun notificationsGranted(): Boolean = device.notificationsGranted()

        override fun requestNotifications() {
            runOnUiThread {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33 && !device.notificationsGranted()) {
                    requestPermissions(arrayOf(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS), REQ_NOTIF)
                }
            }
        }

        override fun notify(title: String, body: String): Boolean = device.notify(title, body)

        override fun share(text: String): Boolean {
            runOnUiThread {
                try {
                    val i = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND)
                    i.type = "text/plain"
                    i.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, text)
                    startActivity(Intent.createChooser(i, null))
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                }
            }
            return true
        }

        override fun bubbleStatus(): Pair<Boolean, Boolean> =
            Pair(BubbleService.canDraw(this@MainActivity), BubbleService.active)

        override fun requestBubblePermission() {
            runOnUiThread {
                try { startActivity(BubbleService.permissionIntent(this@MainActivity)) } catch (e: Exception) { }
            }
        }

        override fun bubbleShow(conv: String, title: String, avatar: String, unread: Int): String? {
            if (!BubbleService.canDraw(this@MainActivity)) return "PERMISSION_DENIED"
            return try {
                BubbleService.show(this@MainActivity, conv, title, avatar, unread)
                null
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                "INTERNAL"
            }
        }

        override fun bubbleUpdate(unread: Int) { BubbleService.update(unread) }

        override fun bubbleHide() { BubbleService.hide(this@MainActivity) }

        override fun takePendingChat(): String? {
            val c = pendingChat
            pendingChat = null
            return c
        }

        override fun setOrientation(mode: String): Boolean {
            val v = when (mode) {
                "portrait" -> ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_PORTRAIT
                "landscape" -> ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_SENSOR_LANDSCAPE
                "auto" -> ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
                else -> return false
            }
            runOnUiThread { requestedOrientation = v }
            return true
        }

        override fun nativeGames(): List<Pair<String, String>> =
            NativeGames.list().map { Pair(it.id, it.runtime) }

        override fun launchNativeGame(id: String): String? {
            val entry = NativeGames.get(id) ?: return "NOT_REGISTERED"
            if (pendingNativeId != null) return "BUSY"
            pendingNativeId = id
            runOnUiThread {
                try {
                    val i = entry.intentFactory(this@MainActivity)
                    i.putExtra(NativeGames.EXTRA_GAME_ID, id)
                    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                    startActivityForResult(i, REQ_NATIVE_GAME)
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    pendingNativeId = null
                    emit("nativeGame", JSONObject().put("id", id).put("status", "error"))
                }
            }
            return null
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(b: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(b)
        NativeGames.registerBuiltIn()
        device = DeviceServices(this)
        pendingChat = intent?.getStringExtra(BubbleService.OPEN_CHAT_EXTRA)
        BubbleService.onEvent = { state, conv ->
            runOnUiThread { emit("bubble", JSONObject().put("state", state).put("conv", conv ?: "")) }
        }

        val bg = Color.parseColor("#05050F")
        window.statusBarColor = bg
        window.navigationBarColor = bg
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28) {
            val lp = window.attributes
            lp.layoutInDisplayCutoutMode = WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_SHORT_EDGES
            window.attributes = lp
        }
        WindowCompat.setDecorFitsSystemWindows(window, false)

        wv = WebView(this)
        val root = FrameLayout(this)
        root.setBackgroundColor(bg)
        root.addView(wv, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))
        setContentView(root)
        wv.setBackgroundColor(bg)

        // Tastatura împinge conținutul în sus; marginile (notch / bare) ajung în CSS ca --sat / --sab
        ViewCompat.setOnApplyWindowInsetsListener(root) { v, ins ->
            val ime = ins.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
            val bars = ins.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars() or WindowInsetsCompat.Type.displayCutout())
            v.setPadding(0, 0, 0, ime.bottom)
            insTop = bars.top
            insBottom = if (ime.bottom > 0) 0 else bars.bottom
            pushInsets()
            WindowInsetsCompat.CONSUMED
        }

        configureWebView()
        wv.addJavascriptInterface(HostBridge(actions, appVersion()), HostBridge.NAME)
        wv.webViewClient = object : WebViewClient() {
            override fun onPageStarted(v: WebView?, url: String?, favicon: android.graphics.Bitmap?) {
                pageTrusted = url != null && url.startsWith(ASSET_PREFIX)
            }

            override fun onPageFinished(v: WebView?, url: String?) { pushInsets() }

            override fun shouldOverrideUrlLoading(v: WebView?, r: WebResourceRequest?): Boolean {
                val u = r?.url?.toString() ?: return true
                if (u.startsWith(ASSET_PREFIX) || u == "about:blank") return false
                // Conținutul extern nu se încarcă niciodată în WebView-ul cu punte: se deschide în browser.
                if (u.startsWith("https://") || u.startsWith("http://") || u.startsWith("mailto:") || u.startsWith("tel:")) {
                    try { startActivity(Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(u))) } catch (e: Exception) {}
                }
                return true
            }

            override fun onRenderProcessGone(view: WebView?, detail: RenderProcessGoneDetail?): Boolean {
                // Procesul de randare a murit (memorie / crash): refacem activitatea în loc să cădem aplicația.
                pageTrusted = false
                Toast.makeText(this@MainActivity, "Aplicația a fost repornită", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                recreate()
                return true
            }
        }
        wv.webChromeClient = object : WebChromeClient() {
            /** Selector de poze/video pentru <input type="file"> (Social: postări, stories, avatar). Doar din pagina locală. */
            override fun onShowFileChooser(v: WebView?, cb: ValueCallback<Array<Uri>>?, p: WebChromeClient.FileChooserParams?): Boolean {
                if (cb == null || p == null || !pageTrusted) { cb?.onReceiveValue(null); return false }
                fileCallback?.onReceiveValue(null)
                fileCallback = cb
                return try {
                    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                    startActivityForResult(p.createIntent(), REQ_FILE)
                    true
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    fileCallback = null
                    cb.onReceiveValue(null)
                    false
                }
            }

            override fun onConsoleMessage(m: ConsoleMessage?): Boolean {
                m?.let { android.util.Log.d("HiBlueJS", "[${it.messageLevel()}] ${it.message()} @${it.sourceId()}:${it.lineNumber()}") }
                return true
            }
        }
        if (b != null) wv.restoreState(b) else wv.loadUrl(START_URL)
        hideBars()
    }

    private fun configureWebView() {
        if ((applicationInfo.flags and ApplicationInfo.FLAG_DEBUGGABLE) != 0) {
            WebView.setWebContentsDebuggingEnabled(true)
        }
        wv.settings.apply {
            javaScriptEnabled = true
            domStorageEnabled = true
            allowFileAccess = false
            allowContentAccess = false
            allowFileAccessFromFileURLs = false
            allowUniversalAccessFromFileURLs = false
            javaScriptCanOpenWindowsAutomatically = false
            setSupportMultipleWindows(false)
            setGeolocationEnabled(false)
            mixedContentMode = WebSettings.MIXED_CONTENT_NEVER_ALLOW
            textZoom = 100
            setSupportZoom(false)
            mediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture = false
        }
        wv.overScrollMode = android.view.View.OVER_SCROLL_NEVER
    }

    private fun appVersion(): String = try {
        packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, 0).versionName ?: "?"
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        "?"
    }

    /** Kotlin -> JS. Apelat doar din firul UI. Datele sunt JSON controlat de host (numere / id-uri). */
    private fun emit(type: String, data: JSONObject) {
        val js = "window.NativeBridge&&NativeBridge._emit(" + JSONObject.quote(type) + "," + data.toString() + ")"
        wv.evaluateJavascript(js, null)
    }

    private fun pushInsets() {
        val d = resources.displayMetrics.density
        wv.evaluateJavascript("var s=document.documentElement.style;s.setProperty('--sat','${insTop / d}px');s.setProperty('--sab','${insBottom / d}px')", null)
    }

    /** Full screen: bare de sistem ascunse (apar la swipe de la margine) */
    private fun hideBars() {
        val c = WindowInsetsControllerCompat(window, window.decorView)
        c.systemBarsBehavior = WindowInsetsControllerCompat.BEHAVIOR_SHOW_TRANSIENT_BARS_BY_SWIPE
        c.hide(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
    }

    override fun onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus: Boolean) {
        super.onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus)
        if (hasFocus) hideBars()
    }

    override fun onPause() {
        // Întâi anunțăm frontend-ul (jocurile se opresc), apoi oprim WebView-ul.
        if (pageTrusted) emit("lifecycle", JSONObject().put("state", "pause"))
        BubbleService.setAppVisible(false)
        // Cât bula de chat e activă, WebView-ul rămâne pornit ca să se verifice mesajele noi.
        if (!BubbleService.active) wv.onPause()
        super.onPause()
    }

    override fun onNewIntent(intent: Intent?) {
        super.onNewIntent(intent)
        setIntent(intent)
        val c = intent?.getStringExtra(BubbleService.OPEN_CHAT_EXTRA)
        if (c != null && c.isNotEmpty()) {
            if (pageTrusted) emit("openChat", JSONObject().put("conv", c)) else pendingChat = c
        }
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        BubbleService.setAppVisible(true)
        wv.onResume()
        if (pageTrusted) emit("lifecycle", JSONObject().put("state", "resume"))
    }

    override fun onRequestPermissionsResult(code: Int, p: Array<out String>, r: IntArray) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(code, p, r)
        if (code == REQ_NOTIF) {
            val ok = r.isNotEmpty() && r[0] == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
            emit("permission", JSONObject().put("name", "notifications").put("granted", ok))
        }
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    override fun onActivityResult(code: Int, result: Int, data: Intent?) {
        super.onActivityResult(code, result, data)
        if (code == REQ_FILE) {
            val cb = fileCallback
            fileCallback = null
            cb?.onReceiveValue(WebChromeClient.FileChooserParams.parseResult(result, data))
            return
        }
        if (code != REQ_NATIVE_GAME) return
        val id = pendingNativeId ?: return
        pendingNativeId = null
        val o = JSONObject().put("id", id)
        if (result == RESULT_OK && data != null) {
            val score = data.getIntExtra(NativeGames.EXTRA_SCORE, -1)
            val secs = data.getIntExtra(NativeGames.EXTRA_SECONDS, 0)
            o.put("status", "finished").put("score", if (score < 0) 0 else score).put("seconds", if (secs < 0) 0 else secs)
        } else {
            o.put("status", "cancelled")
        }
        emit("nativeGame", o)
    }

    override fun onSaveInstanceState(o: Bundle) { super.onSaveInstanceState(o); wv.saveState(o) }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    override fun onBackPressed() {
        wv.evaluateJavascript("(window.HB&&HB.back)?HB.back():false") { r ->
            if (r == "true") return@evaluateJavascript
            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
            if (now - lastBack < 2000) finish()
            else { lastBack = now; Toast.makeText(this, "Apasă încă o dată pentru a ieși", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        pageTrusted = false
        BubbleService.onEvent = null
        try { fileCallback?.onReceiveValue(null) } catch (e: Exception) {}
        fileCallback = null
        try { wv.removeJavascriptInterface(HostBridge.NAME) } catch (e: Exception) {}
        try { (wv.parent as? ViewGroup)?.removeView(wv) } catch (e: Exception) {}
        try { wv.destroy() } catch (e: Exception) {}
        super.onDestroy()
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/index.html ===
<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="ro"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1,maximum-scale=1,user-scalable=no,viewport-fit=cover">
<meta name="theme-color" content="#05050f">
<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="frame-src 'none'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'">

const I=n=>`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">${P[n]}</svg>`;
const MARK=c=>`<svg class="mk ${c||""}" viewBox="0 0 108 108"><circle class="mk-r" cx="54" cy="54" r="34" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.14)" stroke-width="1.2"/><path class="mk-b" pathLength="1" d="M38 78V30H52a12 12 0 0 1 0 24H38H56a12 12 0 0 1 0 24H38" fill="none" stroke="url(#mkG)" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/><circle class="mk-d" cx="76" cy="28" r="3" fill="#22d3ee"/></svg>`;

function paint(){
 $$("[data-i]").forEach(e=>{if(!e.innerHTML)e.innerHTML=I(e.dataset.i)});
 $$("[data-wm]").forEach(e=>{if(!e.innerHTML)e.innerHTML=WM(e.dataset.wm)});
 $$("[data-mark]").forEach(e=>{if(!e.innerHTML)e.innerHTML=MARK(e.dataset.mark)})}

/* ---------- setări (persistente) + haptics ---------- */
const ST=Object.assign({haptics:true,anim:true,notif:true,lang:"ro"},LS.get("set")||{});
function hap(k){if(!ST.haptics)return;try{NativeBridge.vibrate(k||0)}catch(e){}}
function applySet(){document.documentElement.classList.toggle("noanim",!ST.anim);const b=$("#bellB");if(b)b.innerHTML=I(ST.notif?"bell":"belloff")+'<i class="dot" id="bdot" hidden></i>'}
function toggleSet(k,el){ST[k]=!ST[k];el.classList.toggle("on",ST[k]);LS.set("set",ST);applySet();
 if(k=="haptics"&&ST.haptics)hap(2);
 if(k=="notif"&&ST.notif)NativeBridge.notifications.request()}

const SB={ses:LS.get("ses"),profile:LS.get("profile")};
async function http(path,o){o=o||{};const c=new AbortController(),t=setTimeout(()=>c.abort(),15000);
 try{const h=Object.assign({apikey:CFG.key,"Content-Type":"application/json"},o.h||{});if(o.auth&&SB.ses)h.Authorization="Bearer "+SB.ses.access_token;
  const r=await fetch(CFG.url+path,{method:o.m||"GET",headers:h,body:o.b?JSON.stringify(o.b):undefined,signal:c.signal});let j=null;try{j=await r.json()}catch(e){}return{ok:r.ok,s:r.status,j}}
 catch(e){return{ok:false,s:0,net:true,j:null}}finally{clearTimeout(t)}}
const msgOf=r=>(r&&r.j&&(r.j.msg||r.j.message||r.j.error_description||r.j.error))||("HTTP "+(r&&r.s));
function setSes(j){const u=j.user||{};SB.ses={access_token:j.access_token,refresh_token:j.refresh_token,exp:j.expires_at?j.expires_at*1000:Date.now()+(j.expires_in||3600)*1000,uid:u.id||(SB.ses&&SB.ses.uid)||"",email:u.email||(SB.ses&&SB.ses.email)||""};LS.set("ses",SB.ses)}
function clearSes(){SB.ses=null;SB.profile=null;LS.set("ses",null);LS.set("profile",null)}
async function ensure(){if(!SB.ses)return"none";if(SB.ses.exp-60000>Date.now())return"ok";
 const r=await http("/auth/v1/token?grant_type=refresh_token",{m:"POST",b:{refresh_token:SB.ses.refresh_token}});
 if(r.ok&&r.j&&r.j.access_token){setSes(r.j);return"ok"}
 if(r.net||r.s>=500||r.s==429)return"net";clearSes();return"none"}
async function rest(path,o){let r=await http(path,Object.assign({auth:1},o||{}));if(r.s==401&&SB.ses){SB.ses.exp=0;if(await ensure()=="ok")r=await http(path,Object.assign({auth:1},o||{}))}return r}
function am(r){const c=r&&r.j&&(r.j.error_code||r.j.code),m=String(msgOf(r)||"").toLowerCase();
 if(r.net)return"Fără conexiune la internet";
 if(c=="invalid_credentials"||m.includes("invalid login"))return"Email sau parolă greșită";
 if(c=="email_not_confirmed"||m.includes("not confirmed"))return"Confirmă emailul înainte de autentificare";
 if(c=="user_already_exists"||c=="email_exists"||m.includes("already"))return"Email deja folosit – autentifică-te";
 if(c=="weak_password"||m.includes("password"))return"Parolă prea slabă (minim 6 caractere)";
 if(c=="over_request_rate_limit"||c=="over_email_send_rate_limit"||r.s==429)return"Prea multe încercări – așteaptă puțin";
 if(c=="email_address_invalid"||m.includes("email address"))return"Adresă de email invalidă";
 if(m.includes("database error"))return"Numele de utilizator este ocupat sau invalid";
 if(c=="signup_disabled")return"Înregistrările sunt oprite momentan";
 return"Eroare: "+msgOf(r)}
async function signIn(em,pw){const r=await http("/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password",{m:"POST",b:{email:em,password:pw}});
 if(r.ok&&r.j&&r.j.access_token){setSes(r.j);return{ok:1}}return{err:am(r)}}
async function signUp(em,pw,name,handle){const r=await http("/auth/v1/signup",{m:"POST",b:{email:em,password:pw,data:{username:name,handle:handle}}});
 if(r.ok&&r.j&&r.j.access_token){setSes(r.j);return{ok:1}}
 if(r.ok&&r.j&&Array.isArray(r.j.identities)&&!r.j.identities.length)return{err:"Email deja folosit – autentifică-te"};
 if(r.ok)return{info:"Ți-am trimis un email de confirmare. Confirmă-l, apoi autentifică-te."};
 return{err:am(r)}}
async function signOut(){try{if(SB.ses)await http("/auth/v1/logout?scope=local",{m:"POST",auth:1})}catch(e){}clearSes()}
async function handleFree(h){const r=await http("/rest/v1/rpc/handle_available",{m:"POST",b:{h:h}});return r.ok?r.j===true:null}
async function loadProfile(){if(!SB.ses)return null;
 const r=await rest("/rest/v1/profiles?id=eq."+SB.ses.uid+"&select=id,username,handle,bio,avatar");
 if(r.ok&&Array.isArray(r.j)){let p=r.j[0];
  if(!p){const c=await rest("/rest/v1/profiles?on_conflict=id",{m:"POST",h:{Prefer:"resolution=merge-duplicates,return=representation"},b:{id:SB.ses.uid}});p=c.ok&&Array.isArray(c.j)?c.j[0]:null}
  if(p){SB.profile=p;LS.set("profile",p)}}
 return SB.profile}
const isAdminUI=()=>((SB.ses&&SB.ses.email)||"").toLowerCase()==ADMIN_EMAIL;

const emptyPg=(ic,t,s)=>`<div class="empty"><div class="ring">${I(ic)}</div><h2>${esc(t)}</h2><p>${esc(s||"În curând")}</p></div>`;
let cur=null;
function buildShell(){
 $("#stage").innerHTML=ROOMS.map(r=>`<section class="room" id="r-${r.id}"></section>`).join("");
 $("#nav").innerHTML=ROOMS.map(r=>`<button class="tb" data-id="${r.id}"><span class="ic"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">${P[r.ic]}</svg></span>${esc(r.label)}</button>`).join("")}
function go(id){const R=ROOMS.find(x=>x.id==id);if(!R||id==cur)return;const prev=cur;cur=id;
 $$(".tb").forEach(t=>t.classList.toggle("on",t.dataset.id==id));
 $$(".room").forEach(s=>s.classList.toggle("on",s.id=="r-"+id));
 const el=$("#r-"+id);
 if(!el.dataset.m){el.dataset.m=1;const H=Rooms[id];if(H&&H.mount)H.mount(el);else el.innerHTML=emptyPg(R.ic,R.title||R.label)}
 if(prev&&Rooms[prev]&&Rooms[prev].hide)Rooms[prev].hide();
 if(Rooms[id]&&Rooms[id].show)Rooms[id].show();
 $("#srch").hidden=!R.search}
function resetShell(){cur=null;$$(".room").forEach(s=>{delete s.dataset.m;s.innerHTML="";s.classList.remove("on")});$$(".tb").forEach(t=>t.classList.remove("on"))}

function closePages(){while(PG.length)PG.pop().el.remove()}

/* ---------- Sources (API Keys) – extensibil ----------
   Adaugi o sursă într-o linie, ex.:
   SOURCES.push({id:"tmdb",name:"TMDB",desc:"Filme și seriale",ph:"API key (v3)",scan:async key=>({ok:true,msg:"Scanat"})});  */
const SOURCES=[];
const srcKey=id=>LS.get("src."+id)||"";
PAGES.sources={t:()=>"Sources",render(el){
 if(!SOURCES.length){el.innerHTML=`<p class="note">Aici apar sursele (TMDB și altele), una sub alta: nume, câmp pentru cheie API și Rescanare.</p>`+emptyPg("key","Nicio sursă încă","API Keys");return}
 el.innerHTML=SOURCES.map(s=>`<div class="src" data-s="${esc(s.id)}"><div class="sh"><b>${esc(s.name)}</b><span class="chip ${srcKey(s.id)?"ok":""}">${srcKey(s.id)?"Configurat":"Neconfigurat"}</span></div>${s.desc?`<p>${esc(s.desc)}</p>`:""}
  <label class="fld"><span style="display:contents">${I("key")}</span><input type="password" value="${esc(srcKey(s.id))}" placeholder="${esc(s.ph||"API key")}" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false"></label>
  <div class="sb"><button class="btn sm" data-sv="${esc(s.id)}">Salvează</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-sc="${esc(s.id)}">${I("refresh")}<span>Rescanare</span></button></div><p class="ss"></p></div>`).join("")}};
function srcSave(id){const c=document.querySelector(`.src[data-s="${id}"]`);if(!c)return;const v=c.querySelector("input").value.trim();LS.set("src."+id,v||null);
 const ch=c.querySelector(".chip");ch.textContent=v?"Configurat":"Neconfigurat";ch.classList.toggle("ok",!!v);toast(v?"Cheie salvată":"Cheie ștearsă")}
async function srcScan(id,btn){const s=SOURCES.find(x=>x.id==id),c=document.querySelector(`.src[data-s="${id}"]`);if(!s||!c)return;const ss=c.querySelector(".ss");

-- =====================================================================
-- HiBlue · baza de date (Supabase) — v0.1
-- Rulează O SINGURĂ DATĂ în Supabase → SQL Editor. Este idempotent (poate fi rulat din nou).
-- Aduce: tabela profiles, regulile RLS, crearea automată a profilului la înregistrare
--        (din nume + @utilizator trimise de aplicație) și verificarea disponibilității @utilizator.
-- Compatibil cu proiectul vechi Pulse (nu șterge nimic din ce există).
-- =====================================================================

-- ============ 1) PROFILE ============
create table if not exists public.profiles (
  id         uuid primary key references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  username   text,                       -- numele afișat
  handle     text,                       -- @utilizator (unic)
  bio        text,
  avatar     text,                       -- emoji sau cale/URL (în update-urile viitoare)
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
alter table public.profiles add column if not exists updated_at timestamptz not null default now();

alter table public.profiles drop constraint if exists profiles_handle_format;
alter table public.profiles add  constraint profiles_handle_format
  check (handle is null or handle ~ '^[a-z0-9_.]{3,20}$');
create unique index if not exists profiles_handle_lower_key on public.profiles (lower(handle));

-- limite de lungime (le înlocuiesc pe cele strânse din Pulse: avatar putea avea doar 8 caractere)
alter table public.profiles drop constraint if exists profiles_len;
alter table public.profiles drop constraint if exists profiles_len_v2;
alter table public.profiles add  constraint profiles_len_v2
  check (char_length(coalesce(username,'')) <= 32
     and char_length(coalesce(bio,''))      <= 160
     and char_length(coalesce(avatar,''))   <= 500);

-- ============ 2) RLS ============
alter table public.profiles enable row level security;

drop policy if exists profiles_select on public.profiles;
create policy profiles_select on public.profiles for select to authenticated using (true);

drop policy if exists profiles_insert on public.profiles;
create policy profiles_insert on public.profiles for insert to authenticated with check (auth.uid() = id);

drop policy if exists profiles_update on public.profiles;
create policy profiles_update on public.profiles for update to authenticated
  using (auth.uid() = id) with check (auth.uid() = id);

drop policy if exists profiles_delete on public.profiles;
create policy profiles_delete on public.profiles for delete to authenticated using (auth.uid() = id);

-- ============ 3) updated_at automat ============
create or replace function public.touch_updated_at() returns trigger
language plpgsql as $$
begin
  new.updated_at = now();
  return new;
end $$;
drop trigger if exists profiles_touch on public.profiles;
create trigger profiles_touch before update on public.profiles
  for each row execute function public.touch_updated_at();

-- ============ 4) Profil creat automat la înregistrare ============
-- Aplicația trimite username + handle în metadatele înregistrării; trigger-ul le preia.
-- Merge și când contul cere confirmare pe email (încă nu există sesiune, deci clientul nu poate scrie singur).
create or replace function public.handle_new_user() returns trigger
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare
  h text := lower(nullif(trim(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'handle'), ''));
  n text := nullif(trim(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'username'), '');
begin
  if coalesce(new.is_anonymous, false) then return new; end if;
  if n is not null then n := left(n, 32); end if;
  if h is not null and h !~ '^[a-z0-9_.]{3,20}$' then h := null; end if;
  if h is not null and exists (select 1 from public.profiles where lower(handle) = h) then h := null; end if;
  begin
    insert into public.profiles(id, username, handle) values (new.id, n, h) on conflict (id) do nothing;
  exception when unique_violation then
    insert into public.profiles(id, username, handle) values (new.id, n, null) on conflict (id) do nothing;
  end;
  return new;
end $$;

drop trigger if exists on_auth_user_created on auth.users;
create trigger on_auth_user_created after insert on auth.users
  for each row execute function public.handle_new_user();

-- ============ 5) Verificare @utilizator liber (apelată de ecranul de înregistrare) ============
create or replace function public.handle_available(h text) returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select h ~ '^[a-z0-9_.]{3,20}$'
     and not exists (select 1 from public.profiles where lower(handle) = lower(h));
$$;
revoke all on function public.handle_available(text) from public;
grant execute on function public.handle_available(text) to anon, authenticated;
=== FILE: supabase/update0.0.1.sql ===
-- =====================================================================
-- HiBlue · update0.0.1 — Admin Panel (partea de server)
-- Rulează O SINGURĂ DATĂ în Supabase → SQL Editor (după hiblue.sql). Poate fi rulat și din nou.
-- Accesul se verifică PE SERVER: doar contul lingurarmarius444@gmail.com (cu email confirmat).
-- =====================================================================

create or replace function public.is_admin() returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public, auth as $$
  select exists (
    select 1 from auth.users u
    where u.id = auth.uid()
      and lower(u.email) = 'lingurarmarius444@gmail.com'
      and u.email_confirmed_at is not null
  );
$$;

create or replace function public.admin_stats() returns json
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public, auth as $$
begin
  if not public.is_admin() then raise exception 'forbidden' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return json_build_object(
    'total',     (select count(*) from auth.users u where not coalesce(u.is_anonymous, false)),
    'new_24h',   (select count(*) from auth.users u where not coalesce(u.is_anonymous, false) and u.created_at > now() - interval '24 hours'),
    'new_7d',    (select count(*) from auth.users u where not coalesce(u.is_anonymous, false) and u.created_at > now() - interval '7 days'),
    'active_7d', (select count(*) from auth.users u where not coalesce(u.is_anonymous, false) and u.last_sign_in_at > now() - interval '7 days')
  );
end $$;

create or replace function public.admin_list_users(q text default '', lim int default 20, skip int default 0)
returns table (id uuid, email text, username text, handle text, created_at timestamptz, last_sign_in_at timestamptz)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public, auth as $$
begin
  if not public.is_admin() then raise exception 'forbidden' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query
    select u.id, u.email::text, p.username, p.handle, u.created_at, u.last_sign_in_at
    from auth.users u
    left join public.profiles p on p.id = u.id
    where not coalesce(u.is_anonymous, false)
      and (coalesce(q, '') = ''
           or u.email    ilike '%' || q || '%'
           or p.username ilike '%' || q || '%'
           or p.handle   ilike '%' || q || '%')
    order by u.created_at desc
    limit least(greatest(lim, 1), 100) offset greatest(skip, 0);
end $$;

create or replace function public.admin_delete_user(target uuid) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public, auth as $$
begin
  if not public.is_admin() then raise exception 'forbidden' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if target = auth.uid() then raise exception 'nu te poti sterge pe tine' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  delete from auth.users where id = target;   -- profilul se șterge în cascadă
  return found;
end $$;

revoke all on function public.is_admin()                          from public;
revoke all on function public.admin_stats()                       from public;
revoke all on function public.admin_list_users(text, int, int)    from public;
revoke all on function public.admin_delete_user(uuid)             from public;
grant execute on function public.is_admin()                       to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.admin_stats()                    to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.admin_list_users(text, int, int) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.admin_delete_user(uuid)          to authenticated;
=== FILE: supabase/update0.0.2.sql ===
-- =====================================================================
-- HiBlue · update0.0.2 — Games: scoruri Top 10 + rating
-- Rulează O SINGURĂ DATĂ în Supabase → SQL Editor (după hiblue.sql). Poate fi rulat și din nou.
--
-- Reguli:
--  * Pentru fiecare joc, serverul păstrează DOAR Top 10 (un scor = cel mai bun al fiecărui jucător).
--    Orice scor care iese din Top 10 este șters automat, în aceeași operație.
--  * Scorurile se scriu EXCLUSIV prin submit_score() (clientul nu poate scrie direct în tabel).
--  * Orice utilizator autentificat poate da rating 1–5 (o notă / joc, o poate schimba).
-- =====================================================================

-- ============ 1) Catalog jocuri (un joc nou = un rând nou aici) ============
create table if not exists public.games (
  id        text primary key,
  name      text not null,
  max_score int  not null default 9999,
  pace      numeric not null default 0.6   -- secunde minime / punct (anti-trișat grosier)
);
alter table public.games enable row level security;
drop policy if exists games_select on public.games;
create policy games_select on public.games for select to authenticated using (true);

insert into public.games (id, name, max_score, pace) values ('flyby', 'Flyby', 9999, 0.6)
on conflict (id) do update set name = excluded.name, max_score = excluded.max_score, pace = excluded.pace;

-- ============ 2) Scoruri (doar Top 10 / joc) ============
create table if not exists public.game_scores (
  game_id    text not null references public.games(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id    uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  score      int  not null check (score >= 0),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (game_id, user_id)
);
create index if not exists game_scores_rank_idx on public.game_scores (game_id, score desc, updated_at);
alter table public.game_scores enable row level security;
drop policy if exists gs_select on public.game_scores;
create policy gs_select on public.game_scores for select to authenticated using (true);
revoke insert, update, delete on public.game_scores from anon, authenticated;

-- ============ 3) Rating ============
create table if not exists public.game_ratings (
  game_id    text not null references public.games(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id    uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  rating     smallint not null check (rating between 1 and 5),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (game_id, user_id)
);
alter table public.game_ratings enable row level security;
drop policy if exists gr_select on public.game_ratings;
create policy gr_select on public.game_ratings for select to authenticated using (user_id = auth.uid());
drop policy if exists gr_insert on public.game_ratings;
create policy gr_insert on public.game_ratings for insert to authenticated with check (user_id = auth.uid());
drop policy if exists gr_update on public.game_ratings;
create policy gr_update on public.game_ratings for update to authenticated using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());

create or replace function public.game_ratings_touch() returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin new.updated_at = now(); return new; end $$;
drop trigger if exists game_ratings_touch on public.game_ratings;
create trigger game_ratings_touch before update on public.game_ratings for each row execute function public.game_ratings_touch();

-- media + număr voturi (toți utilizatorii)
create or replace function public.game_ratings_summary() returns table (game_id text, mean numeric, votes int)
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select r.game_id, round(avg(r.rating)::numeric, 1), count(*)::int
  from public.game_ratings r group by r.game_id;
$$;

-- ============ 4) Top 10 ============
create or replace function public.game_top(p_game text) returns table (pos int, user_id uuid, username text, handle text, score int)
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select (row_number() over (order by s.score desc, s.updated_at asc, s.user_id))::int,
         s.user_id, p.username, p.handle, s.score
  from public.game_scores s
  left join public.profiles p on p.id = s.user_id
  where s.game_id = p_game
  order by 1
  limit 10;
$$;

-- ============ 5) Trimitere scor (validează, salvează, șterge ce e sub Top 10) ============
create or replace function public.submit_score(p_game text, p_score int, p_secs int default 0) returns json
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare
  uid uuid := auth.uid();
  g public.games%rowtype;
  cur int; cnt int; mn int; rk int;
  improved boolean := false;
begin
  if uid is null then raise exception 'forbidden' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into g from public.games where id = p_game;
  if not found then raise exception 'joc necunoscut' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_score < 0 or p_score > g.max_score
     or p_score > ceil(greatest(coalesce(p_secs, 0), 0) / g.pace)::int + 2 then
    raise exception 'scor invalid' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('score:' || p_game));

  select s.score into cur from public.game_scores s where s.game_id = p_game and s.user_id = uid;
  select count(*), min(t.score) into cnt, mn from (
    select s.score from public.game_scores s where s.game_id = p_game
    order by s.score desc, s.updated_at asc, s.user_id limit 10) t;

  if cur is null or p_score > cur then
    if cur is null and cnt >= 10 and p_score <= mn then
      return json_build_object('in_top', false, 'rank', null, 'best', null, 'min', mn, 'improved', false);
    end if;
    insert into public.game_scores (game_id, user_id, score) values (p_game, uid, p_score)
    on conflict (game_id, user_id) do update set score = excluded.score, updated_at = now();
    improved := true;
    -- ce rămâne sub Top 10 se șterge
    delete from public.game_scores d where d.game_id = p_game and d.user_id in (
      select s.user_id from public.game_scores s where s.game_id = p_game
      order by s.score desc, s.updated_at asc, s.user_id offset 10);
  end if;

  select r.pos into rk from (
    select s.user_id, (row_number() over (order by s.score desc, s.updated_at asc, s.user_id))::int as pos
    from public.game_scores s where s.game_id = p_game) r where r.user_id = uid;
  select s.score into cur from public.game_scores s where s.game_id = p_game and s.user_id = uid;
  return json_build_object('in_top', rk is not null, 'rank', rk, 'best', cur, 'min', mn, 'improved', improved);
end $$;

revoke all on function public.game_ratings_summary()          from public;
revoke all on function public.game_top(text)                  from public;
revoke all on function public.submit_score(text, int, int)    from public;
grant execute on function public.game_ratings_summary()       to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.game_top(text)               to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.submit_score(text, int, int) to authenticated;

(function(){

/* ---------------- copertină (SVG, același stil ca Flyby) ---------------- */
const coverNova=k=>`<svg viewBox="0 0 300 400" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice"><defs>
<linearGradient id="${k}b" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#0b1030"/><stop offset=".55" stop-color="#241257"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#0a2a6e"/></linearGradient>

/* =====================================================================
   FLIP NOVA – original: inversezi gravitația ca să aduni orburi de
   energie și să eviți minele. O singură atingere = o singură comandă.

function FlipNova(host,api){
 const cv=document.createElement("canvas"),c=cv.getContext("2d");host.appendChild(cv);
 const GR=1500,RAD=11;
 let W,H,dpr,top0=0,S,ball,orbs,spikes,parts,stars,score,t,tot=0,grav=1,
     raf,last,flash,shake,pop,ovr,deadT,orbT,spkT;
 function size(){dpr=Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio||1,2.5);W=host.clientWidth;H=host.clientHeight;
  cv.width=Math.round(W*dpr);cv.height=Math.round(H*dpr);cv.style.width=W+"px";cv.style.height=H+"px";c.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
  top0=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue("--sat"))||0;
  stars=[];for(let i=0;i<60;i++){const l=Math.random();stars.push({x:Math.random()*W,y:Math.random()*H,r:.6+l*1.3,l:.15+l*.8,a:.25+Math.random()*.55})}}
 function reset(){ball={x:W*.5,y:H*.5,vx:150,vy:0,trail:[]};orbs=[];spikes=[];parts=[];score=0;t=0;
  flash=0;shake=0;pop=0;ovr=false;deadT=0;orbT=.5;spkT=1.8;grav=1;S="ready"}
 function burst(x,y,n,col,spd){for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=Math.random()*6.283,v=(.3+Math.random())*spd;parts.push({x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*v,vy:Math.sin(a)*v,l:1,d:.6+Math.random()*.7,s:1.5+Math.random()*2.5,c:col})}}
 function spawnOrb(){let x,y,ok=0;
  do{x=30+Math.random()*(W-60);y=70+Math.random()*(H-140);ok++}while(ok<24&&Math.hypot(x-ball.x,y-ball.y)<90);
  orbs.push({x,y,ph:Math.random()*6.28})}
 function spawnSpike(){const sp=70+Math.min(140,score*4.5)+Math.random()*40;
  const horiz=Math.random()<.6;
  const x=horiz?(Math.random()<.5?-26:W+26):50+Math.random()*(W-100);
  const y=horiz?50+Math.random()*(H-100):(Math.random()<.5?-26:H+26);
  spikes.push({x,y,vx:horiz?(x<0?sp:-sp):0,vy:horiz?0:(y<0?sp:-sp),r:15+Math.random()*8,rot:Math.random()*6.28,rs:(Math.random()-.5)*2.6})}
 function die(){if(S!="play")return;S="dead";deadT=0;flash=1;shake=1;
  burst(ball.x,ball.y,30,"#c4b5fd",300);burst(ball.x,ball.y,18,"#ff6b8a",220);api.hap&&api.hap(2)}
 function update(dt){tot+=dt;
  stars.forEach(s=>{s.x-=26*s.l*dt;if(s.x<-2){s.x=W+2;s.y=Math.random()*H}});
  if(S=="ready"){ball.x=W*.5;ball.y=H*.5+Math.sin(tot*2.6)*10;ball.trail.length=0}
  else if(S=="play"){
   t+=dt;
   ball.vy+=grav*GR*dt;
   const tgt=270+Math.min(230,score*7),sp=Math.hypot(ball.vx,ball.vy)||1;
   const ns=sp+(tgt-sp)*Math.min(1,dt*1.6);ball.vx*=ns/sp;ball.vy*=ns/sp;
   ball.x+=ball.vx*dt;ball.y+=ball.vy*dt;
   if(ball.x<RAD){ball.x=RAD;ball.vx=Math.abs(ball.vx);burst(ball.x,ball.y,4,"#8b5cf6",110)}
   if(ball.x>W-RAD){ball.x=W-RAD;ball.vx=-Math.abs(ball.vx);burst(ball.x,ball.y,4,"#8b5cf6",110)}
   if(ball.y<RAD){ball.y=RAD;ball.vy=Math.abs(ball.vy);burst(ball.x,ball.y,4,"#8b5cf6",110)}
   if(ball.y>H-RAD){ball.y=H-RAD;ball.vy=-Math.abs(ball.vy);burst(ball.x,ball.y,4,"#8b5cf6",110)}
   ball.trail.push({x:ball.x,y:ball.y});if(ball.trail.length>14)ball.trail.shift();
   orbT-=dt;if(orbT<=0&&orbs.length<4){spawnOrb();orbT=.8+Math.random()*.5}
   spkT-=dt;if(spkT<=0){spawnSpike();spkT=Math.max(.8,2.3-score*.045)}
   for(let i=orbs.length-1;i>=0;i--){const o=orbs[i];o.ph+=dt*3;
    if(Math.hypot(o.x-ball.x,o.y-ball.y)<26){orbs.splice(i,1);score++;pop=1;api.hap&&api.hap(0);burst(o.x,o.y,14,"#22d3ee",220)}}
   spikes.forEach(s=>{s.x+=s.vx*dt;s.y+=s.vy*dt;s.rot+=s.rs*dt});
   spikes=spikes.filter(s=>s.x>-70&&s.x<W+70&&s.y>-70&&s.y<H+70);
   for(const s of spikes){if(Math.hypot(s.x-ball.x,s.y-ball.y)<s.r*.75+RAD){die();break}}
  }
  else if(S=="dead"){deadT+=dt;if(deadT>.8&&!ovr){ovr=true;api.over(score,Math.round(t))}}
  parts.forEach(p=>{p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.l-=dt/p.d});parts=parts.filter(p=>p.l>0);
  pop=Math.max(0,pop-dt*4);flash=Math.max(0,flash-dt*3);shake=Math.max(0,shake-dt*2.2)}
 function txt(s,x,y,size,al){c.font="800 "+size+"px ui-rounded,system-ui,sans-serif";c.textAlign="center";c.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,"+al+")";c.fillText(s,x,y)}
 function chev(x,y,dir){c.strokeStyle="#67e8f9";c.lineWidth=3;c.lineCap="round";c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-7,y-6*dir);c.lineTo(x,y+6*dir);c.lineTo(x+7,y-6*dir);c.stroke()}
 function draw(){c.save();
  if(shake>0)c.translate((Math.random()-.5)*10*shake,(Math.random()-.5)*10*shake);
  const bg=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);bg.addColorStop(0,"#080a22");bg.addColorStop(.55,"#1a0f3f");bg.addColorStop(1,"#0a1d46");c.fillStyle=bg;c.fillRect(-20,-20,W+40,H+40);
  stars.forEach(s=>{c.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,"+s.a*s.l+")";c.beginPath();c.arc(s.x,s.y,s.r,0,6.283);c.fill()});
  orbs.forEach(o=>{const p=1+Math.sin(o.ph)*.15;
   c.shadowColor="rgba(34,211,238,.8)";c.shadowBlur=14;
   c.fillStyle="#22d3ee";c.beginPath();c.arc(o.x,o.y,9*p,0,6.283);c.fill();c.shadowBlur=0;
   c.strokeStyle="rgba(34,211,238,.4)";c.lineWidth=1.5;c.beginPath();c.arc(o.x,o.y,9*p+5,0,6.283);c.stroke()});
  spikes.forEach(s=>{c.save();c.translate(s.x,s.y);c.rotate(s.rot);
   c.shadowColor="rgba(255,107,138,.7)";c.shadowBlur=12;c.fillStyle="#1c0a18";
   c.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<16;i++){const a=i/16*6.283,rr2=i%2?s.r:s.r*.55;c.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*rr2,Math.sin(a)*rr2)}c.closePath();c.fill();
   c.strokeStyle="#ff6b8a";c.lineWidth=2;c.stroke();c.shadowBlur=0;
   c.fillStyle="#ff9db3";c.beginPath();c.arc(0,0,s.r*.18,0,6.283);c.fill();c.restore()});
  if(S!="dead"){
   ball.trail.forEach((p,i)=>{const f=i/ball.trail.length;c.globalAlpha=f*.35;c.fillStyle="#22d3ee";c.beginPath();c.arc(p.x,p.y,RAD*f*.9,0,6.283);c.fill()});c.globalAlpha=1;
   c.shadowColor="rgba(34,211,238,.9)";c.shadowBlur=18;
   const sg=c.createLinearGradient(ball.x-RAD,ball.y-RAD,ball.x+RAD,ball.y+RAD);sg.addColorStop(0,"#c084fc");sg.addColorStop(1,"#22d3ee");c.fillStyle=sg;
   c.beginPath();c.arc(ball.x,ball.y,RAD,0,6.283);c.fill();c.shadowBlur=0;
   c.fillStyle="#fff";c.beginPath();c.arc(ball.x-3,ball.y-3,2.4,0,6.283);c.fill();
   const gy=H*.5;
   c.globalAlpha=grav>0?.9:.22;chev(W-22,gy+14,1);
   c.globalAlpha=grav<0?.9:.22;chev(W-22,gy-14,-1);
   c.globalAlpha=1}
  c.shadowColor="rgba(0,0,0,.5)";c.shadowBlur=10;
  if(S!="dead")txt(score,W/2,top0+92,58+pop*12,1);
  if(S=="ready"){txt("Atinge ca să inversezi gravitația",W/2,H*.7,20,.95);txt("Adună orburi · evită minele",W/2,H*.7+26,14,.6);const b=api.best?api.best():0;if(b)txt("Cel mai bun: "+b,W/2,H*.7+54,14,.6)}
  if(S=="pause")txt("Atinge pentru a continua",W/2,H*.5,22,.95);
  c.shadowBlur=0;
  if(flash>0){c.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,"+flash*.45+")";c.fillRect(-20,-20,W+40,H+40)}
  c.restore()}
 function loop(now){raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);const dt=Math.min(.033,(now-last)/1000||0);last=now;update(dt);draw()}
 function down(e){e.preventDefault();
  if(S=="ready")S="play";
  else if(S=="play"){grav*=-1;ball.vy+=grav*130;burst(ball.x,ball.y,6,grav>0?"#22d3ee":"#c084fc",140);api.hap&&api.hap(1)}
  else if(S=="pause")S="play"}
 const vis=()=>{if(document.hidden&&S=="play")S="pause"};
 const rs=()=>{const sx=W?ball.x/W:.5,sy=H?ball.y/H:.5;size();ball.x=W*sx;ball.y=H*sy};
 size();reset();
 host.addEventListener("pointerdown",down);document.addEventListener("visibilitychange",vis);window.addEventListener("resize",rs);
 last=performance.now();raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);
 return{restart(){reset()},destroy(){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);host.removeEventListener("pointerdown",down);document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange",vis);window.removeEventListener("resize",rs);cv.remove()}}
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/ro/lingurar/hiblue/bridge/HostBridge.kt ===
package ro.lingurar.hiblue.bridge

import android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

/**
 * Acțiunile pe care host-ul le pune la dispoziția punții. Implementate de MainActivity.
 * Metodele pot fi apelate din firul JavaBridge (nu din UI); implementarea trebuie să sară pe UI când e nevoie.
 */
interface HostActions {
    /** Pagina încărcată acum în WebView este pagina locală a aplicației (assets)? */
    fun trustedPage(): Boolean
    fun vibrate(kind: Int)
    fun notificationsGranted(): Boolean
    fun requestNotifications()
    fun notify(title: String, body: String): Boolean
    fun setOrientation(mode: String): Boolean
    fun share(text: String): Boolean
    /** (permisiune de afișare peste alte aplicații, bulă activă) */
    fun bubbleStatus(): Pair<Boolean, Boolean>
    fun requestBubblePermission()
    /** null = pornită, altfel codul erorii */
    fun bubbleShow(conv: String, title: String, avatar: String, unread: Int): String?
    fun bubbleUpdate(unread: Int)
    fun bubbleHide()
    /** conversația cerută la pornirea din bulă/notificare (consumată o singură dată) */
    fun takePendingChat(): String?
    /** null = pornit, altfel codul erorii */
    fun launchNativeGame(id: String): String?
    /** (id, runtime) */
    fun nativeGames(): List<Pair<String, String>>
}

/**
 * Singura suprafață Kotlin expusă JavaScript-ului: window.HBNative.invoke(json) -> json.
 *
 * Cerere:   {"v":1,"method":"device.vibrate","params":{"kind":1}}
 * Răspuns:  {"v":1,"ok":true,"data":{...}}  sau  {"v":1,"ok":false,"error":{"code":"...","message":"..."}}
 *
 * Reguli: metode pe listă albă, parametri validați strict, cerere limitată ca dimensiune,
 * refuz dacă pagina curentă nu este cea locală. Nu se expun tokenuri, parole sau chei.
 */
class HostBridge(private val actions: HostActions, private val appVersion: String) {

    companion object {
        const val NAME = "HBNative"
        const val PROTOCOL = 1
        private const val MAX_REQUEST = 8 * 1024
        private val CAPABILITIES = listOf(
            "device.vibrate",
            "device.notifications",
            "device.notify",
            "device.share",
            "host.orientation",
            "game.native",
            "chat.bubble"
        )
        private val GAME_ID = Regex("^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{1,31}$")
        private val UUID_RE = Regex("^[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}$")
    }

    private fun ok(data: JSONObject): String =
        JSONObject().put("v", PROTOCOL).put("ok", true).put("data", data).toString()

    private fun fail(code: String, message: String): String =
        JSONObject().put("v", PROTOCOL).put("ok", false)
            .put("error", JSONObject().put("code", code).put("message", message)).toString()

    @JavascriptInterface
    fun invoke(request: String?): String {
        if (!actions.trustedPage()) return fail("FORBIDDEN", "Pagină neautorizată")
        if (request == null || request.length > MAX_REQUEST) return fail("BAD_REQUEST", "Cerere invalidă")
        val req: JSONObject = try {
            JSONObject(request)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            return fail("BAD_REQUEST", "Cerere invalidă")
        }
        val method = req.optString("method", "")
        val params = req.optJSONObject("params") ?: JSONObject()
        return try {
            dispatch(method, params)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            fail("INTERNAL", "Eroare internă")
        }
    }

    private fun dispatch(method: String, p: JSONObject): String {
        when (method) {
            "host.info" -> {
                return ok(
                    JSONObject().put("protocol", PROTOCOL).put("app", appVersion)
                        .put("capabilities", JSONArray(CAPABILITIES))
                )
            }
            "device.vibrate" -> {
                val k = p.opt("kind")
                if (k !is Int || k < 0 || k > 2) return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "kind trebuie să fie 0, 1 sau 2")
                actions.vibrate(k)
                return ok(JSONObject())
            }
            "device.notifications.status" -> {
                return ok(JSONObject().put("granted", actions.notificationsGranted()))
            }
            "device.notifications.request" -> {
                actions.requestNotifications()
                return ok(JSONObject().put("requested", true))
            }
            "device.notify" -> {
                val t = p.opt("title")
                val b = p.opt("body")
                if (t !is String || b !is String || t.isEmpty() || t.length > 60 || b.length > 200) {
                    return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "title (1-60) și body (0-200) sunt obligatorii")
                }
                if (!actions.notificationsGranted()) return fail("PERMISSION_DENIED", "Notificările nu sunt permise")
                return if (actions.notify(t, b)) ok(JSONObject()) else fail("INTERNAL", "Notificarea nu a putut fi afișată")
            }
            "device.share" -> {
                val t = p.opt("text")
                if (t !is String || t.isEmpty() || t.length > 1000) return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "text (1-1000) este obligatoriu")
                return if (actions.share(t)) ok(JSONObject()) else fail("INTERNAL", "Partajarea nu este disponibilă")
            }
            "host.orientation" -> {
                val m = p.opt("mode")
                if (m !is String || (m != "portrait" && m != "landscape" && m != "auto")) {
                    return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "mode: portrait | landscape | auto")
                }
                return if (actions.setOrientation(m)) ok(JSONObject()) else fail("INTERNAL", "Orientare neschimbată")
            }
            "bubble.status" -> {
                val s = actions.bubbleStatus()
                return ok(JSONObject().put("permission", s.first).put("active", s.second))
            }
            "bubble.permission.request" -> {
                actions.requestBubblePermission()
                return ok(JSONObject().put("requested", true))
            }
            "bubble.show" -> {
                val conv = p.opt("conv")
                val title = p.opt("title")
                val avatar = p.opt("avatar")
                val unread = p.opt("unread")
                if (conv !is String || !UUID_RE.matches(conv)) return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "conv invalid")
                if (title !is String || title.isEmpty() || title.length > 40) return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "title 1-40")
                if (avatar !is String || avatar.length > 8) return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "avatar max 8")
                if (unread !is Int || unread < 0 || unread > 99) return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "unread 0..99")
                val err = actions.bubbleShow(conv, title, avatar, unread)
                return if (err == null) ok(JSONObject()) else fail(err, "Bula nu a putut porni")
            }
            "bubble.update" -> {
                val unread = p.opt("unread")
                if (unread !is Int || unread < 0 || unread > 99) return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "unread 0..99")
                actions.bubbleUpdate(unread)
                return ok(JSONObject())
            }
            "bubble.hide" -> {
                actions.bubbleHide()
                return ok(JSONObject())
            }
            "host.pending" -> {
                val c = actions.takePendingChat()
                val o = JSONObject()
                if (c != null) o.put("chat", c)
                return ok(o)
            }
            "game.native.list" -> {
                val arr = JSONArray()
                for (g in actions.nativeGames()) arr.put(JSONObject().put("id", g.first).put("runtime", g.second))
                return ok(JSONObject().put("games", arr))
            }
            "game.native.launch" -> {
                val id = p.opt("id")
                if (id !is String || !GAME_ID.matches(id)) return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "id de joc invalid")
                val err = actions.launchNativeGame(id)
                return if (err == null) ok(JSONObject().put("launched", true)) else fail(err, "Jocul nativ nu a putut porni")
            }
            else -> return fail("UNKNOWN_METHOD", "Metodă necunoscută")
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: app/src/main/java/ro/lingurar/hiblue/host/DeviceServices.kt ===
package ro.lingurar.hiblue.host

import android.Manifest
import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.VibrationEffect
import android.os.Vibrator
import ro.lingurar.hiblue.MainActivity

/** Funcții native ale dispozitivului: vibrații și notificări. Apelate doar prin HostBridge. */
class DeviceServices(private val ctx: Context) {

    private companion object {
        const val CHANNEL = "hiblue_default"
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    fun vibrate(kind: Int) {
        try {
            val v = ctx.getSystemService(Context.VIBRATOR_SERVICE) as Vibrator
            if (!v.hasVibrator()) return
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 29) {
                val e = when (kind) {
                    0 -> VibrationEffect.EFFECT_TICK
                    1 -> VibrationEffect.EFFECT_CLICK
                    else -> VibrationEffect.EFFECT_HEAVY_CLICK
                }
                v.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createPredefined(e))
            } else {
                val ms = when (kind) {
                    0 -> 12L
                    1 -> 20L
                    else -> 32L
                }
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
                    v.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createOneShot(ms, VibrationEffect.DEFAULT_AMPLITUDE))
                } else {
                    v.vibrate(ms)
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
        }
    }

    fun notificationsGranted(): Boolean =
        Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 33 ||
            ctx.checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    fun notify(title: String, body: String): Boolean {
        if (!notificationsGranted()) return false
        return try {
            val nm = ctx.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
            val builder: Notification.Builder
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
                nm.createNotificationChannel(NotificationChannel(CHANNEL, "HiBlue", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT))
                builder = Notification.Builder(ctx, CHANNEL)
            } else {
                builder = Notification.Builder(ctx)
            }
            val open = PendingIntent.getActivity(
                ctx, 0,
                Intent(ctx, MainActivity::class.java).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP),
                PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT
            )
            builder.setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.ic_dialog_info)
                .setContentTitle(title)
                .setContentText(body)
                .setAutoCancel(true)
                .setContentIntent(open)
            nm.notify(1001, builder.build())
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }
}

package ro.lingurar.hiblue.nativegames

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent

/**
 * Registru generic pentru jocuri Android native.
 * Jocurile care nu sunt înregistrate nu apar în lista disponibilă și nu pot fi lansate.
 */
class NativeGameEntry(
    val id: String,
    val runtime: String,
    val intentFactory: (Context) -> Intent
)

object NativeGames {
    const val EXTRA_GAME_ID = "hb.game.id"
    const val EXTRA_SCORE = "hb.game.score"
    const val EXTRA_SECONDS = "hb.game.seconds"

    private val idRe = Regex("^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{1,31}$")
    private val runtimes = setOf("kotlin", "godot", "unity")
    private val entries = LinkedHashMap<String, NativeGameEntry>()

    @Synchronized
    fun register(entry: NativeGameEntry) {
        require(idRe.matches(entry.id)) { "id de joc invalid: ${entry.id}" }
        require(entry.runtime in runtimes) { "runtime invalid: ${entry.runtime}" }
        entries[entry.id] = entry
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun get(id: String): NativeGameEntry? = entries[id]

    @Synchronized
    fun list(): List<NativeGameEntry> = entries.values.toList()

    /** Înregistrările jocurilor native integrate în această versiune. */
    fun registerBuiltIn() {
        // Niciun joc nativ integrat în versiunea 0.0.12.
    }
}
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/js/native-bridge.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · NativeBridge (client JS al punții către Android)
   Singurul loc din frontend care vorbește cu partea nativă. Restul codului
   (UI, jocuri, SDK) apelează NativeBridge.*, nu window.HBNative.

   Protocol v1 (JSON, vezi bridge/HostBridge.kt):
     cerere  {v:1, method, params}
     răspuns {v:1, ok:true, data} | {v:1, ok:false, error:{code,message}}
   Toate metodele întorc {ok,data|error}; NU aruncă excepții.
   În browser / fără Android: funcțiile indisponibile întorc {ok:false, error:{code:"UNAVAILABLE"}}
   iar vibrate() cade pe navigator.vibrate când există.

   Evenimente Kotlin -> JS:  NativeBridge.on("lifecycle"|"permission"|"nativeGame", fn)
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var PROTOCOL = 1;
  var listeners = {};
  var cachedInfo = null;

  function raw() {
    try {
      var n = root.HBNative;
      return n && typeof n.invoke === "function" ? n : null;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  function fail(code, message) { return { ok: false, error: { code: code, message: message || code } }; }

  function call(method, params) {
    var n = raw();
    if (!n) return fail("UNAVAILABLE", "Funcția nativă nu este disponibilă");
    if (typeof method !== "string" || !method) return fail("BAD_PARAMS", "method lipsește");
    var out;
    try {
      out = JSON.parse(n.invoke(JSON.stringify({ v: PROTOCOL, method: method, params: params || {} })));
    } catch (e) {
      return fail("INTERNAL", String(e && e.message || e));
    }
    if (out && out.ok === true) return { ok: true, data: out.data || {} };
    var er = out && out.error || {};
    return fail(er.code || "INTERNAL", er.message);
  }

  function info() {
    if (cachedInfo) return cachedInfo;
    var r = call("host.info");
    if (r.ok) cachedInfo = r;
    return r;
  }
  function has(cap) {
    var i = info();
    return !!(i.ok && Array.isArray(i.data.capabilities) && i.data.capabilities.indexOf(cap) >= 0);
  }

  function vibrate(kind) {
    var k = kind === 1 || kind === 2 ? kind : 0;
    if (raw()) return call("device.vibrate", { kind: k }).ok;
    try {
      if (root.navigator && root.navigator.vibrate) return !!root.navigator.vibrate(k === 2 ? 28 : k === 1 ? 18 : 10);
    } catch (e) { /* ignorat */ }
    return false;
  }

  var notifications = {
    granted: function () {
      var r = call("device.notifications.status");
      return r.ok ? !!r.data.granted : false;
    },
    request: function () { return call("device.notifications.request"); },
    notify: function (title, body) {
      return call("device.notify", { title: String(title == null ? "" : title).slice(0, 60), body: String(body == null ? "" : body).slice(0, 200) });
    }
  };

  function share(text) { return call("device.share", { text: String(text == null ? "" : text).slice(0, 1000) }); }

  var nativeGames = {
    list: function () {
      var r = call("game.native.list");
      return r.ok && Array.isArray(r.data.games) ? r.data.games : [];
    },
    launch: function (id) { return call("game.native.launch", { id: id }); }
  };

  /* Audio: implementat în WebAudio (nu există audio nativ în host). Contextul se creează la primul gest. */
  var actx = null;
  var audio = {
    available: function () { return !!(root.AudioContext || root.webkitAudioContext); },
    tone: function (freq, ms, vol) {
      try {
        var AC = root.AudioContext || root.webkitAudioContext;
        if (!AC) return false;
        if (!actx) actx = new AC();
        if (actx.state === "suspended" && actx.resume) actx.resume();
        var f = Math.max(40, Math.min(4000, Number(freq) || 440));
        var d = Math.max(10, Math.min(2000, Number(ms) || 120)) / 1000;
        var v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol == null ? 0.15 : Number(vol) || 0));
        var o = actx.createOscillator(), g = actx.createGain(), t = actx.currentTime;
        o.frequency.value = f;
        g.gain.setValueAtTime(v, t);
        g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + d);
        o.connect(g); g.connect(actx.destination);
        o.start(t); o.stop(t + d + 0.02);
        return true;
      } catch (e) { return false; }
    }
  };

  function on(type, fn) {
    if (typeof fn !== "function") return function () {};
    (listeners[type] = listeners[type] || []).push(fn);
    return function () {
      var a = listeners[type] || [], i = a.indexOf(fn);
      if (i >= 0) a.splice(i, 1);
    };
  }
  /* Apelat DOAR de Kotlin (MainActivity.emit). */
  function emit(type, data) {
    var a = (listeners[type] || []).slice();
    for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      try { a[i](data); } catch (e) { try { console.error("NativeBridge listener", type, e); } catch (x) { } }
    }
  }

  root.NativeBridge = {
    protocol: PROTOCOL,
    get available() { return !!raw(); },
    call: call,
    info: info,
    has: has,
    vibrate: vibrate,
    notifications: notifications,
    nativeGames: nativeGames,
    audio: audio,
    share: share,
    setOrientation: function (mode) { return call("host.orientation", { mode: mode }); },
    on: on,
    _emit: emit
  };
})(window);
=== FILE: docs/HOST_AND_BRIDGE.md ===
# HiBlue · Android Host și NativeBridge

## Rolul Android Host
Android (Kotlin) este stratul nativ de bază. Interfața rămâne în `assets/` (HTML/CSS/JS).

| Responsabilitate | Fișier |
|---|---|
| Lifecycle, WebView, inset-uri, Back, permisiuni | `MainActivity.kt` |
| Puntea JS → Kotlin (listă albă de metode, validare) | `bridge/HostBridge.kt` |
| Vibrații, notificări | `host/DeviceServices.kt` |
| Registru generic pentru jocuri native | `nativegames/NativeGames.kt` |
| Client JS al punții | `assets/js/native-bridge.js` |

## Protocol
JS apelează `window.HBNative.invoke(json)` (prin `NativeBridge.call`). Metodele disponibile includ `host.info`, `device.vibrate`, `device.notifications.status`, `device.notifications.request`, `device.notify`, `host.orientation`, `game.native.list` și `game.native.launch`.

Evenimentele Kotlin → JS includ lifecycle, permisiuni și rezultatul unui joc nativ. Registrul este gol în versiunea 0.0.12; infrastructura este păstrată pentru eventuale jocuri native viitoare.

## Securitate
- Se expune o singură metodă (`invoke`); nu există tokenuri, parole sau chei în punte.
- Puntea refuză apelurile dacă pagina curentă nu provine din `file:///android_asset/...`.
- Navigarea WebView către adrese externe este interceptată; linkurile acceptate se deschid în browserul sistemului.
- WebView nu primește acces general la fișiere externe sau geolocație.

## Originea locală și compatibilitatea
Aplicația continuă să folosească `file:///android_asset/` pentru a păstra originea WebView și datele locale existente ale instalărilor. O schimbare de origine ar necesita o migrare explicită a datelor locale.

function Flyby(host,api){
 const cv=document.createElement("canvas"),c=cv.getContext("2d");host.appendChild(cv);
 const GR=1480,BOOST=-420,RAD=10,GW=60,SP=240,M=76;
 let W,H,dpr,top0=0,S,ship,gates,parts,stars,score,speed,t,tot=0,dist=0,raf,last,lastCY,deadT,flash,shake,pop,ovr;
 function size(){dpr=Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio||1,2.5);W=host.clientWidth;H=host.clientHeight;cv.width=Math.round(W*dpr);cv.height=Math.round(H*dpr);cv.style.width=W+"px";cv.style.height=H+"px";c.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
  top0=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue("--sat"))||0;
  stars=[];for(let i=0;i<70;i++){const l=Math.random();stars.push({x:Math.random()*W,y:Math.random()*H,r:.6+l*1.4,l:.15+l*.85,a:.3+Math.random()*.6})}}
 function reset(){ship={x:W*.3,y:H*.46,vy:0,rot:0};gates=[];parts=[];score=0;speed=190;t=0;dist=0;deadT=0;flash=0;shake=0;pop=0;ovr=false;lastCY=H/2;S="ready";spawn()}
 const gapNow=()=>Math.max(132,196-score*1.6);
 function spawn(){let lx=gates.length?gates[gates.length-1].x:W*.3+SP*.8;
  while(lx<W+SP){lx+=SP;const gap=gapNow();let cy=lastCY+(Math.random()*2-1)*150;cy=Math.max(M+gap/2,Math.min(H-M-gap/2,cy));lastCY=cy;gates.push({x:lx,cy,gap,p:0})}}
 function burst(x,y,n,col,spd){for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=Math.random()*6.283,v=(.3+Math.random())*spd;parts.push({x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*v,vy:Math.sin(a)*v,l:1,d:.6+Math.random()*.7,s:1.5+Math.random()*2.5,c:col})}}
 function die(){if(S!="play")return;S="dead";deadT=0;flash=1;shake=1;burst(ship.x,ship.y,28,"#c4b5fd",280);burst(ship.x,ship.y,18,"#67e8f9",200);api.hap&&api.hap(2)}
 function update(dt){tot+=dt;
  stars.forEach(s=>{s.x-=(S=="play"?speed:36)*s.l*.2*dt;if(s.x<-2){s.x=W+2;s.y=Math.random()*H}});
  if(S=="ready"){ship.y=H*.46+Math.sin(tot*3)*9;ship.rot=Math.sin(tot*3)*.08}
  else if(S=="play"){
   t+=dt;speed=Math.min(340,190+score*4.2);dist+=speed*dt;
   ship.vy+=GR*dt;ship.y+=ship.vy*dt;ship.rot=Math.max(-.5,Math.min(1.1,ship.vy/520));
   if(ship.y<RAD){ship.y=RAD;if(ship.vy<0)ship.vy=0}
   gates.forEach(g=>{g.x-=speed*dt});
   parts.push({x:ship.x-10,y:ship.y,vx:-70-Math.random()*40,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*30,l:1,d:.45,s:2.4,c:"#67e8f9"});
   for(const g of gates){
    if(!g.p&&g.x+GW/2<ship.x-RAD){g.p=1;score++;pop=1;api.hap&&api.hap(0)}
    if(Math.abs(g.x-ship.x)<GW/2+RAD){const a=g.cy-g.gap/2,b=g.cy+g.gap/2;if(ship.y-RAD<a||ship.y+RAD>b){die();break}}}
   if(S=="play"&&ship.y>H-RAD)die();
   if(S=="play"){gates=gates.filter(g=>g.x>-GW);spawn()}}
  else if(S=="dead"){deadT+=dt;if(deadT>.75&&!ovr){ovr=true;api.over(score,Math.round(t))}}
  parts.forEach(p=>{p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.l-=dt/p.d});parts=parts.filter(p=>p.l>0);
  pop=Math.max(0,pop-dt*4);flash=Math.max(0,flash-dt*3);shake=Math.max(0,shake-dt*2.2)}
 function rr(x,y,w,h,r){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+r,y);c.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r);c.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r);c.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r);c.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r);c.closePath()}
 function txt(s,x,y,size,al){c.font="800 "+size+"px ui-rounded,system-ui,sans-serif";c.textAlign="center";c.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,"+al+")";c.fillText(s,x,y)}
 function draw(){c.save();
  if(shake>0)c.translate((Math.random()-.5)*10*shake,(Math.random()-.5)*10*shake);
  const bg=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);bg.addColorStop(0,"#080a22");bg.addColorStop(.55,"#1a0f3f");bg.addColorStop(1,"#0a1d46");c.fillStyle=bg;c.fillRect(-20,-20,W+40,H+40);
  const L=W+300,px=((W*.8-dist*.05)%L+L)%L-150,py=H*.2;
