=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/adapters/native.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · Native Game Adapter
   runtime: "native-kotlin" | "native-godot" | "native-unity"

   STARE: INFRASTRUCTURĂ. Nu există încă niciun joc nativ înregistrat în host (NativeGames.kt),
   deci orice lansare se oprește cu NOT_REGISTERED. Adaptorul este complet față de contract:
     load   -> verifică puntea, capabilitatea "game.native" și că jocul e înregistrat cu runtime-ul potrivit
     start  -> game.native.launch (host-ul pornește Activity-ul jocului)
     rezultat -> evenimentul NativeBridge "nativeGame" {id,status,score,seconds} -> ctx.over / ctx.exit
   Jocul nativ trăiește într-un Activity separat, cu propriul lifecycle: pause/resume/restart sunt no-op aici.
   Scorul primit este tratat ca nesigur: trece prin SDK (scores.submit) și este revalidat pe server.
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;
  GP.adapters = GP.adapters || {};

  function make(runtime, nb) {
    var kind = runtime.slice(7);                // kotlin | godot | unity
    return {
      runtime: runtime,
      nb: nb || null,                           // înlocuibil (teste); implicit window.NativeBridge
      bridge: function () { return this.nb || root.NativeBridge; },
      load: function (manifest) {
        var b = this.bridge();
        if (!b || !b.available || !b.has("game.native")) return Promise.reject(GP.err("RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE", "Jocurile native necesită aplicația Android"));
        var found = (b.nativeGames.list() || []).filter(function (g) { return g.id === manifest.entry && g.runtime === kind; })[0];
        if (!found) return Promise.reject(GP.err("NOT_REGISTERED", "Jocul nativ '" + manifest.entry + "' (" + kind + ") nu este înregistrat în aplicație"));
        return Promise.resolve({ nativeId: manifest.entry, off: null });
      },
      start: function (handle, ctx) {
        var b = this.bridge();
        handle.off = b.on("nativeGame", function (ev) {
          if (!ev || ev.id !== handle.nativeId) return;
          if (ev.status === "finished") { ctx.over(ev.score | 0, ev.seconds | 0); ctx.exit("native-finished"); }
          else if (ev.status === "cancelled") ctx.exit("native-cancelled");
          else ctx.fail("START_FAILED", "Jocul nativ nu a putut porni");
        });
        var r = b.nativeGames.launch(handle.nativeId);
        if (!r.ok) { handle.off(); handle.off = null; throw GP.err(r.error.code === "BUSY" ? "BUSY" : r.error.code === "NOT_REGISTERED" ? "NOT_REGISTERED" : "START_FAILED", r.error.message); }
      },
      pause: function () { }, resume: function () { }, restart: function () { },
      destroy: function (h) { if (h && h.off) { h.off(); h.off = null; } }
    };
  }
  GP.adapters.makeNative = make;
  ["native-kotlin", "native-godot", "native-unity"].forEach(function (r) { GP.adapters[r] = make(r); });
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/adapters/web.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · Web Game Adapter  (runtime "web")
   Rulează jocuri HTML/CSS/JS în WebView-ul existent: NU creează WebView-uri sau iframe-uri suplimentare.
   Jocul primește un element gazdă (host) și un obiect `api`:

     factory(host, api) -> { restart?(), pause?(), resume?(), destroy() }
     api = {
       over(score, secs)   // joc terminat -> platforma trimite scorul prin SDK
       best()              // cel mai bun scor local
       hap(k)              // vibrație 0|1|2
       sdk                 // Shared Game SDK complet (identitate, progres, scoruri, clasamente...)
     }
   Contractul vechi (over/best/hap) este păstrat; jocurile existente rulează nemodificate.
   Jocurile de încredere (first-party) rulează în același context JS: izolarea este de contract, nu de sandbox.
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;
  GP.adapters = GP.adapters || {};
  var pendingScripts = {};

  function defaultLoadScript(src) {
    if (pendingScripts[src]) return pendingScripts[src];
    var p = new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      var s = root.document.createElement("script");
      s.src = src; s.async = false;
      s.onload = function () { res(); };
      s.onerror = function () { delete pendingScripts[src]; rej(GP.err("LOAD_FAILED", "Nu s-a putut încărca " + src)); };
      root.document.head.appendChild(s);
    });
    pendingScripts[src] = p;
    return p;
  }

  var web = {
    runtime: "web",
    loadScript: defaultLoadScript,           // înlocuibil (teste)
    load: function (manifest) {
      var b = GP.registry.getWeb(manifest.id);
      if (b) return Promise.resolve({ factory: b.factory, inst: null });
      return web.loadScript(manifest.entry).then(function () {
        var c = GP.registry.getWeb(manifest.id);
        if (!c) throw GP.err("LOAD_FAILED", "Scriptul " + manifest.entry + " nu a înregistrat jocul " + manifest.id);
        return { factory: c.factory, inst: null };
      });
    },
    start: function (handle, ctx) {
      var api = {
        over: function (score, secs) { ctx.over(score, secs); },
        best: function () { return ctx.localBest(); },
        hap: function (k) { ctx.haptic(k); },
        sdk: ctx.sdk
      };
      var inst = handle.factory(ctx.host, api);
      if (!inst || typeof inst.destroy !== "function") {
        try { if (inst && inst.destroy) inst.destroy(); } catch (e) { }
        throw GP.err("START_FAILED", "Jocul trebuie să returneze un obiect cu destroy()");
      }
      handle.inst = inst;
    },
    pause: function (h) { if (h.inst && typeof h.inst.pause === "function") h.inst.pause(); },
    resume: function (h) { if (h.inst && typeof h.inst.resume === "function") h.inst.resume(); },
    restart: function (h) { if (h.inst && typeof h.inst.restart === "function") h.inst.restart(); },
    destroy: function (h) {
      var i = h && h.inst;
      if (!i) return;
      h.inst = null;
      try { i.destroy(); } catch (e) { try { console.error("destroy joc", e); } catch (x) { } }
    }
  };
  GP.adapters.web = web;
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/backend.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · Backend Supabase
   Singurul modul din platformă care cunoaște REST/RPC Supabase.
   Folosește funcția rest() a aplicației (sesiunea utilizatorului, cheia publishable).
   NU folosește și nu cere cheia service_role; nu expune tokenuri jocurilor.

   Toate metodele întorc:  {ok:true, data}  |  {ok:false, retriable, code, message}
   retriable = eroare de rețea / server (se reîncearcă mai târziu); false = respins definitiv.
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;

  function msgOf(r) {
    var j = r && r.j;
    return (j && (j.message || j.msg || j.error_description || j.error)) || ("HTTP " + (r && r.s));
  }
  function cls(r) {
    if (r && r.ok) return { ok: true, data: r.j };
    var net = !!(r && r.net), s = r ? r.s : 0;
    var code = net ? "OFFLINE" : (s === 401 || s === 403) ? "FORBIDDEN" : s === 404 ? "NOT_FOUND" : s >= 500 ? "INTERNAL" : "BAD_REQUEST";
    return { ok: false, retriable: net || s >= 500 || s === 429 || s === 401, code: code, message: String(msgOf(r)) };
  }

  /* host: { rest(path, opts) -> Promise<{ok,s,j,net}>, session() -> {uid,...}|null, profile() -> {username,handle}|null } */
  GP.createBackend = function (host) {
    function rpc(name, body) { return host.rest("/rest/v1/rpc/" + name, { m: "POST", b: body || {} }).then(cls); }
    return {
      identity: function () {
        var s = host.session(), p = host.profile() || {};
        if (!s || !s.uid) return { kind: "guest", id: null, name: null, handle: null };
        return { kind: "user", id: s.uid, name: p.username || null, handle: p.handle || null };
      },
      submitScore: function (game, score, secs) { return rpc("submit_score", { p_game: game, p_score: score, p_secs: secs }); },
      top: function (game) { return rpc("game_top", { p_game: game }); },
      progressGet: function (game, slot) { return rpc("game_progress_get", { p_game: game, p_slot: slot }); },
      progressPut: function (game, slot, data, base) { return rpc("game_progress_put", { p_game: game, p_slot: slot, p_data: data, p_base: base }); },
      ratingsSummary: function () { return rpc("game_ratings_summary", {}); },
      ratingsMine: function () {
        var s = host.session();
        if (!s) return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, retriable: false, code: "FORBIDDEN", message: "fără sesiune" });
        return host.rest("/rest/v1/game_ratings?user_id=eq." + s.uid + "&select=game_id,rating").then(cls);
      },
      rate: function (game, n) {
        var s = host.session();
        if (!s) return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, retriable: false, code: "FORBIDDEN", message: "fără sesiune" });
        return host.rest("/rest/v1/game_ratings?on_conflict=game_id,user_id", {
          m: "POST", h: { Prefer: "resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal" }, b: { game_id: game, user_id: s.uid, rating: n }
        }).then(cls);
      }
    };
  };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/boot.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · boot
   Leagă platforma de aplicație. Aplicația îi dă DOAR serviciile de care are nevoie:
     rest, session, profile, ls, haptic, toast, esc, settings, rooms
   Jocurile nu primesc niciodată aceste obiecte: ele văd doar `api` / `sdk`.
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;

  /* host: { rest, session(), profile(), ls:{get,set}, haptic(k), toast(t), esc(s), settings, rooms } */
  GP.init = function (host) {
    if (GP.ready) return GP;
    var NB = root.NativeBridge || null;
    var backend = GP.createBackend({ rest: host.rest, session: host.session, profile: host.profile });
    var storage = GP.createStorage({ ls: host.ls, uid: function () { var s = host.session(); return s && s.uid; } });
    var sdkHost = GP.createSdkHost({
      backend: backend, storage: storage,
      identity: backend.identity,
      haptic: function (k) { host.haptic(k); },
      audio: function (f, ms, v) { return NB ? NB.audio.tone(f, ms, v) : false; }
    });
    var adapters = {
      web: GP.adapters.web,
      "native-kotlin": GP.adapters["native-kotlin"],
      "native-godot": GP.adapters["native-godot"],
      "native-unity": GP.adapters["native-unity"]
    };
    Object.keys(adapters).forEach(function (k) { GP.runtime.assertAdapter(adapters[k]); });
    var launcher = GP.createLauncher({ catalog: GP.catalog, adapters: adapters, sdkHost: sdkHost, storage: storage, nb: NB });
    var ui = GP.createUi({
      backend: backend, storage: storage, launcher: launcher, catalog: GP.catalog, registry: GP.registry,
      esc: host.esc, toast: host.toast, settings: host.settings, session: host.session, rooms: host.rooms
    });

    /* lifecycle: host Android (pause/resume) + vizibilitatea paginii */
    if (NB) NB.on("lifecycle", function (e) {
      if (e && e.state === "pause") launcher.pause("host");
      else if (e && e.state === "resume") { launcher.resume("host"); sdkHost.flush().catch(function () { }); }
    });
    if (root.document) root.document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", function () {
      if (root.document.hidden) launcher.pause("hidden");
      else { launcher.resume("visible"); sdkHost.flush().catch(function () { }); }
    });

    GP.services = { backend: backend, storage: storage, sdk: sdkHost, launcher: launcher };
    GP.launcher = launcher;
    GP.back = ui.back;
    GP.reset = function () { ui.reset(); };
    GP.ready = true;
    return GP;
  };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/catalog.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · Game Catalog
   Lista jocurilor + manifestul fiecăruia. Manifestul este DATĂ (fără funcții).

   Manifest:
   {
     id:           "flyby"                       // unic, ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{1,31}$
     name:         "Flyby"
     version:      "0.0.2"                       // x.y.z
     type:         "arcade"                      // categorie liberă (litere mici)
     runtime:      "web" | "native-kotlin" | "native-godot" | "native-unity"
     entry:        "games/flyby.js"              // web: scriptul jocului (cale relativă, doar .js)
                                                 // native-*: id-ul jocului înregistrat în host
     orientation:  "portrait" | "landscape" | "auto"
     capabilities: ["identity","scores",...]     // ce servicii SDK poate folosi jocul
     meta:         { tagline, developer, description }
     launch:       { continueAfterScore: bool }  // informații de lansare
   }
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;
  var RUNTIMES = ["web", "native-kotlin", "native-godot", "native-unity"];
  var ORIENT = ["portrait", "landscape", "auto"];
  var CAPS = ["identity", "progress", "scores", "leaderboard", "sync", "haptics", "audio", "events"];

  function validate(m) {
    var e = [];
    if (!GP.is.obj(m)) return ["manifestul trebuie să fie obiect"];
    if (!GP.is.id(m.id)) e.push("id invalid");
    if (!GP.is.str(m.name, 1, 40)) e.push("name invalid (1-40)");
    if (typeof m.version !== "string" || !/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test(m.version)) e.push("version invalid (x.y.z)");
    if (typeof m.type !== "string" || !/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,23}$/.test(m.type)) e.push("type invalid");
    if (RUNTIMES.indexOf(m.runtime) < 0) e.push("runtime invalid (" + RUNTIMES.join("|") + ")");
    if (m.runtime === "web") {
      if (typeof m.entry !== "string" || !/^[a-z0-9_-]+(\/[a-z0-9_-]+)*\.js$/.test(m.entry)) e.push("entry invalid (cale relativă .js)");
    } else if (RUNTIMES.indexOf(m.runtime) > 0) {
      if (!GP.is.id(m.entry)) e.push("entry invalid (id de joc nativ)");
    }
    if (ORIENT.indexOf(m.orientation) < 0) e.push("orientation invalid");
    if (!Array.isArray(m.capabilities)) e.push("capabilities trebuie să fie listă");
    else {
      var seen = {};
      m.capabilities.forEach(function (c) {
        if (CAPS.indexOf(c) < 0) e.push("capabilitate necunoscută: " + c);
        if (seen[c]) e.push("capabilitate duplicată: " + c);
        seen[c] = 1;
      });
    }
    var meta = m.meta;
    if (!GP.is.obj(meta)) e.push("meta lipsește");
    else {
      if (!GP.is.str(meta.tagline, 0, 60)) e.push("meta.tagline invalid (max 60)");
      if (!GP.is.str(meta.developer, 1, 40)) e.push("meta.developer invalid (1-40)");
      if (!GP.is.str(meta.description, 0, 400)) e.push("meta.description invalid (max 400)");
    }
    if (m.launch !== undefined) {
      if (!GP.is.obj(m.launch)) e.push("launch trebuie să fie obiect");
      else if (m.launch.continueAfterScore !== undefined && typeof m.launch.continueAfterScore !== "boolean") e.push("launch.continueAfterScore invalid");
    }
    return e;
  }

  function copy(m) {
    return Object.freeze({
      id: m.id, name: m.name, version: m.version, type: m.type, runtime: m.runtime, entry: m.entry,
      orientation: m.orientation,
      capabilities: Object.freeze(m.capabilities.slice()),
      meta: Object.freeze({ tagline: m.meta.tagline, developer: m.meta.developer, description: m.meta.description }),
      launch: Object.freeze({ continueAfterScore: !!(m.launch && m.launch.continueAfterScore) })
    });
  }

  var items = [], byId = {}, em = GP.emitter();

  GP.catalog = {
    RUNTIMES: RUNTIMES, CAPABILITIES: CAPS,
    validate: validate,
    /* -> {ok:true, manifest} | {ok:false, errors:[...]} */
    add: function (m) {
      var errs = validate(m);
      if (!errs.length && byId[m.id]) errs.push("DUPLICATE: id deja înregistrat");
      if (errs.length) return { ok: false, errors: errs };
      var c = copy(m);
      items.push(c); byId[c.id] = c;
      em.emit("change", { type: "add", id: c.id });
      return { ok: true, manifest: c };
    },
    get: function (id) { return byId[id] || null; },
    list: function () { return items.slice(); },
    has: function (id) { return !!byId[id]; },
    on: em.on,
    _reset: function () { items = []; byId = {}; }
  };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/core.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · core  (update0.0.7)
   Namespace GamePlatform, erori standard, emitter, validatoare.
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform = root.GamePlatform || {};
  GP.version = "1.0.0";
  GP.SDK_VERSION = 1;

  /* Coduri de eroare comune (platformă + SDK). */
  GP.CODES = [
    "BAD_REQUEST", "BAD_PARAMS", "UNKNOWN_METHOD", "CAPABILITY_DENIED", "SESSION_INVALID",
    "RATE_LIMITED", "NOT_FOUND", "BUSY", "RUNTIME_UNSUPPORTED", "RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE",
    "NOT_REGISTERED", "LOAD_FAILED", "START_FAILED", "TIMEOUT", "OFFLINE", "FORBIDDEN", "INTERNAL"
  ];

  function PlatformError(code, message, details) {
    var e = new Error(message || code);
    e.name = "PlatformError";
    e.code = code;
    if (details !== undefined) e.details = details;
    return e;
  }
  GP.err = PlatformError;
  GP.errInfo = function (e) {
    return { code: (e && e.code) || "INTERNAL", message: String((e && e.message) || e || "Eroare") };
  };

  /* Emitter mic: on(tip, fn) -> off(); emit(tip, date); un listener defect nu oprește ceilalți. */
  GP.emitter = function () {
    var L = {};
    return {
      on: function (type, fn) {
        if (typeof fn !== "function") return function () {};
        (L[type] = L[type] || []).push(fn);
        return function () {
          var a = L[type] || [], i = a.indexOf(fn);
          if (i >= 0) a.splice(i, 1);
        };
      },
      emit: function (type, data) {
        var a = (L[type] || []).slice();
        for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
          try { a[i](data); } catch (e) { try { console.error("GamePlatform listener", type, e); } catch (x) { } }
        }
      },
      clear: function () { L = {}; }
    };
  };

  /* Validatoare (întorc true/false; nu aruncă). */
  GP.is = {
    obj: function (v) { return v !== null && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v); },
    str: function (v, min, max) { return typeof v === "string" && v.length >= min && v.length <= max; },
    int: function (v, min, max) { return typeof v === "number" && isFinite(v) && Math.floor(v) === v && v >= min && v <= max; },
    id: function (v) { return typeof v === "string" && /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{1,31}$/.test(v); },
    slot: function (v) { return typeof v === "string" && /^[a-z0-9_-]{1,24}$/.test(v); }
  };

  /* Id unic de sesiune / token (nu este secret criptografic: autorizează apeluri SDK pentru sesiunea activă). */
  GP.randomId = function () {
    try {
      var c = root.crypto;
      if (c && c.getRandomValues) {
        var a = new Uint32Array(3);
        c.getRandomValues(a);
        return a[0].toString(36) + a[1].toString(36) + a[2].toString(36);
      }
    } catch (e) { }
    return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2) + Date.now().toString(36);
  };
  GP.now = function () { return Date.now(); };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/launcher.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · Game Launcher
   Singurul responsabil de pornirea/oprirea jocurilor.
     launch(id, {host}) -> Promise<{ok:true, session} | {ok:false, error:{code,message}}>
     pause(reason) / resume(reason) / restart() / exit(reason)
     active() ; on("state"|"over"|"error"|"exit", fn)
   O singură sesiune activă. Un joc nu poate lansa alt joc (SDK-ul nu are o astfel de metodă).
   Orice eroare duce la eliberarea completă a resurselor (adaptor, SDK, orientare).
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;
  function noop() { }

  /* deps: { catalog, adapters:{runtime->adaptor}, sdkHost, storage, nb?, loadTimeoutMs? } */
  GP.createLauncher = function (deps) {
    var catalog = deps.catalog, adapters = deps.adapters, sdkHost = deps.sdkHost, storage = deps.storage;
    var nb = deps.nb || null, loadTimeout = deps.loadTimeoutMs || 15000;
    var em = GP.emitter(), active = null;

    function failRes(code, message) { return { ok: false, error: { code: code, message: message || code } }; }

    function withTimeout(p, ms) {
      return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
        var t = setTimeout(function () { rej(GP.err("TIMEOUT", "Încărcarea jocului a durat prea mult")); }, ms);
        p.then(function (v) { clearTimeout(t); res(v); }, function (e) { clearTimeout(t); rej(e); });
      });
    }

    function cleanup(rt) {
      if (rt.cleaned) return;
      rt.cleaned = true;
      if (rt.handle) { try { rt.adapter.destroy(rt.handle); } catch (e) { } rt.handle = null; }
      if (rt.sdkH) { rt.sdkH.dispose(); rt.sdkH = null; }
      if (rt.orient && nb) { try { nb.setOrientation("portrait"); } catch (e) { } }
      if (active === rt) active = null;
    }

    function failure(rt, e) {
      var info = GP.errInfo(e), s = rt.session;
      if (GP.runtime.canTransition(s.state, "error")) s.transition("error");
      em.emit("error", { id: s.gameId, code: info.code, message: info.message });
      cleanup(rt);
      if (GP.runtime.canTransition(s.state, "destroyed")) s.transition("destroyed");
      return failRes(info.code, info.message);
    }

    function makeCtx(rt) {
      var m = rt.session.manifest, sdk = rt.sdkH.sdk;
      return {
        manifest: m, sdk: sdk, host: rt.host,
        localBest: function () { return storage.best(m.id); },
        haptic: function (k) { try { sdk.haptics.vibrate(k).catch(noop); } catch (e) { } },
        exit: function (reason) { exit(reason, rt); },
        fail: function (code, msg) { if (active === rt) failure(rt, GP.err(code, msg)); },
        over: function (score, secs) {
          if (active !== rt || !isFinite(score)) return;
          score = Math.max(0, Math.floor(score));
          secs = Math.max(0, Math.min(86400, Math.floor(secs) || 0));
          var prev = storage.best(m.id), rec = score > prev;
          if (score > 0) storage.setBest(m.id, score);
          var result = (score > 0 && m.capabilities.indexOf("scores") >= 0)
            ? sdk.scores.submit(score, secs).catch(function () { return null; })
            : Promise.resolve(null);
          em.emit("over", { id: m.id, score: score, secs: secs, record: rec, prev: prev, result: result });
        }
      };
    }

    function launch(id, opts) {
      opts = opts || {};
      if (active) return Promise.resolve(failRes("BUSY", "Un alt joc rulează deja"));
      var m = catalog.get(id);
      if (!m) return Promise.resolve(failRes("NOT_FOUND", "Joc necunoscut: " + id));
      var adapter = adapters[m.runtime];
      if (!adapter) return Promise.resolve(failRes("RUNTIME_UNSUPPORTED", "Runtime nesuportat: " + m.runtime));
      var session = GP.runtime.createSession(m);
      var rt = { session: session, adapter: adapter, handle: null, sdkH: null, orient: false, host: opts.host, cleaned: false };
      active = rt;
      session.events.on("state", function (e) { em.emit("state", e); });
      session.transition("loading");
      var loaded;
      try { loaded = withTimeout(Promise.resolve(adapter.load(m, {})), loadTimeout); }
      catch (e) { loaded = Promise.reject(e); }
      return loaded.then(function (h) {
        if (active !== rt) { try { adapter.destroy(h); } catch (e) { } return failRes("INTERNAL", "Lansare anulată"); }
        rt.handle = h;
        if (m.orientation !== "portrait" && nb && nb.has && nb.has("host.orientation")) {
          if (nb.setOrientation(m.orientation).ok) rt.orient = true;
        }
        rt.sdkH = sdkHost.open(session, { requestExit: function (r) { exit(r, rt); } });
        adapter.start(h, makeCtx(rt));
        if (active !== rt) return failRes("INTERNAL", "Lansare anulată");     // jocul a cerut exit/fail în start()
        session.transition("running");
        return { ok: true, session: session };
      }).catch(function (e) {
        if (active !== rt) return failRes(GP.errInfo(e).code, GP.errInfo(e).message);
        return failure(rt, e);
      });
    }

    function exit(reason, only) {
      var rt = active;
      if (!rt || (only && only !== rt)) return false;
      var s = rt.session;
      if (!GP.runtime.canTransition(s.state, "exiting")) return false;
      var secs = s.elapsedSecs();
      s.transition("exiting");
      if (rt.sdkH) rt.sdkH.notify("exit");
      try { sdkHost.flush().catch(noop); } catch (e) { }
      cleanup(rt);
      s.transition("destroyed");
      em.emit("exit", { id: s.gameId, reason: reason || "user", secs: secs });
      return true;
    }

    function pause(reason) {
      var rt = active;
      if (!rt || rt.session.state !== "running") return false;
      try { rt.adapter.pause(rt.handle); } catch (e) { }
      rt.session.transition("paused");
      if (rt.sdkH) rt.sdkH.notify("pause");
      return true;
    }
    function resume(reason) {
      var rt = active;
      if (!rt || rt.session.state !== "paused") return false;
      try { rt.adapter.resume(rt.handle); } catch (e) { }
      rt.session.transition("running");
      if (rt.sdkH) rt.sdkH.notify("resume");
      return true;
    }
    function restart() {
      var rt = active;
      if (!rt || (rt.session.state !== "running" && rt.session.state !== "paused")) return false;
      try { rt.adapter.restart(rt.handle); return true; } catch (e) { failure(rt, e); return false; }
    }

    return {
      launch: launch, exit: function (r) { return exit(r); }, pause: pause, resume: resume, restart: restart,
      active: function () { return active ? active.session : null; },
      on: em.on
    };
  };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/legacy.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · compatibilitate cu contractul vechi (update0.0.2 – 0.0.5)
   Jocurile existente se înregistrează cu:   const G = window.GM; G.games.push({id,name,...,play,cover})
   Acest shim le conectează la Registry/Catalog fără să le modifice.
   Pentru jocuri NOI folosește direct GamePlatform.registry.registerWeb(...) + manifest în games/catalog.js.
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;
  root.GM = {
    games: {
      push: function (def) {
        if (!def || !GP.is.id(def.id) || typeof def.play !== "function") throw GP.err("BAD_PARAMS", "definiție de joc invalidă");
        if (!GP.catalog.has(def.id)) {
          // joc fără manifest: derivăm unul minimal (runtime web, capabilități standard)
          var r = GP.catalog.add({
            id: def.id, name: String(def.name || def.id).slice(0, 40), version: "0.0.0", type: "arcade", runtime: "web",
            entry: "games/" + def.id + ".js", orientation: "portrait",
            capabilities: ["identity", "scores", "leaderboard", "haptics", "events"],
            meta: { tagline: String(def.tag || "").slice(0, 60), developer: String(def.dev || "HiBlue").slice(0, 40), description: String(def.desc || "").slice(0, 400) },
            launch: { continueAfterScore: !!def.continueAfterScore }
          });
          if (!r.ok) throw GP.err("BAD_PARAMS", "manifest derivat invalid: " + r.errors.join("; "));
        }
        GP.registry.registerWeb(def.id, def.play, { cover: def.cover });
        return 1;
      }
    },
    back: function () { return GP.back ? GP.back() : false; },
    reset: function () { if (GP.reset) GP.reset(); }
  };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/registry.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · Game Registry
   Leagă un id din catalog de implementarea lui în runtime-ul Web:
     factory(host, api) -> { restart?(), pause?(), resume?(), destroy() }
     presentation: { cover(k) -> șir SVG }
   Jocurile native nu se înregistrează aici: sunt înregistrate în host (NativeGames.kt).
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;
  var web = {};
  var pres = {};
  GP.registry = {
    registerWeb: function (id, factory, presentation) {
      if (!GP.is.id(id)) throw GP.err("BAD_PARAMS", "id de joc invalid");
      if (typeof factory !== "function") throw GP.err("BAD_PARAMS", "factory trebuie să fie funcție");
      var p = presentation || {};
      web[id] = { factory: factory, presentation: { cover: typeof p.cover === "function" ? p.cover : null } };
      return true;
    },
    /* Copertă / prezentare pentru jocuri care nu au implementare web (ex. native). */
    registerPresentation: function (id, p) {
      if (!GP.is.id(id)) throw GP.err("BAD_PARAMS", "id de joc invalid");
      pres[id] = { cover: p && typeof p.cover === "function" ? p.cover : null };
      return true;
    },
    presentation: function (id) {
      var w = web[id];
      if (w && w.presentation.cover) return w.presentation;
      return pres[id] || null;
    },
    getWeb: function (id) { return web[id] || null; },
    has: function (id) { return !!web[id]; },
    unregister: function (id) { delete web[id]; },
    _reset: function () { web = {}; pres = {}; }
  };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/runtime.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · Game Runtime
   Regulile comune pentru orice joc, indiferent de tehnologie:

   1. Un joc rulează într-o SESIUNE creată de Launcher; doar Launcher o pornește și o oprește.
   2. Un joc nu lansează și nu administrează alte jocuri.
   3. Stări: idle -> loading -> running <-> paused -> exiting -> destroyed ; oricând -> error -> destroyed.
   4. Un adaptor (runtime) implementează:
        load(manifest, ctx)  -> Promise<handle>
        start(handle, ctx)   -> void          (poate arunca)
        pause(handle) / resume(handle) / restart(handle)   (opționale în sens: pot fi no-op)
        destroy(handle)      -> void          (OBLIGATORIU, idempotent, eliberează tot)
   5. ctx (dat adaptorului): { manifest, sdk, host, over(score, secs), fail(code,msg), exit(reason), haptic(k), localBest() }
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;
  var NEXT = {
    idle: ["loading"],
    loading: ["running", "error", "exiting"],
    running: ["paused", "exiting", "error"],
    paused: ["running", "exiting", "error"],
    exiting: ["destroyed"],
    error: ["destroyed"],
    destroyed: []
  };
  var seq = 0;

  function Session(manifest) {
    var st = "idle", t0 = 0, pausedAt = 0, pausedTotal = 0;
    var em = GP.emitter();
    var s = {
      id: "s" + (++seq),
      gameId: manifest.id,
      manifest: manifest,
      token: GP.randomId(),
      events: em,
      get state() { return st; },
      transition: function (to) {
        if ((NEXT[st] || []).indexOf(to) < 0) throw GP.err("INTERNAL", "Tranziție ilegală: " + st + " -> " + to);
        var now = GP.now();
        if (to === "running" && !t0) t0 = now;
        if (to === "paused") pausedAt = now;
        if (st === "paused" && to !== "paused" && pausedAt) { pausedTotal += now - pausedAt; pausedAt = 0; }
        var from = st; st = to;
        em.emit("state", { from: from, to: to, id: s.id, game: manifest.id });
        return s;
      },
      /* secunde de joc efectiv (fără pauze) */
      elapsedSecs: function () {
        if (!t0) return 0;
        var now = GP.now(), p = pausedTotal + (pausedAt ? now - pausedAt : 0);
        return Math.max(0, Math.round((now - t0 - p) / 1000));
      }
    };
    return s;
  }

  var METHODS = ["load", "start", "pause", "resume", "restart", "destroy"];
  GP.runtime = {
    STATES: Object.keys(NEXT),
    createSession: function (manifest) { return Session(manifest); },
    canTransition: function (from, to) { return (NEXT[from] || []).indexOf(to) >= 0; },
    /* un adaptor trebuie să aibă runtime (string) și metodele din contract */
    assertAdapter: function (a) {
      if (!a || typeof a.runtime !== "string") throw GP.err("BAD_PARAMS", "adaptor fără runtime");
      METHODS.forEach(function (m) {
        if (typeof a[m] !== "function") throw GP.err("BAD_PARAMS", "adaptorul " + a.runtime + " nu implementează " + m);
      });
      return a;
    }
  };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/sdk.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Shared Game SDK
   Contract comun pentru ORICE joc (web, kotlin, godot, unity).

   Straturi separate:
     1. API expus jocului ......... obiectul `sdk` (createSdkHost().open(sesiune).sdk)
     2. Protocol de mesaje ........ dispatch(msg): {v:1, id, session, game, method, params}
                                    -> {v:1, id, ok:true, data} | {v:1, id, ok:false, error:{code,message}}
     3. Implementarea Web/host .... serviciile primite la creare (backend, storage, haptic, audio)
     4. Backend ................... platform/backend.js (Supabase)

   Securitate: apelurile sunt autorizate de sesiunea activă (token), jocul și capabilitățile din manifest,
   origine permisă, metode pe listă albă, parametri validați, limite de rată. SDK-ul nu întoarce
   tokenuri, parole sau chei; scorurile sunt doar trimise: validarea reală e pe server (submit_score).
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;
  var MAX_PROGRESS = 64 * 1024, MAX_EVENT = 2048;

  function bad(msg) { return GP.err("BAD_PARAMS", msg); }
  function noParams(p) { if (p !== undefined && !(GP.is.obj(p) && !Object.keys(p).length)) throw bad("fără parametri"); return {}; }
  function slotOf(p) {
    var s = p.slot === undefined ? "main" : p.slot;
    if (!GP.is.slot(s)) throw bad("slot invalid");
    return s;
  }
  function jsonSize(v) {
    try { return JSON.stringify(v).length; } catch (e) { throw bad("date neserializabile"); }
  }
  function obj(p) { if (!GP.is.obj(p)) throw bad("parametrii trebuie să fie obiect"); return p; }

  /* nume -> { cap, check(params)->params curățați, rate:[max, fereastră ms] } ; handler-ul e în createSdkHost */
  var SPEC = {
    "identity.get":      { cap: "identity",    rate: [60, 10000], check: noParams },
    "progress.load":     { cap: "progress",    rate: [20, 60000], check: function (p) { p = obj(p || {}); return { slot: slotOf(p) }; } },
    "progress.save":     { cap: "progress",    rate: [30, 60000], check: function (p) {
        p = obj(p);
        if (!(GP.is.obj(p.data) || Array.isArray(p.data))) throw bad("data trebuie să fie obiect sau listă");
        if (jsonSize(p.data) > MAX_PROGRESS) throw bad("progres prea mare (max 64 KB)");
        return { slot: slotOf(p), data: p.data };
      } },
    "scores.submit":     { cap: "scores",      rate: [10, 60000], check: function (p) {
        p = obj(p);
        if (!GP.is.int(p.score, 0, 1000000000)) throw bad("score întreg 0..1e9");
        if (!GP.is.int(p.secs, 0, 86400)) throw bad("secs întreg 0..86400");
        return { score: p.score, secs: p.secs };
      } },
    "scores.best":       { cap: "scores",      rate: [60, 10000], check: noParams },
    "leaderboard.top":   { cap: "leaderboard", rate: [20, 60000], check: function (p) {
        p = obj(p || {});
        var l = p.limit === undefined ? 10 : p.limit;
        if (!GP.is.int(l, 1, 10)) throw bad("limit 1..10");
        return { limit: l };
      } },
    "sync.flush":        { cap: "sync",        rate: [10, 60000], check: noParams },
    "haptics.vibrate":   { cap: "haptics",     rate: [60, 1000],  check: function (p) {
        p = obj(p);
        if (!GP.is.int(p.kind, 0, 2)) throw bad("kind 0..2");
        return { kind: p.kind };
      } },
    "audio.tone":        { cap: "audio",       rate: [30, 1000],  check: function (p) {
        p = obj(p);
        if (typeof p.freq !== "number" || !(p.freq >= 40 && p.freq <= 4000)) throw bad("freq 40..4000");
        if (!GP.is.int(p.ms, 10, 2000)) throw bad("ms 10..2000");
        var v = p.vol === undefined ? 0.15 : p.vol;
        if (typeof v !== "number" || !(v >= 0 && v <= 1)) throw bad("vol 0..1");
        return { freq: p.freq, ms: p.ms, vol: v };
      } },
    "events.emit":       { cap: "events",      rate: [100, 10000], check: function (p) {
        p = obj(p);
        if (typeof p.name !== "string" || !/^[a-z][a-z0-9_.]{0,31}$/.test(p.name)) throw bad("name invalid");
        if (p.data !== undefined && jsonSize(p.data) > MAX_EVENT) throw bad("data prea mare (max 2 KB)");
        return { name: p.name, data: p.data };
      } },
    "session.end":      { cap: null,          rate: [5, 10000],  check: function (p) {
        p = obj(p || {});
        var r = p.reason === undefined ? "exit" : p.reason;
        if (["exit", "over", "error"].indexOf(r) < 0) throw bad("reason: exit|over|error");
        return { reason: r };
      } }
  };

  /* svc: { backend, storage, identity(), haptic(kind), audio(freq,ms,vol)->bool, now?() } */
  GP.createSdkHost = function (svc) {
    var now = svc.now || GP.now;
    var open = {};                       // token -> intrare sesiune
    var allowed = ["internal", "native-host"];
    var em = GP.emitter();

    function fail(id, code, message) { return { v: 1, id: id, ok: false, error: { code: code, message: message } }; }

    function rateOk(entry, method, rate) {
      var a = entry.rate[method] = entry.rate[method] || [], t = now();
      while (a.length && t - a[0] >= rate[1]) a.shift();
      if (a.length >= rate[0]) return false;
      a.push(t);
      return true;
    }

    /* ---------- handlere ---------- */
    function loadProgress(game, slot) {
      var local = svc.storage.read(game, slot), id = svc.identity();
      if (id.kind !== "user") return Promise.resolve(local ? local.data : null);
      return svc.backend.progressGet(game, slot).then(function (r) {
        if (r.ok && r.data && GP.is.int(r.data.version, 0, 2147483647)) {
          var rem = r.data;
          if (!local || (!local.dirty && rem.version > local.ver)) {
            local = svc.storage.write(game, slot, { data: rem.data, ver: rem.version, dirty: false });
          } else if (local.dirty && rem.version > local.ver) {
            // conflict: păstrăm datele locale, dar ne aliniem la versiunea serverului (ultima scriere câștigă)
            local = svc.storage.write(game, slot, { data: local.data, ver: rem.version, dirty: true });
          }
        }
        return local ? local.data : null;
      });
    }
    function pushProgress(game, slot, tries) {
      var rec = svc.storage.read(game, slot);
      if (!rec || !rec.dirty) return Promise.resolve(true);
      if (svc.identity().kind !== "user") return Promise.resolve(false);
      return svc.backend.progressPut(game, slot, rec.data, rec.ver).then(function (r) {
        if (!r.ok || !r.data) return false;
        if (r.data.conflict && GP.is.int(r.data.version, 0, 2147483647)) {
          svc.storage.write(game, slot, { data: rec.data, ver: r.data.version, dirty: true });
          return tries > 0 ? pushProgress(game, slot, tries - 1) : false;
        }
        if (r.data.ok && GP.is.int(r.data.version, 0, 2147483647)) {
          svc.storage.write(game, slot, { data: rec.data, ver: r.data.version, dirty: false });
          return true;
        }
        return false;
      });
    }
    function saveProgress(game, slot, data) {
      var prev = svc.storage.read(game, slot);
      svc.storage.write(game, slot, { data: data, ver: prev ? prev.ver : 0, dirty: true });
      return pushProgress(game, slot, 1).then(function (synced) { return { saved: true, synced: synced }; });
    }
    function submitScore(game, score, secs) {
      var prev = svc.storage.best(game), rec = score > prev;
      svc.storage.setBest(game, score);
      var base = { record: rec, local_best: Math.max(prev, score) };
      if (svc.identity().kind !== "user") return Promise.resolve(Object.assign({ submitted: false, reason: "guest" }, base));
      return svc.backend.submitScore(game, score, secs).then(function (r) {
        if (r.ok) { svc.storage.clearPending(game); return Object.assign({ submitted: true }, base, r.data || {}); }
        if (r.retriable) { svc.storage.setPending(game, { score: score, secs: secs }); return Object.assign({ submitted: false, reason: "offline" }, base); }
        return Object.assign({ submitted: false, reason: "rejected", message: r.message }, base);
      });
    }
    function flush() {
      var pushed = 0, left = 0;
      if (svc.identity().kind !== "user") return Promise.resolve({ pushed: 0, pending: svc.storage.dirtyList().length + Object.keys(svc.storage.pending()).length });
      var jobs = [];
      svc.storage.dirtyList().forEach(function (d) {
        jobs.push(pushProgress(d.game, d.slot, 1).then(function (ok) { if (ok) pushed++; else left++; }));
      });
      var pend = svc.storage.pending();
      Object.keys(pend).forEach(function (g) {
        var p = pend[g];
        jobs.push(svc.backend.submitScore(g, p.score, p.secs).then(function (r) {
          if (r.ok || !r.retriable) { svc.storage.clearPending(g); if (r.ok) pushed++; } else left++;
        }));
      });
      return Promise.all(jobs).then(function () { return { pushed: pushed, pending: left }; });
    }

    var RUN = {
      "identity.get": function (c) { var i = svc.identity(); return Promise.resolve({ kind: i.kind, id: i.id, name: i.name, handle: i.handle }); },
      "progress.load": function (c, p) { return loadProgress(c.game, p.slot); },
      "progress.save": function (c, p) { return saveProgress(c.game, p.slot, p.data); },
      "scores.submit": function (c, p) { return submitScore(c.game, p.score, p.secs); },
      "scores.best": function (c) { return Promise.resolve({ best: svc.storage.best(c.game) }); },
      "leaderboard.top": function (c, p) {
        if (svc.identity().kind !== "user") return Promise.reject(GP.err("FORBIDDEN", "Clasamentul necesită cont"));
        return svc.backend.top(c.game).then(function (r) {
          if (!r.ok) throw GP.err(r.code, r.message);
          var me = svc.identity().id;
          return (r.data || []).slice(0, p.limit).map(function (x) {
            return { pos: x.pos, name: x.username || x.handle || null, handle: x.handle || null, score: x.score, me: x.user_id === me };
          });
        });
      },
      "sync.flush": function () { return flush(); },
      "haptics.vibrate": function (c, p) { svc.haptic(p.kind); return Promise.resolve({}); },
      "audio.tone": function (c, p) { return Promise.resolve({ played: !!svc.audio(p.freq, p.ms, p.vol) }); },
      "events.emit": function (c, p) { em.emit("event", { game: c.game, name: p.name, data: p.data }); return Promise.resolve({}); },
      "session.end": function (c, p, entry) {
        if (entry.hooks && entry.hooks.requestExit) entry.hooks.requestExit(p.reason);
        return Promise.resolve({});
      }
    };

    /* ---------- protocolul de mesaje ---------- */
    function dispatch(msg, meta) {
      var id = msg && msg.id;
      try {
        if (!GP.is.obj(msg) || msg.v !== 1) return Promise.resolve(fail(id, "BAD_REQUEST", "mesaj invalid"));
        if (!(typeof id === "number" || typeof id === "string")) return Promise.resolve(fail(null, "BAD_REQUEST", "id lipsește"));
        var origin = meta && meta.origin;
        if (allowed.indexOf(origin) < 0) return Promise.resolve(fail(id, "FORBIDDEN", "origine nepermisă"));
        var entry = typeof msg.session === "string" ? open[msg.session] : null;
        if (!entry) return Promise.resolve(fail(id, "SESSION_INVALID", "sesiune inexistentă sau închisă"));
        if (msg.game !== entry.session.gameId) return Promise.resolve(fail(id, "FORBIDDEN", "joc diferit de sesiune"));
        if (typeof msg.method !== "string" || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SPEC, msg.method)) return Promise.resolve(fail(id, "UNKNOWN_METHOD", "metodă necunoscută"));
        var sp = SPEC[msg.method];
        if (sp.cap && entry.session.manifest.capabilities.indexOf(sp.cap) < 0) return Promise.resolve(fail(id, "CAPABILITY_DENIED", "capabilitate lipsă în manifest: " + sp.cap));
        var params = sp.check(msg.params);
        if (!rateOk(entry, msg.method, sp.rate)) return Promise.resolve(fail(id, "RATE_LIMITED", "prea multe apeluri"));
        var ctx = { game: entry.session.gameId };
        return RUN[msg.method](ctx, params, entry).then(
          function (data) { return { v: 1, id: id, ok: true, data: data }; },
          function (e) { var i = GP.errInfo(e); return fail(id, i.code, i.message); }
        );
      } catch (e) {
        var i2 = GP.errInfo(e);
        return Promise.resolve(fail(id, i2.code === "BAD_PARAMS" ? "BAD_PARAMS" : "INTERNAL", i2.message));
      }
    }

    /* ---------- fațada dată jocului ---------- */
    function openSession(session, hooks) {
      var entry = { session: session, hooks: hooks || null, rate: {}, em: GP.emitter() };
      open[session.token] = entry;
      var n = 0;
      function call(method, params) {
        return dispatch({ v: 1, id: ++n, session: session.token, game: session.gameId, method: method, params: params }, { origin: "internal" })
          .then(function (res) { if (res.ok) return res.data; throw GP.err(res.error.code, res.error.message); });
      }
      var m = session.manifest;
      var sdk = Object.freeze({
        version: GP.SDK_VERSION,
        game: Object.freeze({ id: m.id, name: m.name, version: m.version }),
        identity: Object.freeze({ get: function () { return call("identity.get"); } }),
        progress: Object.freeze({
          load: function (slot) { return call("progress.load", slot === undefined ? {} : { slot: slot }); },
          save: function (data, slot) { var p = { data: data }; if (slot !== undefined) p.slot = slot; return call("progress.save", p); }
        }),
        scores: Object.freeze({
          submit: function (score, secs) { return call("scores.submit", { score: score, secs: secs }); },
          best: function () { return call("scores.best").then(function (r) { return r.best; }); }
        }),
        leaderboard: Object.freeze({ top: function (limit) { return call("leaderboard.top", limit === undefined ? {} : { limit: limit }); } }),
        sync: Object.freeze({ flush: function () { return call("sync.flush"); } }),
        haptics: Object.freeze({ vibrate: function (kind) { return call("haptics.vibrate", { kind: kind }); } }),
        audio: Object.freeze({ tone: function (freq, ms, vol) { var p = { freq: freq, ms: ms }; if (vol !== undefined) p.vol = vol; return call("audio.tone", p); } }),
        events: Object.freeze({ emit: function (name, data) { return call("events.emit", { name: name, data: data }); } }),
        session: Object.freeze({
          end: function (reason) { return call("session.end", reason === undefined ? {} : { reason: reason }); },
          onPause: function (fn) { return entry.em.on("pause", fn); },
          onResume: function (fn) { return entry.em.on("resume", fn); },
          onExit: function (fn) { return entry.em.on("exit", fn); }
        })
      });
      return {
        sdk: sdk,
        notify: function (type) { entry.em.emit(type, {}); },
        dispose: function () { delete open[session.token]; entry.em.clear(); }
      };
    }

    return {
      open: openSession,
      dispatch: dispatch,
      flush: flush,
      allowOrigin: function (o) { if (typeof o === "string" && o && allowed.indexOf(o) < 0) allowed.push(o); },
      on: em.on,
      methods: function () { return Object.keys(SPEC); }
    };
  };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/platform/storage.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Game Platform · Game Storage
   Stocare locală (local-first) pentru progres, cel mai bun scor și scoruri în așteptare.
   Totul este izolat pe utilizator: cheia conține uid-ul (sau "guest").
   Chei (LS adaugă prefixul "hb."):
     gp.<uid>.<joc>.<slot>   {d: date, v: versiune server cunoscută, ts, dirty}
     gp.<uid>.index          ["joc:slot", ...]  (înregistrări de sincronizat)
     gp.<uid>.pending        {joc: {score, secs}}  (scoruri netrimise: offline)
     best.<uid|x>.<joc>      cel mai bun scor local (format păstrat din 0.0.2)
   ===================================================================== */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  var GP = root.GamePlatform;

  GP.createStorage = function (opts) {
    var ls = opts.ls, uid = opts.uid, now = opts.now || GP.now;
    var who = function () { return uid() || "guest"; };
    var pkey = function (g, s) { return "gp." + who() + "." + g + "." + s; };
    var ikey = function () { return "gp." + who() + ".index"; };
    var qkey = function () { return "gp." + who() + ".pending"; };
    var bkey = function (g) { return "best." + (uid() || "x") + "." + g; };

    function index() { var a = ls.get(ikey()); return Array.isArray(a) ? a : []; }
    function setIndex(a) { ls.set(ikey(), a.length ? a : null); }

    function read(game, slot) {
      var r = ls.get(pkey(game, slot));
      if (!GP.is.obj(r) || r.d === undefined || !GP.is.int(r.v, 0, 2147483647)) return null;
      return { data: r.d, ver: r.v, ts: r.ts || 0, dirty: !!r.dirty };
    }
    function write(game, slot, rec) {
      ls.set(pkey(game, slot), { d: rec.data, v: rec.ver | 0, ts: rec.ts || now(), dirty: !!rec.dirty });
      var id = game + ":" + slot, ix = index(), i = ix.indexOf(id);
      if (rec.dirty && i < 0) { ix.push(id); setIndex(ix); }
      if (!rec.dirty && i >= 0) { ix.splice(i, 1); setIndex(ix); }
      return read(game, slot);
    }
    function dirtyList() {
      var out = [];
      index().forEach(function (id) {
        var p = id.split(":"), r = read(p[0], p[1]);
        if (r && r.dirty) out.push({ game: p[0], slot: p[1], rec: r });
      });
      return out;
    }
    function best(game) { return +ls.get(bkey(game)) || 0; }
    function setBest(game, score) { if (score > best(game)) ls.set(bkey(game), score); }

    function pending() { var o = ls.get(qkey()); return GP.is.obj(o) ? o : {}; }
    function setPending(game, p) {
      var o = pending(), cur = o[game];
      if (cur && cur.score >= p.score) return;
      o[game] = { score: p.score, secs: p.secs };
      ls.set(qkey(), o);
    }
    function clearPending(game) {
      var o = pending();
      if (!(game in o)) return;
      delete o[game];
      ls.set(qkey(), Object.keys(o).length ? o : null);
    }
    return { read: read, write: write, dirtyList: dirtyList, best: best, setBest: setBest, pending: pending, setPending: setPending, clearPending: clearPending };
  };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
