/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Social · Feed (update0.0.10)
   Camera Feed: bandă „+” + stories prietenilor, apoi postările. Două vizualizări: „Pentru tine” (prieteni + public)
   și „Prieteni”. Paginare infinită, reîmprospătare automată la revenire, stare goală utilă.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  var S = window.Social;

  Rooms.feed = (function () {
    var list = null, strip = null, scope = "all", loadedAt = 0, root = null;
    function empty() {
      return '<div class="fd-empty"><span>' + I("people") + '</span><h3>' + (scope === "friends" ? "Încă nicio postare de la prieteni" : "Feed-ul tău e liniștit") + '</h3><p>Caută oameni, trimite cereri de prietenie sau creează prima postare.</p><div class="fd-ea"><button class="btn sm" data-k="search">' + I("search") + 'Caută oameni</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-k="create">' + I("plus") + 'Creează</button></div></div>';
    }
    function setup(el) {
      root = el;
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="fd"><div class="fd-seg"><button class="on" data-s="all">Pentru tine</button><button data-s="friends">Prieteni</button><i></i><button class="ib sm rf" data-k="refresh" aria-label="Reîmprospătează">' + I("refresh") + '</button></div>' +
        '<div class="strip" id="fdStrip"></div><div id="fdPosts"></div></div>';
      strip = S.storyStrip(el.querySelector("#fdStrip"));
      mountList();
      el.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        var b = e.target.closest("[data-s],[data-k]"); if (!b) return;
        if (b.dataset.s) {
          scope = b.dataset.s; el.querySelectorAll(".fd-seg [data-s]").forEach(function (x) { x.classList.toggle("on", x === b); });
          el.querySelector(".fd-seg").dataset.on = scope; list.refresh(); return;
        }
        if (b.dataset.k === "refresh") return refresh(true);
        if (b.dataset.k === "search") return S.openSearch();
        if (b.dataset.k === "create") return S.compose.open();
      });
    }
    function mountList() {
      list = S.postList(root.querySelector("#fdPosts"), {
        scroller: root,
        fetch: function (before) { return S.api("feed_page", { p_scope: scope, p_before: before, p_limit: 12 }).then(function (r) { loadedAt = Date.now(); return r; }); },
        empty: empty()
      });
      list.refresh();
    }
    function refresh(force) {
      if (!list) return;
      strip.refresh(); if (force) root.scrollTop = 0; return list.refresh();
    }
    S.on("post-added", function (p) { if (list && root && root.isConnected) { root.scrollTop = 0; list.add(p); } });
    S.on("reset", function () { list = null; strip = null; loadedAt = 0; if (root) root.innerHTML = ""; root = null; scope = "all"; });
    return {
      mount: setup,
      show: function () {
        if (!list) return;
        strip.refresh();
        if (Date.now() - loadedAt > 120000 && root.scrollTop < 80) list.refresh();
      }
    };
  })();
})();
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/social/posts.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Social · postări (update0.0.10)
   Card de postare (text, media, sondaj, distribuire), listă cu paginare, comentarii, partajare, lightbox.
   Toată comunicarea cu serverul trece prin Social.api (RPC). Textul utilizatorului este ÎNTOTDEAUNA escapat.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  var S = window.Social, e2 = esc;

  /* ---------- text bogat (escapat) ---------- */
  S.rich = function (text) {
    var t = e2(text || "");
    t = t.replace(/(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g, function (u) { return '<a href="' + u + '" rel="noopener">' + u.replace(/^https?:\/\//, "").slice(0, 38) + '</a>'; });
    t = t.replace(/(^|[\s>])(#[\wăâîșțĂÂÎȘȚ]{2,30})/g, '$1<span class="tag">$2</span>');
    t = t.replace(/(^|[\s>])(@[a-z0-9_.]{3,20})/g, '$1<span class="tag">$2</span>');
    return t.replace(/\n/g, "<br>");
  };

  var hidden = function () { return LS.get("hiddenPosts") || {}; };

  /* ---------- media ---------- */
  function ratio(m) {
    var w = +m.w || 0, h = +m.h || 0; if (!w || !h) return "4/5";
    var r = w / h; r = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.91, r)); return r.toFixed(3);
  }
  function mediaItem(m, i, eager) {
    var u = e2(m.url);
    if (m.type === "video") return '<div class="mi" data-a="media" data-i="' + i + '"><video src="' + u + '" ' + (m.poster ? 'poster="' + e2(m.poster) + '"' : "") + ' controls playsinline preload="metadata"></video></div>';
    return '<div class="mi" data-a="media" data-i="' + i + '"><img src="' + u + '" alt="" loading="' + (eager ? "eager" : "lazy") + '" draggable="false"></div>';
  }
  function mediaHTML(list) {
    if (!list || !list.length) return "";
    if (list.length === 1) return '<div class="pm one" style="aspect-ratio:' + ratio(list[0]) + '">' + mediaItem(list[0], 0, true) + '</div>';
    return '<div class="pm car" style="aspect-ratio:' + ratio(list[0]) + '"><div class="cv">' + list.map(function (m, i) { return mediaItem(m, i, i === 0); }).join("") + '</div><span class="cn">1/' + list.length + '</span></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- sondaj ---------- */
  function pollHTML(p) {
    var q = p.poll; if (!q) return "";
    var multi = q.multiple && !q.closed && (!q.voted || q.allow_change);
    var sel = p._sel || (p._sel = q.options.filter(function (o) { return o.mine; }).map(function (o) { return o.id; }));
    var total = q.total || 0;
    var opts = q.options.map(function (o) {
      var pct = q.show && total ? Math.round((o.votes || 0) / total * 100) : 0;
      var picked = multi ? sel.indexOf(o.id) >= 0 : o.mine;
      var cls = "po" + (picked ? " mine" : "") + (q.quiz && o.correct === true ? " ok" : "") + (q.quiz && o.mine && o.correct === false ? " no" : "") + (multi ? " mu" : "");
      var mark = q.quiz && o.correct === true ? "✓ " : (q.quiz && o.mine && o.correct === false ? "✗ " : "");
      var dis = q.closed || (q.voted && !q.allow_change && !multi);
      return '<button class="' + cls + '" data-a="vote" data-o="' + o.id + '"' + (dis ? " disabled" : "") + '><i class="pbar" style="width:' + (q.show ? pct : 0) + '%"></i><span class="pbox"></span><span class="pl">' + e2(o.label) + '</span><span class="pv">' + mark + (q.show ? pct + "%" : "") + '</span></button>';
    }).join("");
    var same = JSON.stringify(sel.slice().sort()) === JSON.stringify(q.options.filter(function (o) { return o.mine; }).map(function (o) { return o.id; }).sort());
    var tags = [];
    if (q.quiz) tags.push("Quiz"); if (q.multiple) tags.push("Alegere multiplă"); if (q.anonymous) tags.push("Anonim");
    if (q.results === "after_vote") tags.push("Rezultate după vot"); if (q.results === "after_close") tags.push("Rezultate la final");
    var meta = [];
    if (q.show) meta.push(total + (total === 1 ? " vot" : " voturi"));
    if (q.closes_at) meta.push(S.until(q.closes_at));
    return '<div class="pq" data-q="1"><div class="pq-t">' + I("pollI") + '<b>' + e2(q.question) + '</b></div><div class="pq-o">' + opts + '</div>' +
      (multi && !same ? '<button class="btn sm pq-go" data-a="submit">Votează</button>' : "") +
      (q.explanation ? '<p class="pq-x">' + e2(q.explanation) + '</p>' : "") +
      '<div class="pq-m">' + meta.map(e2).join(" · ") + (!q.anonymous && q.show && total ? ' · <button class="lnk" data-a="voters">Votanți</button>' : "") + (q.allow_add && !q.closed ? ' · <button class="lnk" data-a="addopt">+ Adaugă opțiune</button>' : "") + '</div>' +
      (tags.length ? '<div class="pq-g">' + tags.map(function (t) { return '<span class="chip">' + e2(t) + '</span>'; }).join("") + '</div>' : "") + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- card ---------- */
  function sharedHTML(sh) {
    if (!sh) return "";
    return '<div class="psh"><div class="psh-h">' + S.av(sh.author, 24) + '<span>' + S.nameLine(sh.author || {}) + '</span><small>' + S.ago(sh.created_at) + '</small></div>' +
      (sh.body ? '<div class="psh-b">' + S.rich(sh.body.length > 280 ? sh.body.slice(0, 280) + "…" : sh.body) + '</div>' : "") + mediaHTML((sh.media || []).slice(0, 1)) + '</div>';
  }
  S.postHTML = function (p) {
    var a = p.author || {}, long = (p.body || "").length > 260 || (p.body || "").split("\n").length > 6;
    return '<article class="pc" data-pid="' + e2(p.id) + '">' +
      '<header class="pc-h">' + S.av(a, 42) + '<div class="pc-w"><div class="pc-n" data-prof="' + e2(a.id) + '">' + S.nameLine(a) + '</div><small>' + S.ago(p.created_at) + (p.edited_at ? " · editat" : "") + ' · ' + (p.audience === "friends" ? "Prieteni" : "Public") + '</small></div>' +
      '<button class="ib sm" data-a="more" aria-label="Mai multe">' + I("dots") + '</button></header>' +
      (p.body ? '<div class="pc-b' + (long ? " clamp" : "") + '">' + S.rich(p.body) + '</div>' + (long ? '<button class="lnk pc-x" data-a="expand">mai mult</button>' : "") : "") +
      sharedHTML(p.shared) + mediaHTML(p.media) + pollHTML(p) +
      '<footer class="pc-f"><button class="ac' + (p.liked ? " on" : "") + '" data-a="like">' + I("heart") + '<span class="n">' + (p.likes ? S.count(p.likes) : "") + '</span></button>' +
      '<button class="ac" data-a="cmt">' + I("cmt") + '<span class="n">' + (p.comments ? S.count(p.comments) : "") + '</span></button>' +
      '<button class="ac" data-a="share">' + I("share") + '</button><span class="grow"></span>' +
      '<button class="ac' + (p.saved ? " on sv" : "") + '" data-a="save" aria-label="Salvează">' + I("bookmark") + '</button></footer>' +
      (p.likes ? '<button class="pc-l lnk" data-a="likers">' + S.count(p.likes) + (p.likes === 1 ? " apreciere" : " aprecieri") + '</button>' : "") + '</article>';
  };

  /* ---------- listă cu paginare + evenimente ---------- */
  /* o: { fetch(before)->Promise<{ok,data,err}>, scroller, cursor(item)->iso, empty, pageSize } */
  S.postList = function (el, o) {
    var items = [], done = false, loading = false, gen = 0, size = o.pageSize || 12;
    var cursor = o.cursor || function (p) { return p.created_at; };
    el.classList.add("pl");
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="pl-i"></div><div class="pl-m"></div>';
    var box = el.firstChild, more = el.lastChild, scroller = o.scroller || el;
    var find = function (id) { for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) if (items[i].id === id) return items[i]; return null; };
    var vis = function () { var h = hidden(); return items.filter(function (p) { return !h[p.id]; }); };
    function paint() {
      var v = vis();
      box.innerHTML = v.map(S.postHTML).join("");
      if (!v.length && done) more.innerHTML = o.empty || '<p class="note">Nimic de afișat.</p>';
      else more.innerHTML = loading ? '<div class="sk"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>' : (done ? "" : '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-a="more-load">Mai multe</button>');
    }
    function load(reset) {
      if (loading || (done && !reset)) return Promise.resolve();
      if (reset) { gen++; items = []; done = false; }
      loading = true; var my = gen; paint();
      var before = items.length ? cursor(items[items.length - 1]) : null;
      return o.fetch(before).then(function (r) {
        if (my !== gen) return;
        loading = false;
        if (!r.ok) { more.innerHTML = '<p class="note">' + e2(r.err) + '</p><button class="btn sm ghost" data-a="more-load">Reîncearcă</button>'; return; }
        var a = r.data || []; items = items.concat(a); if (a.length < size) done = true;
        paint();
      });
    }
    function replace(p) { var c = box.querySelector('[data-pid="' + p.id + '"]'); if (c) { var t = document.createElement("div"); t.innerHTML = S.postHTML(p); c.replaceWith(t.firstChild); } }
    function refreshCard(p) { var c = box.querySelector('[data-pid="' + p.id + '"]'); if (!c) return; var f = c.querySelector(".pc-f"), l = c.querySelector(".pc-l"); var t = document.createElement("div"); t.innerHTML = S.postHTML(p); var n = t.firstChild; f.replaceWith(n.querySelector(".pc-f")); if (l) l.remove(); var nl = n.querySelector(".pc-l"); if (nl) c.appendChild(nl); }
    function replacePoll(p) { var c = box.querySelector('[data-pid="' + p.id + '"] .pq'); if (!c) return; var t = document.createElement("div"); t.innerHTML = pollHTML(p); c.replaceWith(t.firstChild); }
    function drop(id) { items = items.filter(function (p) { return p.id !== id; }); paint(); }

    var lastTap = 0, lastEl = null;
    el.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest("[data-a]"); if (!b) return;
      var a = b.dataset.a;
      if (a === "more-load") { load(false); return; }
      var card = b.closest(".pc"); if (!card) return;
      var p = find(card.dataset.pid); if (!p) return;
      if (a === "media") {
        var now = Date.now();
        if (lastEl === b && now - lastTap < 300) { lastTap = 0; if (!p.liked) toggleLike(p, true); burst(b); return; }
        lastTap = now; lastEl = b;
        if (b.querySelector("video")) return;
        setTimeout(function () { if (lastTap === now) S.openLightbox(p.media, +b.dataset.i); }, 310);
        return;
      }
      if (a === "like") return toggleLike(p);
      if (a === "save") return toggleSave(p);
      if (a === "cmt") return S.openComments(p, function () { refreshCard(p); });
      if (a === "share") return S.sharePost(p);
      if (a === "more") return postMenu(p);
      if (a === "likers") return S.showUsers("Aprecieri", S.api("post_likers", { p_id: p.id }));
      if (a === "expand") { var bd = card.querySelector(".pc-b"); bd.classList.toggle("clamp"); b.textContent = bd.classList.contains("clamp") ? "mai mult" : "mai puțin"; return; }
      if (a === "vote") return vote(p, +b.dataset.o);
      if (a === "submit") return submitMulti(p);
      if (a === "addopt") return addOpt(p);
      if (a === "voters") return voters(p);
    });
    el.addEventListener("scroll", function () { }, { passive: true });
    var onScroll = function () { var s = scroller; if (!done && !loading && s.scrollHeight - s.scrollTop - s.clientHeight < 700) load(false); };
    scroller.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    el.addEventListener("scroll", function (e) {
      var c = e.target; if (c && c.classList && c.classList.contains("cv")) {
        var k = Math.round(c.scrollLeft / Math.max(1, c.clientWidth)), n = c.parentNode.querySelector(".cn"); if (n) n.textContent = (k + 1) + "/" + c.children.length;
      }
    }, true);

    function burst(host) {
      var h = document.createElement("span"); h.className = "burst"; h.innerHTML = I("heart"); host.appendChild(h); setTimeout(function () { h.remove(); }, 700);
    }
    function toggleLike(p, force) {
      var on = force != null ? force : !p.liked; if (on === p.liked) return;
      p.liked = on; p.likes += on ? 1 : -1; refreshCard(p); if (on) hap(0);
      S.api("post_like", { p_id: p.id, p_on: on }).then(function (r) {
        if (!r.ok) { p.liked = !on; p.likes += on ? -1 : 1; refreshCard(p); toast(r.err); return; }
        p.likes = r.data.likes; refreshCard(p);
      });
    }
    function toggleSave(p) {
      var on = !p.saved; p.saved = on; refreshCard(p); toast(on ? "Salvat la favorite" : "Eliminat din salvate");
      S.api("post_save", { p_id: p.id, p_on: on }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) { p.saved = !on; refreshCard(p); toast(r.err); } else S.emit("saved", { id: p.id, on: on }); });
    }
    function postMenu(p) {
      var a = p.author || {}, own = p.mine;
      S.menu([
        { ic: "bookmark", t: p.saved ? "Elimină din salvate" : "Salvează", fn: function () { toggleSave(p); } },
        p.body ? { ic: "copy", t: "Copiază textul", fn: function () { S.copy(p.body); } } : null,
        own ? { ic: "edit", t: "Editează textul", fn: function () { editPost(p); } } : null,
        own ? { ic: "trash", t: "Șterge postarea", danger: true, fn: function () { delPost(p); } } : null,
        !own ? { ic: "close", t: "Ascunde postarea", s: "Nu o vei mai vedea pe acest telefon", fn: function () { var h = hidden(); h[p.id] = 1; LS.set("hiddenPosts", h); paint(); } } : null,
        !own ? { ic: "flag", t: "Raportează postarea", danger: true, fn: function () { S.report("post", p.id, a); } } : null,
        !own ? { ic: "ban", t: "Blochează @" + (a.handle || "utilizator"), danger: true, fn: function () { S.blockUser(a); } } : null
      ]);
    }
    function delPost(p) {
      ask("Ștergi postarea?", "Va fi ștearsă definitiv, împreună cu comentariile.", "Șterge").then(function (ok) {
        if (!ok) return;
        S.api("post_delete", { p_id: p.id }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); drop(p.id); toast("Postare ștearsă"); S.emit("post-removed", { id: p.id }); });
      });
    }
    function editPost(p) {
      var sh = S.sheet('<textarea class="ta" id="epT" maxlength="3000"></textarea><button class="btn" id="epGo">Salvează</button>', { title: "Editează postarea" });
      var ta = sh.body.querySelector("#epT"); ta.value = p.body || "";
      sh.body.querySelector("#epGo").addEventListener("click", function () {
        var btn = this; btn.classList.add("busy");
        S.api("post_edit", { p_id: p.id, p_body: ta.value }).then(function (r) {
          btn.classList.remove("busy"); if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err);
          Object.assign(p, r.data, { _sel: null }); replace(p); sh.close();
        });
      });
    }
    /* sondaj */
    function applyPoll(p, r) { if (!r.ok) { toast(r.err); return; } p.poll = r.data; p._sel = null; replacePoll(p); }
    function vote(p, oid) {
      var q = p.poll; if (!q || q.closed) return;
      if (q.multiple) { var s = p._sel || (p._sel = []), i = s.indexOf(oid); if (i >= 0) s.splice(i, 1); else s.push(oid); replacePoll(p); return; }
      var mine = q.options.filter(function (o) { return o.mine; })[0];
      var ids = (mine && mine.id === oid) ? [] : [oid];
      if (mine && mine.id === oid && !q.allow_change) return;
      if (q.voted && !q.allow_change) return;
      S.api("poll_vote", { p_post: p.id, p_options: ids }).then(function (r) { applyPoll(p, r); });
    }
    function submitMulti(p) { S.api("poll_vote", { p_post: p.id, p_options: (p._sel || []).slice() }).then(function (r) { applyPoll(p, r); if (r.ok) hap(0); }); }
    function addOpt(p) {
      var sh = S.sheet('<input class="inp" id="aoT" maxlength="100" placeholder="Opțiune nouă"><button class="btn" id="aoGo">Adaugă</button>', { title: "Adaugă o opțiune" });
      sh.body.querySelector("#aoGo").addEventListener("click", function () {
        var t = sh.body.querySelector("#aoT").value.trim(); if (!t) return;
        S.api("poll_add_option", { p_post: p.id, p_label: t }).then(function (r) { if (r.ok) sh.close(); applyPoll(p, r); });
      });
    }
    function voters(p) {
      var q = p.poll; var sh = S.sheet('<p class="note">Se încarcă…</p>', { title: "Votanți", cls: "tall" });
      Promise.all(q.options.map(function (o) { return S.api("poll_voters", { p_post: p.id, p_option: o.id }).then(function (r) { return { o: o, u: r.ok ? r.data : [] }; }); })).then(function (all) {
        sh.body.innerHTML = all.map(function (x) {
          return '<div class="vg"><div class="vg-t">' + e2(x.o.label) + ' <small>' + (x.o.votes || 0) + '</small></div>' + (x.u.length ? x.u.map(function (u) { return '<div class="usr2 ro">' + S.av(u, 32) + '<span class="ui"><b>' + e2(u.name || "") + '</b></span></div>'; }).join("") : '<p class="note">Niciun vot</p>') + '</div>';
        }).join("");
      });
    }

    var off = S.on("relation", function () { if (!el.isConnected) { off(); return; } load(true); });
    return {
      refresh: function () { return load(true); },
      more: function () { return load(false); },
      add: function (p) { items.unshift(p); paint(); },
      remove: drop,
      count: function () { return items.length; },
      destroy: function () { off(); scroller.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll); }
    };
  };

  /* ---------- listă de utilizatori într-un sheet ---------- */
  S.showUsers = function (title, promise) {
    var sh = S.sheet('<p class="note">Se încarcă…</p>', { title: title, cls: "tall" });
    promise.then(function (r) {
      if (!r.ok) { sh.body.innerHTML = '<p class="note">' + e2(r.err) + '</p>'; return; }
      var a = r.data || [];
      sh.body.innerHTML = a.length ? a.map(function (u) {
        return '<div class="usr2" data-prof="' + e2(u.id) + '">' + S.av(u, 40, { nav: false }) + '<span class="ui"><b>' + e2(u.name || "") + '</b><small>@' + e2(u.handle || "") + '</small></span></div>';
      }).join("") : '<p class="note">Nimeni încă.</p>';
    });
    return sh;
  };

  /* ---------- comentarii (fără previzualizare în feed: doar în acest panou) ---------- */
  S.openComments = function (post, onChange) {
    var list = [], replyTo = null;
    var sh = S.sheet('<div class="cm-l"><p class="note">Se încarcă…</p></div><div class="cm-bar"><div class="cm-r" hidden></div><div class="cm-i"><input id="cmT" maxlength="1000" placeholder="Scrie un comentariu…" autocomplete="off"><button class="ib" id="cmGo" aria-label="Trimite">' + I("share") + '</button></div></div>', { title: "Comentarii", cls: "tall cm" });
    var L = sh.body.querySelector(".cm-l"), T = sh.body.querySelector("#cmT"), R = sh.body.querySelector(".cm-r");
    function item(c, reply) {
      var a = c.author || {};
      return '<div class="cm' + (reply ? " rp" : "") + '" data-c="' + e2(c.id) + '">' + S.av(a, reply ? 28 : 34) + '<div class="cm-m"><div class="cm-b"><b data-prof="' + e2(a.id) + '">' + e2(a.name || a.handle || "") + '</b> ' + S.rich(c.body) + '</div><div class="cm-a"><span>' + S.ago(c.created_at) + '</span>' +
        '<button class="lnk" data-k="reply">Răspunde</button><button class="lnk" data-k="more">⋯</button></div></div>' +
        '<button class="cm-l2' + (c.liked ? " on" : "") + '" data-k="like">' + I("heart") + (c.likes ? '<small>' + c.likes + '</small>' : "") + '</button></div>';
    }
    function paint() {
      var top = list.filter(function (c) { return !c.parent; }), rep = {};
      list.forEach(function (c) { if (c.parent) (rep[c.parent] = rep[c.parent] || []).push(c); });
      L.innerHTML = top.length ? top.map(function (c) { return item(c, false) + (rep[c.id] || []).map(function (r) { return item(r, true); }).join(""); }).join("") : '<p class="note">Fii primul care comentează.</p>';
      var cnt = sh.box.querySelector(".sh-t span"); if (cnt) cnt.textContent = "Comentarii" + (list.length ? " · " + list.length : "");
      post.comments = list.length; if (onChange) onChange();
    }
    S.api("comments_list", { p_post: post.id }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) { L.innerHTML = '<p class="note">' + e2(r.err) + '</p>'; return; } list = r.data || []; paint(); });
    function setReply(c) { replyTo = c; R.hidden = !c; R.innerHTML = c ? 'Răspunzi lui <b>' + e2(c.author.name || c.author.handle || "") + '</b> <button class="lnk" data-k="x">anulează</button>' : ""; if (c) T.focus(); }
    R.addEventListener("click", function (e) { if (e.target.closest('[data-k="x"]')) setReply(null); });
    function send() {
      var t = T.value.trim(); if (!t) return;
      var btn = sh.body.querySelector("#cmGo"); btn.disabled = true;
      S.api("comment_add", { p_post: post.id, p_body: t, p_parent: replyTo ? replyTo.id : null }).then(function (r) {
        btn.disabled = false; if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err);
        T.value = ""; list.push(r.data); setReply(null); paint(); L.scrollTop = L.scrollHeight;
      });
    }
    sh.body.querySelector("#cmGo").addEventListener("click", send);
    T.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) { if (e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); send(); } });
    L.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest("[data-k]"); if (!b) return;
      var row = b.closest(".cm"), c = list.filter(function (x) { return x.id === row.dataset.c; })[0]; if (!c) return;
      var k = b.dataset.k;
      if (k === "reply") return setReply(c);
      if (k === "like") {
        var on = !c.liked; c.liked = on; c.likes += on ? 1 : -1; paint();
        S.api("comment_like", { p_id: c.id, p_on: on }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) { c.liked = !on; c.likes += on ? -1 : 1; paint(); toast(r.err); } });
        return;
      }
      if (k === "more") {
        S.menu([
          { ic: "copy", t: "Copiază", fn: function () { S.copy(c.body); } },
          c.can_delete ? { ic: "trash", t: "Șterge comentariul", danger: true, fn: function () {
            S.api("comment_delete", { p_id: c.id }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); list = list.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== c.id && x.parent !== c.id; }); paint(); }); } } : null,
          !c.mine ? { ic: "flag", t: "Raportează", danger: true, fn: function () { S.report("comment", c.id, c.author); } } : null
        ]);
      }
    });
    return sh;
  };

  /* ---------- partajare ---------- */
  S.sharePost = function (p) {
    var a = p.author || {}, text = (a.name || "HiBlue") + (a.handle ? " (@" + a.handle + ")" : "") + " pe HiBlue:\n" + (p.body ? p.body.slice(0, 300) : (p.poll ? p.poll.question : "o postare"));
    S.menu([
      { ic: "share", t: "Distribuie pe profilul tău", s: "Apare ca postare a ta, cu autorul original", fn: function () { if (S.composeShare) S.composeShare(p); } },
      S.shareToChat ? { ic: "chat", t: "Trimite unui prieten în chat", fn: function () { S.shareToChat(p); } } : null,
      { ic: "globe", t: "Distribuie în altă aplicație", fn: function () {
        var r = NativeBridge.call("device.share", { text: text });
        if (!r.ok) S.copy(text);
      } },
      { ic: "copy", t: "Copiază textul", fn: function () { S.copy(text); } }
    ], "Partajează");
  };

  /* ---------- lightbox ---------- */
  S.lightbox = function (items, start) {
    var w = document.createElement("div"); w.className = "lb";
    w.innerHTML = '<button class="ib lb-x" aria-label="Închide">' + I("close") + '</button><div class="lb-c">' + items.map(function (m) {
      return '<div class="lb-i">' + (m.type === "video" ? '<video src="' + e2(m.url) + '" controls autoplay playsinline></video>' : '<img src="' + e2(m.url) + '" alt="" draggable="false">') + '</div>';
    }).join("") + '</div>' + (items.length > 1 ? '<span class="lb-n">1/' + items.length + '</span>' : "");
    document.body.appendChild(w);
    var c = w.querySelector(".lb-c"), n = w.querySelector(".lb-n"), off;
    var close = function () { if (off) off(); w.remove(); };
    off = S.addBack(function () { if (!w.isConnected) return false; close(); return true; });
    w.querySelector(".lb-x").addEventListener("click", close);
    w.addEventListener("click", function (e) { if (e.target.classList.contains("lb-i") || e.target === c) close(); });
    c.addEventListener("scroll", function () { if (n) n.textContent = (Math.round(c.scrollLeft / Math.max(1, c.clientWidth)) + 1) + "/" + items.length; }, { passive: true });
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { c.scrollLeft = (start || 0) * c.clientWidth; w.classList.add("on"); });
  };

  S._t = Object.assign(S._t || {}, { ratio: ratio, pollHTML: pollHTML });
})();

/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Social · profil (update0.0.10)
   Profil propriu și al altora, prietenie (stare + acțiuni), mesaj (doar la alții), meniu ⋯ (blochează / raportează),
   editare profil, Salvate, Utilizatori blocați, Notificări (cereri de prietenie).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  var S = window.Social, e2 = esc;

  var COVERS = {
    c1: "linear-gradient(135deg,#2b1a7c,#6a5cff 55%,#b44cff)", c2: "linear-gradient(135deg,#0f2027,#2c5364)", c3: "linear-gradient(135deg,#ff5f6d,#ffc371)",
    c4: "linear-gradient(135deg,#11998e,#38ef7d)", c5: "linear-gradient(135deg,#232526,#414345)", c6: "linear-gradient(135deg,#00c6ff,#0072ff)"
  };
  var AVATARS = "😀 😎 🦊 🐼 🦁 🐸 🐙 🦄 🐺 🐯 🐻 🐨 🦉 🐧 🐲 👾 🤖 👻 🌟 🔥".split(" ");
  function coverStyle(c) {
    if (c && S.isUrl(c)) return 'background:#111 center/cover url(' + "'" + e2(c) + "'" + ')';
    return "background:" + (COVERS[c] || COVERS.c1);
  }

  /* ---------- relația cu un utilizator: bloc de acțiuni ---------- */
  function relBlock(p) {
    var r = p.relation, R = S.REL[r] || S.REL.none;
    var status = '<span class="rel ' + r + '">' + I(R.ic) + '<span>' + e2(R.t) + '</span></span>';
    var btns = "";
    if (r === "none") btns = '<button class="btn sm" data-k="add">' + I("userplus") + 'Adaugă</button>';
    else if (r === "pending_out") btns = '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-k="cancel">Anulează cererea</button>';
    else if (r === "pending_in") btns = '<button class="btn sm" data-k="accept">Acceptă</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-k="decline">Refuză</button>';
    else if (r === "friends") btns = '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-k="unfriend">Prieteni ✓</button>';
    else if (r === "blocked_by_me") btns = '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-k="unblock">Deblochează</button>';
    return '<div class="pf-rel">' + status + '</div><div class="pf-act">' + btns +
      (r !== "blocked_by_me" && r !== "blocked_me" ? '<button class="ib msg" data-k="msg" aria-label="Mesaj">' + I("chat") + '</button>' : "") + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- pagina de profil (propriu sau al altuia) ---------- */
  function renderProfile(el, uid) {
    var own = S.isMe(uid), data = null, list = null;
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="pf"><div class="pf-cv"></div><div class="pf-bd"><div class="sk big"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div></div></div>';
    var pf = el.firstChild, scroller = el.closest(".pb") || el;

    function paint() {
      var p = data, u = { id: p.id, name: p.name, handle: p.handle, avatar: p.avatar };
      var ring = p.story && p.story.count > 0 ? (own ? "ring mine" : (p.story.seen ? "ring seen" : "ring new")) : "";
      pf.innerHTML =
        '<div class="pf-cv" style="' + coverStyle(p.cover) + '">' + (own ? "" : '<button class="ib pf-dots" data-k="dots" aria-label="Mai multe">' + I("dots") + '</button>') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="pf-bd"><div class="pf-top"><span class="sav s84 ' + ring + '" data-prof="' + e2(p.id) + '"' + (ring ? ' data-story="1"' : "") + '><span class="sav-in">' + (S.isUrl(p.avatar) ? '<img src="' + e2(p.avatar) + '" alt="" draggable="false">' : '<b>' + e2(p.avatar && p.avatar.length <= 8 ? p.avatar : S.initial(u)) + '</b>') + '</span></span>' +
        (own ? '<div class="pf-act"><button class="btn sm ghost" data-k="edit">' + I("edit") + 'Editează</button><button class="ib" data-k="saved" aria-label="Salvate">' + I("bookmark") + '</button></div>' : relBlock(p)) + '</div>' +
        '<h2 class="pf-n">' + e2(p.name || "") + '</h2><div class="pf-h">@' + e2(p.handle || "") + '</div>' +
        (p.bio ? '<p class="pf-bio">' + S.rich(p.bio) + '</p>' : (own ? '<p class="pf-bio dim">Adaugă o descriere din „Editează”.</p>' : "")) +
        '<div class="pf-st"><div><b>' + S.count(p.posts) + '</b><small>Postări</small></div><button data-k="friends"><b>' + S.count(p.friends) + '</b><small>Prieteni</small></button>' + (own ? "" : '<div><b>' + S.count(p.mutual) + '</b><small>În comun</small></div>') + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="pf-ps"></div>';
      if (p.relation === "blocked_by_me") { pf.querySelector(".pf-ps").innerHTML = '<p class="note">Ai blocat acest utilizator. Nu vă vedeți conținutul.</p>'; return; }
      list = S.postList(pf.querySelector(".pf-ps"), {
        scroller: scroller,
        fetch: function (before) { return S.api("profile_posts", { p_user: uid, p_before: before, p_limit: 12 }); },
        empty: '<p class="note">' + (own ? "Nu ai postat încă. Apasă „+” din Feed." : (p.relation === "friends" ? "Nicio postare încă." : "Nicio postare publică.")) + '</p>'
      });
      list.refresh();
    }
    function load() {
      return S.api("profile_view", { p_user: uid }).then(function (r) {
        if (!r.ok || !r.data) { pf.innerHTML = '<div class="pf-bd"><p class="note">' + e2(r.ok ? "Profilul nu există." : r.err) + '</p></div>'; return; }
        if (r.data.relation === "blocked_me") { pf.innerHTML = '<div class="pf-bd"><p class="note">Acest profil nu este disponibil.</p></div>'; return; }
        data = r.data; paint();
      });
    }
    function setRel(rel) { if (!rel) return; data.relation = rel; if (rel === "friends" || rel === "none") { load(); } else paint(); }
    pf.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest("[data-k]"); if (!b || !data) return;
      var k = b.dataset.k, u = { id: data.id, name: data.name, handle: data.handle };
      if (k === "add") return S.friendAction(uid, "add").then(setRel);
      if (k === "cancel") return S.friendAction(uid, "cancel").then(setRel);
      if (k === "accept") return S.friendAction(uid, "accept").then(setRel);
      if (k === "decline") return S.friendAction(uid, "decline").then(setRel);
      if (k === "unfriend") return ask("Ștergi din prieteni?", "Nu veți mai vedea postările „doar pentru prieteni” una altuia.", "Șterge").then(function (ok) { if (ok) S.friendAction(uid, "remove").then(setRel); });
      if (k === "unblock") return S.unblockUser(u).then(function (rel) { if (rel) load(); });
      if (k === "msg") { closePages(); setTimeout(function () { S.openChat(uid); }, 60); return; }
      if (k === "friends") return S.showUsers("Prieteni", S.api("friends_of", { p_user: uid }));
      if (k === "edit") return page("editprofile");
      if (k === "saved") return page("saved");
      if (k === "dots") return S.menu([
        { ic: "flag", t: "Raportează profilul", danger: true, fn: function () { S.report("user", uid, u); } },
        data.relation === "blocked_by_me" ? { ic: "ban", t: "Deblochează", fn: function () { S.unblockUser(u).then(function (rel) { if (rel) load(); }); } }
          : { ic: "ban", t: "Blochează @" + (data.handle || ""), danger: true, fn: function () { S.blockUser(u).then(function (rel) { if (rel) { closePages(); } }); } }
      ], "@" + (data.handle || ""));
    });
    var offs = [S.on("relation", function (d) { if (!el.isConnected) return; if (d && d.id === uid && data) load(); }), S.on("story-added", function () { if (el.isConnected && data) load(); })];
    load();
    el._cleanup = function () { offs.forEach(function (f) { f(); }); if (list) list.destroy(); };
  }

  PAGES.profile = { t: function () { return "Profilul meu"; }, render: function (el) { renderProfile(el, S.me().id); } };
  PAGES.userprofile = { t: function () { return "Profil"; }, render: function (el, uid) { renderProfile(el, uid); } };

  /* ---------- editare profil ---------- */
  PAGES.editprofile = {
    t: function () { return "Editează profilul"; },
    render: function (el) {

          '<div class="ep-av"><span class="sav s84"><span class="sav-in">' + (S.isUrl(st.avatar) ? '<img src="' + e2(st.avatar) + '" alt="">' : '<b>' + e2(st.avatar || S.initial({ name: st.name })) + '</b>') + '</span></span><div><button class="btn sm ghost" data-k="photo">Schimbă poza</button><button class="lnk" data-k="emoji">sau alege un emoji</button></div></div>' +

        var k = b.dataset.k;
        if (k === "emoji") { var sh = S.sheet('<div class="emg">' + AVATARS.map(function (a) { return '<button data-e="' + a + '">' + a + '</button>'; }).join("") + '</div>', { title: "Alege avatarul" }); sh.body.addEventListener("click", function (ev) { var x = ev.target.closest("[data-e]"); if (!x) return; st.avatar = x.dataset.e; sh.close(); paint(); }); return; }
        if (k === "photo") return S.pick("image/*", false).then(function (fs) {
          if (!fs[0]) return; toast("Se încarcă poza…");
          S.compress(fs[0], 512, 0.85, true).then(function (c) { return S.upload(c.blob); }).then(function (u) { st.avatar = u.url; paint(); }).catch(function (er) { toast(er.message || "Nu s-a putut încărca"); });
        });
        if (k === "cover") {
          var rows = Object.keys(COVERS).map(function (c) { return '<button class="cvp" data-c="' + c + '" style="background:' + COVERS[c] + '"></button>'; }).join("");
          var sh2 = S.sheet('<div class="cvg">' + rows + '</div><button class="btn sm ghost" data-up="1">' + I("image") + 'Din galerie</button>', { title: "Copertă" });
          sh2.body.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
            var x = ev.target.closest("[data-c]"); if (x) { st.cover = x.dataset.c; sh2.close(); paint(); return; }
            if (ev.target.closest("[data-up]")) S.pick("image/*", false).then(function (fs) {
              if (!fs[0]) return; sh2.close(); toast("Se încarcă coperta…");
              S.compress(fs[0], 1400, 0.82).then(function (c) { return S.upload(c.blob); }).then(function (u) { st.cover = u.url; paint(); }).catch(function (er) { toast(er.message || "Nu s-a putut încărca"); });
            });
          });
          return;
        }
        if (k === "save") {
          if (!st.name.trim()) return toast("Numele nu poate fi gol");
          b.classList.add("busy");
          rest("/rest/v1/profiles?id=eq." + SB.ses.uid, { m: "PATCH", h: { Prefer: "return=representation" }, b: { username: st.name.trim(), bio: st.bio.trim(), avatar: st.avatar || null, cover: st.cover } }).then(function (r) {
            b.classList.remove("busy");
            if (!r.ok) return toast(r.s === 400 && /cover/i.test(JSON.stringify(r.j || "")) ? "Lipsește update0.0.10.sql în Supabase" : "Nu s-a putut salva");
            var row = Array.isArray(r.j) ? r.j[0] : r.j; if (row) { SB.profile = Object.assign(SB.profile || {}, row); try { LS.set("profile", SB.profile); } catch (er) { } }
            toast("Profil salvat"); S.emit("profile-updated"); closePages();
          });
        }
      });
    }
  };

    t: function () { return "Salvate"; },

      el.innerHTML = '<div class="sv-pg"></div>';

    }

    t: function () { return "Confidențialitate"; },
    render: function (el, arg) {
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="pg-pad"><div class="gt">Utilizatori blocați</div><div id="blk"><p class="note">Se încarcă…</p></div></div>';
      var box = el.querySelector("#blk");
      function load() {

          if (!r.ok) { box.innerHTML = '<p class="note">' + e2(r.err) + '</p>'; return; }
          var a = r.data || [];
          box.innerHTML = a.length ? a.map(function (u) { return '<div class="usr2" data-u="' + e2(u.id) + '">' + S.av(u, 40, { nav: false }) + '<span class="ui"><b>' + e2(u.name || "") + '</b><small>@' + e2(u.handle || "") + '</small></span><button class="btn sm ghost" data-un="' + e2(u.id) + '">Deblochează</button></div>'; }).join("") : '<p class="note">Nu ai blocat pe nimeni.</p>';
        });
      }
      box.addEventListener("click", function (e) { var b = e.target.closest("[data-un]"); if (!b) return; S.unblockUser({ id: b.dataset.un }).then(function (r) { if (r) load(); }); });
      load();
    }
  };

  PAGES.notifications = {
    t: function () { return "Notificări"; },
    render: function (el) {
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="pg-pad"><div class="gt">Cereri de prietenie</div><div id="rq"><p class="note">Se încarcă…</p></div></div>';
      var box = el.querySelector("#rq");
      function load() {
        S.api("friend_requests").then(function (r) {
          if (!r.ok) { box.innerHTML = '<p class="note">' + e2(r.err) + '</p>'; return; }
          var a = r.data || []; S.refreshBadges();
          box.innerHTML = a.length ? a.map(function (u) { return '<div class="usr2" data-prof="' + e2(u.id) + '">' + S.av(u, 44, { nav: false }) + '<span class="ui"><b>' + e2(u.name || "") + '</b><small>@' + e2(u.handle || "") + ' · ' + S.ago(u.at) + '</small></span><span class="rq-b"><button class="btn sm" data-ac="' + e2(u.id) + '" data-noprof="1">Acceptă</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-de="' + e2(u.id) + '" data-noprof="1">Refuză</button></span></div>'; }).join("") : '<p class="note">Nicio cerere nouă.</p>';
        });
      }
      box.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        var a = e.target.closest("[data-ac]"), d = e.target.closest("[data-de]");
        if (a) S.friendAction(a.dataset.ac, "accept").then(function (r) { if (r) { toast("Ați devenit prieteni"); load(); } });
        if (d) S.friendAction(d.dataset.de, "decline").then(function (r) { if (r) load(); });
      });
      load();
    }
  };
  S.openNotifications = function () { page("notifications"); };
})();

/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Social · căutare (update0.0.10)
   Pictograma lupă din antet deschide căutarea: oameni acum, grupuri în curând (secțiune rezervată).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  var S = window.Social, e2 = esc;

  S.openSearch = function () {
    if (document.querySelector(".sr")) return;
    var w = document.createElement("div"); w.className = "sr";
    w.innerHTML = '<div class="sr-top"><button class="ib" data-k="x" aria-label="Înapoi">' + I("back") + '</button><div class="sr-in">' + I("search") + '<input id="srQ" placeholder="Caută oameni sau grupuri" autocomplete="off" maxlength="40"><button class="ib sm" data-k="clr" hidden>' + I("close") + '</button></div></div>' +
      '<div class="sr-b"><div id="srR"></div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(w);
    var q = w.querySelector("#srQ"), R = w.querySelector("#srR"), clr = w.querySelector('[data-k="clr"]'), seq = 0;
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { w.classList.add("on"); q.focus(); });
    var off = S.addBack(function () { if (!w.isConnected) return false; close(); return true; });
    function close() { off(); w.classList.remove("on"); setTimeout(function () { w.remove(); }, 200); }
    function groupsCard() { return '<div class="sr-g"><div class="gt">Grupuri</div><div class="soon-c">' + I("people") + '<div><b>Grupurile vin în curând</b><small>Vei putea căuta și te alătura comunităților.</small></div></div></div>'; }
    function recents() {
      var a = LS.get("recentSearch") || [];
      R.innerHTML = (a.length ? '<div class="gt">Recente <button class="lnk" data-k="clrr">Șterge</button></div>' + a.map(function (t) { return '<button class="rc" data-q="' + e2(t) + '">' + I("clock") + '<span>' + e2(t) + '</span></button>'; }).join("") : '<p class="note">Caută după nume sau @utilizator.</p>') + groupsCard();
    }
    function remember(t) { var a = (LS.get("recentSearch") || []).filter(function (x) { return x !== t; }); a.unshift(t); LS.set("recentSearch", a.slice(0, 8)); }
    function row(u) {
      var r = S.REL[u.relation] || S.REL.none;
      return '<div class="usr2" data-go="' + e2(u.id) + '">' + S.av(u, 46, { nav: false }) + '<span class="ui"><b>' + e2(u.name || "") + '</b><small>@' + e2(u.handle || "") + '</small></span><span class="rel s ' + u.relation + '">' + I(r.ic) + (u.relation === "friends" || u.relation === "pending_out" ? '<span>' + e2(r.t) + '</span>' : "") + '</span>' +
        (u.relation === "none" ? '<button class="btn sm" data-add="' + e2(u.id) + '" data-noprof="1">Adaugă</button>' : "") + '</div>';
    }
    var run = S.debounce(function () {
      var t = q.value.trim(); clr.hidden = !t;
      if (t.length < 2) { recents(); return; }
      var my = ++seq; R.innerHTML = '<div class="sk"><i></i><i></i></div>';
      S.api("search_users", { q: t, lim: 25 }).then(function (r) {
        if (my !== seq) return;
        if (!r.ok) { R.innerHTML = '<p class="note">' + e2(r.err) + '</p>'; return; }
        var a = r.data || [];
        R.innerHTML = '<div class="gt">Oameni</div>' + (a.length ? a.map(row).join("") : '<p class="note">Niciun rezultat pentru „' + e2(t) + '”.</p>') + groupsCard();
      });
    }, 300);
    q.addEventListener("input", run);
    w.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest("[data-k],[data-q],[data-add],[data-go]"); if (!b) return;
      if (b.dataset.k === "x") return close();
      if (b.dataset.k === "clr") { q.value = ""; recents(); clr.hidden = true; q.focus(); return; }
      if (b.dataset.k === "clrr") { LS.set("recentSearch", null); recents(); return; }
      if (b.dataset.q) { q.value = b.dataset.q; run(); return; }
      if (b.dataset.add) { e.stopPropagation(); S.friendAction(b.dataset.add, "add").then(function (rel) { if (rel) { toast("Cerere trimisă"); run(); } }); return; }
      if (b.dataset.go) { remember(q.value.trim()); var id = b.dataset.go; close(); setTimeout(function () { S.openProfile(id); }, 120); }
    }, true);
    recents();
  };
})();

=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/social/stories.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Social · stories (update0.0.10)
   Bandă de stories (lângă butonul „+”) și vizualizator pe tot ecranul:
   bare de progres, atingere stânga/dreapta, ține apăsat = pauză, glisare jos = închide,
   glisare sus (la povestea ta) = cine a văzut. Textul/stickerele sunt în meta (randate live, nu „arse” în imagine).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  var S = window.Social, e2 = esc;

  S.STORY_BG = {
    g1: "linear-gradient(160deg,#6a5cff,#b44cff)", g2: "linear-gradient(160deg,#ff5f6d,#ffc371)", g3: "linear-gradient(160deg,#11998e,#38ef7d)",
    g4: "linear-gradient(160deg,#0f2027,#2c5364)", g5: "linear-gradient(160deg,#fc466b,#3f5efb)", g6: "linear-gradient(160deg,#f7971e,#ffd200)",
    g7: "linear-gradient(160deg,#232526,#414345)", g8: "linear-gradient(160deg,#00c6ff,#0072ff)"
  };
  S.STORY_FILTERS = {
    none: ["Original", ""], vivid: ["Vivid", "saturate(1.4) contrast(1.1)"], warm: ["Cald", "sepia(.25) saturate(1.2) hue-rotate(-10deg)"],
    cool: ["Rece", "saturate(1.1) hue-rotate(15deg) brightness(1.03)"], noir: ["Noir", "grayscale(1) contrast(1.15)"],
    fade: ["Fade", "contrast(.9) brightness(1.1) saturate(.85)"], dream: ["Vis", "brightness(1.08) saturate(1.3) contrast(.95)"]
  };

  /* conținutul unei povești (folosit și de compunere pentru previzualizare) */
  S.storyBody = function (s) {
    var m = s.meta || {}, f = (S.STORY_FILTERS[m.filter] || [0, ""])[1], inner = "";
    if (s.kind === "text") {
      var t = s.body || "", sz = t.length > 120 ? 6 : t.length > 50 ? 8 : 11;
      inner = '<div class="sc-t" style="background:' + (S.STORY_BG[s.bg] || S.STORY_BG.g1) + '"><span style="font-size:calc(var(--u,4px)*' + sz + ')">' + e2(t) + '</span></div>';
    } else if (s.kind === "video") {
      inner = '<video class="sc-m" src="' + e2(s.media_url) + '" playsinline autoplay ' + (f ? 'style="filter:' + f + '"' : "") + '></video>';
    } else {
      inner = '<img class="sc-m" src="' + e2(s.media_url) + '" alt="" draggable="false" ' + (f ? 'style="filter:' + f + '"' : "") + '>';
    }
    var items = (m.items || []).map(function (it) {
      var st = "left:" + (+it.x || 50) + "%;top:" + (+it.y || 50) + "%;font-size:calc(var(--u,4px)*" + (+it.s || 6) + ");" + (it.k === "t" ? "color:" + e2(/^#[0-9a-f]{3,8}$/i.test(it.c || "") ? it.c : "#fff") + ";" : "");
      return '<span class="sc-i ' + (it.k === "t" ? "tx" : "em") + '" style="' + st + '">' + e2(it.v) + '</span>';
    }).join("");
    return inner + items;
  };

  /* ---------- bandă ---------- */
  S.storyStrip = function (el) {
    var rail = [];
    function paint() {
      var mine = rail.filter(function (r) { return r.own; })[0], others = rail.filter(function (r) { return !r.own; });
      var me = S.me();
      var h = '<button class="st add" data-k="add" aria-label="Creează"><span class="sav s62"><span class="sav-in plus">' + I("plus") + '</span></span><small>Creează</small></button>';
      function item(r, label) {
        var u = r.user, ring = r.own ? "ring mine" : (r.seen ? "ring seen" : "ring new");
        return '<button class="st" data-k="open" data-u="' + e2(u.id) + '">' + '<span class="sav s62 ' + ring + '"><span class="sav-in">' + (S.isUrl(u.avatar) ? '<img src="' + e2(u.avatar) + '" alt="" draggable="false">' : '<b>' + e2(u.avatar && u.avatar.length <= 8 ? u.avatar : S.initial(u)) + '</b>') + '</span></span><small>' + e2(label || u.name || u.handle || "") + '</small></button>';
      }
      if (mine) h += item(mine, "Povestea ta");
      h += others.map(function (r) { return item(r); }).join("");
      el.innerHTML = h;
    }
    el.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest(".st"); if (!b) return;
      if (b.dataset.k === "add") return S.compose && S.compose.open();
      var order = rail.map(function (r) { return r.user.id; });
      S.storyOpen(b.dataset.u, order);
    });
    function refresh() {
      return S.api("story_rail").then(function (r) { if (r.ok) { rail = r.data || []; } paint(); return rail; });
    }
    paint();
    S.on("story-added", refresh); S.on("story-seen", refresh); S.on("relation", refresh);
    return { refresh: refresh, rail: function () { return rail; } };
  };

  /* ---------- vizualizator ---------- */
  S.storyOpen = function (userId, order) {
    if (document.querySelector(".sv")) return;
    order = order && order.length ? order.slice() : [userId];
    var ui = Math.max(0, order.indexOf(userId)), list = [], si = 0, t0 = 0, dur = 5000, el = 0, paused = false, raf = 0, closed = false, info = {}, holdT = 0, menuOpen = false;
    var w = document.createElement("div"); w.className = "sv";
    w.innerHTML = '<div class="sv-box"><div class="sv-st"></div></div><div class="sv-bars"></div><div class="sv-h"></div><div class="sv-f"></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(w);
    var box = w.querySelector(".sv-box"), st = w.querySelector(".sv-st"), bars = w.querySelector(".sv-bars"), head = w.querySelector(".sv-h"), foot = w.querySelector(".sv-f");
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { w.classList.add("on"); });
    var off = S.addBack(function () { if (!w.isConnected) return false; close(); return true; });
    function setU() { st.style.setProperty("--u", (st.clientWidth / 100) + "px"); }

    var offNav = S.on("navigate", function () { close(); });
    function close() { if (closed) return; closed = true; cancelAnimationFrame(raf); off(); offNav(); w.classList.remove("on"); setTimeout(function () { w.remove(); }, 220); S.emit("story-seen"); }
    function curUser() { return order[ui]; }

    function loadUser() {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf); list = []; si = 0;
      st.innerHTML = ""; bars.innerHTML = ""; head.innerHTML = ""; foot.innerHTML = "";
      Promise.all([S.api("stories_of", { p_user: curUser() }), S.api("profile_view", { p_user: curUser() })]).then(function (rr) {
        if (closed) return;
        var r = rr[0], p = rr[1];
        if (!r.ok || !(r.data || []).length) { return nextUser(); }
        list = r.data; info = p.ok && p.data ? p.data : {};
        var first = list.findIndex(function (s) { return !s.seen; });
        si = S.isMe(curUser()) || first < 0 ? 0 : first;
        show();
      });
    }
    function nextUser() { if (ui + 1 < order.length) { ui++; loadUser(); } else close(); }
    function prevUser() { if (ui > 0) { ui--; loadUser(); } else { si = 0; show(); } }
    function next() { if (si + 1 < list.length) { si++; show(); } else nextUser(); }
    function prev() { if (si > 0) { si--; show(); } else prevUser(); }

    function show() {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      var s = list[si]; if (!s) return;
      var own = S.isMe(curUser());
      bars.innerHTML = list.map(function (x, i) { return '<i class="' + (i < si ? "done" : "") + '"><b></b></i>'; }).join("");
      var u = { id: curUser(), name: info.name, handle: info.handle, avatar: info.avatar };
      head.innerHTML = S.av(u, 36, { nav: false }) + '<div class="sv-w"><b data-prof="' + e2(u.id) + '">' + e2(u.name || u.handle || "") + '</b><small>' + S.ago(s.created_at) + '</small></div><button class="ib sv-b" data-k="menu">' + I("dots") + '</button><button class="ib sv-b" data-k="x">' + I("close") + '</button>';
      st.innerHTML = S.storyBody(s); setU();
      foot.innerHTML = own
        ? '<button class="sv-v" data-k="views">' + I("eye") + '<span>' + (s.views || 0) + '</span><i>' + I("heart") + '</i><span>' + (s.likes || 0) + '</span></button>'
        : '<button class="sv-m" data-k="msg">' + I("chat") + '<span>Trimite un mesaj</span></button><button class="sv-l' + (s.liked ? " on" : "") + '" data-k="like">' + I("heart") + '</button>';
      if (!own && !s.seen) { s.seen = true; S.api("story_view", { p_id: s.id }); }
      var nx = list[si + 1]; if (nx && nx.kind === "image") { var im = new Image(); im.src = nx.media_url; }
      dur = 5000; el = 0; paused = false; t0 = performance.now();
      var v = st.querySelector("video");
      if (v) { v.muted = false; v.onloadedmetadata = function () { dur = Math.min(60000, Math.max(3000, (v.duration || 5) * 1000)); }; var pr = v.play(); if (pr && pr.catch) pr.catch(function () { v.muted = true; v.play(); }); }
      tick();
    }
    function tick() {
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
        if (closed) return;
        var now = performance.now();
        if (!paused) { el += now - t0; }
        t0 = now;
        var b = bars.children[si] && bars.children[si].firstChild; if (b) b.style.width = Math.min(100, el / dur * 100) + "%";
        if (el >= dur) return next();
        tick();
      });
    }
    function pause(on) { paused = on; var v = st.querySelector("video"); if (v) { if (on) v.pause(); else v.play(); } }

    var sx = 0, sy = 0, tt = 0, held = false;
    box.addEventListener("touchstart", function (e) { sx = e.touches[0].clientX; sy = e.touches[0].clientY; tt = Date.now(); held = false; clearTimeout(holdT); holdT = setTimeout(function () { held = true; pause(true); }, 220); }, { passive: true });
    box.addEventListener("touchend", function (e) {
      clearTimeout(holdT);
      var t = e.changedTouches[0], dx = t.clientX - sx, dy = t.clientY - sy;
      if (held) { pause(false); return; }
      if (dy > 90 && Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx)) return close();
      if (dy < -90 && Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx) && S.isMe(curUser())) return openViews();
      if (Math.abs(dx) < 14 && Math.abs(dy) < 14) { var r = box.getBoundingClientRect(); (t.clientX - r.left) < r.width * 0.33 ? prev() : next(); }
    }, { passive: true });
    box.addEventListener("click", function (e) { if (e.pointerType === "mouse") { var r = box.getBoundingClientRect(); (e.clientX - r.left) < r.width * 0.33 ? prev() : next(); } });

    function openViews() {
      var s = list[si]; if (!s) return; pause(true);
      var sh = S.sheet('<p class="note">Se încarcă…</p>', { title: "Vizualizări", cls: "tall", onClose: function () { pause(false); } });
      S.api("story_viewers", { p_id: s.id }).then(function (r) {
        var a = r.ok ? r.data : [];
        sh.body.innerHTML = a.length ? a.map(function (u) { return '<div class="usr2" data-prof="' + e2(u.id) + '">' + S.av(u, 40, { nav: false }) + '<span class="ui"><b>' + e2(u.name || "") + '</b><small>' + S.ago(u.at) + '</small></span>' + (u.liked ? '<span class="hh">' + I("heart") + '</span>' : "") + '</div>'; }).join("") : '<p class="note">Încă nicio vizualizare.</p>';
      });
    }
    w.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest("[data-k]"); if (!b) return;
      var k = b.dataset.k, s = list[si]; if (!s) return;
      if (k === "x") return close();
      if (k === "views") return openViews();
      if (k === "msg") { close(); setTimeout(function () { S.openChat(curUser()); }, 240); return; }
      if (k === "like") { var on = !s.liked; s.liked = on; b.classList.toggle("on", on); S.api("story_like", { p_id: s.id, p_on: on }); return; }
      if (k === "menu") {
        pause(true); var own = S.isMe(curUser());
        S.menu([
          own ? { ic: "eye", t: "Cine a văzut", fn: openViews } : null,
          own ? { ic: "trash", t: "Șterge povestea", danger: true, fn: function () {
            S.api("story_delete", { p_id: s.id }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); list.splice(si, 1); S.emit("story-added"); if (!list.length) close(); else { if (si >= list.length) si = list.length - 1; show(); } }); } } : null,
          !own ? { ic: "flag", t: "Raportează povestea", danger: true, fn: function () { S.report("story", s.id, { id: curUser(), name: info.name, handle: info.handle }); } } : null,
          !own ? { ic: "ban", t: "Blochează utilizatorul", danger: true, fn: function () { S.blockUser({ id: curUser(), name: info.name, handle: info.handle }).then(function (x) { if (x) close(); }); } } : null
        ], null, function () { if (!document.querySelector(".sh-wrap.on")) pause(false); });
      }
    });
    loadUser();
  };
})();
=== FILE: supabase/update0.0.10.sql ===
-- =====================================================================
-- HiBlue · update0.0.10 — Social: prietenii, block/raportare, postări, sondaje, comentarii,
--                         salvate, stories, căutare, stocare media.
-- Rulează O SINGURĂ DATĂ în Supabase → SQL Editor (după update0.0.4.sql). Idempotent.
--
-- Principii:
--  * Tabelele au RLS activ FĂRĂ politici: clientul NU poate citi/scrie direct, doar prin funcțiile RPC de mai jos
--    (security definer). Fiecare funcție verifică auth.uid(), block-urile și prietenia.
--  * Nu se folosește service_role în client. Nu se modifică tabelele existente, în afară de două coloane noi în profiles.
--  * Media (poze/video) stă în bucket-ul public 'media', doar în folderul <uid>/ al utilizatorului.
-- =====================================================================

-- ============ 0) profiles: copertă ============
alter table public.profiles add column if not exists cover text;
alter table public.profiles drop constraint if exists profiles_cover_len;
alter table public.profiles add  constraint profiles_cover_len check (char_length(coalesce(cover,'')) <= 500);

-- ============ 1) prietenii / block / raportări ============
create table if not exists public.friendships (
  user_a      uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  user_b      uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  requester   uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  status      text not null default 'pending' check (status in ('pending','accepted')),
  created_at  timestamptz not null default now(),
  accepted_at timestamptz,
  primary key (user_a, user_b),
  check (user_a < user_b),
  check (requester = user_a or requester = user_b)
);
create index if not exists friendships_b_idx on public.friendships (user_b);

create table if not exists public.blocks (
  blocker    uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  blocked    uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (blocker, blocked),
  check (blocker <> blocked)
);
create index if not exists blocks_blocked_idx on public.blocks (blocked);

create table if not exists public.reports (
  id          bigserial primary key,
  reporter    uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  target_type text not null check (target_type in ('user','post','comment','story','message')),
  target_id   text not null,
  target_user uuid,
  reason      text not null check (reason in ('spam','harassment','hate','nudity','violence','scam','impersonation','self_harm','other')),
  details     text check (char_length(coalesce(details,'')) <= 500),
  status      text not null default 'open',
  created_at  timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index if not exists reports_reporter_idx on public.reports (reporter, created_at desc);

-- ============ 2) postări, sondaje, aprecieri, comentarii, salvate ============
create table if not exists public.posts (
  id          uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  author      uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  body        text not null default '' check (char_length(body) <= 3000),
  media       jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb check (jsonb_typeof(media) = 'array' and jsonb_array_length(media) <= 10),
  audience    text not null default 'public' check (audience in ('public','friends')),
  shared_from uuid references public.posts(id) on delete set null,
  created_at  timestamptz not null default now(),
  edited_at   timestamptz
);
create index if not exists posts_author_idx  on public.posts (author, created_at desc);
create index if not exists posts_created_idx on public.posts (created_at desc, id desc);

create table if not exists public.polls (
  post_id      uuid primary key references public.posts(id) on delete cascade,
  question     text not null check (char_length(question) between 1 and 200),
  multiple     boolean not null default false,
  anonymous    boolean not null default false,
  quiz         boolean not null default false,
  explanation  text check (char_length(coalesce(explanation,'')) <= 300),
  allow_add    boolean not null default false,
  allow_change boolean not null default true,
  results      text not null default 'always' check (results in ('always','after_vote','after_close')),
  closes_at    timestamptz
);
create table if not exists public.poll_options (
  id       bigserial primary key,
  post_id  uuid not null references public.polls(post_id) on delete cascade,
  idx      int  not null,
  label    text not null check (char_length(label) between 1 and 100),
  correct  boolean not null default false,
  added_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  unique (post_id, idx)
);
create table if not exists public.poll_votes (
  option_id  bigint not null references public.poll_options(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id    uuid   not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  post_id    uuid   not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (option_id, user_id)
);
create index if not exists poll_votes_post_idx on public.poll_votes (post_id, user_id);

create table if not exists public.post_likes (
  post_id    uuid not null references public.posts(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id    uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (post_id, user_id)
);
create table if not exists public.comments (
  id         uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  post_id    uuid not null references public.posts(id) on delete cascade,
  author     uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  parent_id  uuid references public.comments(id) on delete cascade,
  body       text not null check (char_length(body) between 1 and 1000),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index if not exists comments_post_idx on public.comments (post_id, created_at);
create table if not exists public.comment_likes (
  comment_id uuid not null references public.comments(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id    uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  primary key (comment_id, user_id)
);
create table if not exists public.saves (
  user_id    uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  post_id    uuid not null references public.posts(id) on delete cascade,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (user_id, post_id)
);

-- ============ 3) stories ============
create table if not exists public.stories (
  id         uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  author     uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  kind       text not null check (kind in ('image','video','text')),
  media_url  text,
  body       text check (char_length(coalesce(body,'')) <= 300),
  bg         text check (char_length(coalesce(bg,'')) <= 40),
  meta       jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  expires_at timestamptz not null default (now() + interval '24 hours')
);
create index if not exists stories_author_idx on public.stories (author, created_at desc);
create index if not exists stories_exp_idx on public.stories (expires_at);
create table if not exists public.story_views (
  story_id  uuid not null references public.stories(id) on delete cascade,
  viewer    uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  viewed_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (story_id, viewer)
);
create table if not exists public.story_likes (
  story_id uuid not null references public.stories(id) on delete cascade,
  user_id  uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  primary key (story_id, user_id)
);

-- ============ 4) RLS: activ, fără politici (acces doar prin funcții) ============
alter table public.friendships    enable row level security;
alter table public.blocks         enable row level security;
alter table public.reports        enable row level security;
alter table public.posts          enable row level security;
alter table public.polls          enable row level security;
alter table public.poll_options   enable row level security;
alter table public.poll_votes     enable row level security;
alter table public.post_likes     enable row level security;
alter table public.comments       enable row level security;
alter table public.comment_likes  enable row level security;
alter table public.saves          enable row level security;
alter table public.stories        enable row level security;
alter table public.story_views    enable row level security;
alter table public.story_likes    enable row level security;

-- ============ 5) funcții ajutătoare (NU sunt expuse clientului) ============
create or replace function public.is_blocked_pair(a uuid, b uuid) returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select exists (select 1 from public.blocks where (blocker = a and blocked = b) or (blocker = b and blocked = a));
$$;

create or replace function public.are_friends(a uuid, b uuid) returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select exists (select 1 from public.friendships f
                 where f.user_a = least(a, b) and f.user_b = greatest(a, b) and f.status = 'accepted');
$$;

create or replace function public.can_view_post(p_author uuid, p_audience text) returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
  select auth.uid() is not null
     and not public.is_blocked_pair(auth.uid(), p_author)
     and (p_author = auth.uid() or p_audience = 'public' or public.are_friends(auth.uid(), p_author));
$$;

create or replace function public.require_user() returns uuid
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
begin
  if auth.uid() is null then raise exception 'forbidden' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return auth.uid();
end $$;

-- o postare -> JSON complet pentru client (autor, media, contoare, starea mea, sondaj)
create or replace function public.post_to_json(p public.posts) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare
  me uuid := auth.uid();
  a record; pl record; poll jsonb := null; sh jsonb := null; opts jsonb;
  voted boolean; show boolean; total int; closed boolean;
begin
  select id, username, handle, avatar into a from public.profiles where id = p.author;
  if p.shared_from is not null then
    select jsonb_build_object('id', q.id, 'body', q.body, 'media', q.media, 'created_at', q.created_at,
                              'author', jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar))
      into sh
      from public.posts q left join public.profiles pr on pr.id = q.author
     where q.id = p.shared_from and public.can_view_post(q.author, q.audience);
  end if;
  select * into pl from public.polls where post_id = p.id;
  if found then
    closed := pl.closes_at is not null and pl.closes_at <= now();
    voted  := exists (select 1 from public.poll_votes v where v.post_id = p.id and v.user_id = me);
    show   := pl.results = 'always' or (pl.results = 'after_vote' and (voted or p.author = me)) or (pl.results = 'after_close' and closed);
    select count(distinct user_id) into total from public.poll_votes where post_id = p.id;
    select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
             'id', o.id, 'label', o.label,
             'votes', case when show then (select count(*) from public.poll_votes v where v.option_id = o.id) else null end,
             'mine',  exists (select 1 from public.poll_votes v where v.option_id = o.id and v.user_id = me),
             'correct', case when pl.quiz and (voted or closed) then o.correct else null end) order by o.idx)
      into opts from public.poll_options o where o.post_id = p.id;
    poll := jsonb_build_object(
      'question', pl.question, 'multiple', pl.multiple, 'anonymous', pl.anonymous, 'quiz', pl.quiz,
      'allow_add', pl.allow_add, 'allow_change', pl.allow_change, 'results', pl.results,
      'closes_at', pl.closes_at, 'closed', closed, 'voted', voted, 'show', show,
      'total', case when show then total else null end,
      'options', coalesce(opts, '[]'::jsonb),
      'explanation', case when pl.quiz and (voted or closed) then pl.explanation else null end);
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'id', p.id,
    'author', jsonb_build_object('id', a.id, 'name', a.username, 'handle', a.handle, 'avatar', a.avatar),
    'body', p.body, 'media', p.media, 'audience', p.audience,
    'created_at', p.created_at, 'edited_at', p.edited_at, 'shared', sh, 'poll', poll,
    'likes',    (select count(*) from public.post_likes where post_id = p.id),
    'comments', (select count(*) from public.comments  where post_id = p.id),
    'liked', exists (select 1 from public.post_likes where post_id = p.id and user_id = me),
    'saved', exists (select 1 from public.saves      where post_id = p.id and user_id = me),
    'mine', p.author = me);
end $$;

-- ============ 6) prietenii ============
create or replace function public.relation_of(t uuid) returns text
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); f record;
begin
  if t = me then return 'self'; end if;
  if exists (select 1 from public.blocks where blocker = me and blocked = t) then return 'blocked_by_me'; end if;
  if exists (select 1 from public.blocks where blocker = t and blocked = me) then return 'blocked_me'; end if;
  select status, requester into f from public.friendships where user_a = least(me, t) and user_b = greatest(me, t);
  if not found then return 'none'; end if;
  if f.status = 'accepted' then return 'friends'; end if;
  if f.requester = me then return 'pending_out'; end if;
  return 'pending_in';
end $$;

create or replace function public.friend_request(target uuid) returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); rel text;
begin
  if target is null or target = me then raise exception 'invalid' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.profiles where id = target) then raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  rel := public.relation_of(target);
  if rel in ('blocked_by_me', 'blocked_me') then raise exception 'forbidden' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if rel = 'pending_in' then
    update public.friendships set status = 'accepted', accepted_at = now()
     where user_a = least(me, target) and user_b = greatest(me, target);
  elsif rel = 'none' then
    insert into public.friendships (user_a, user_b, requester) values (least(me, target), greatest(me, target), me);
  end if;
  return public.relation_of(target);
end $$;

create or replace function public.friend_respond(target uuid, accept boolean) returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user();
begin
  if accept then
    update public.friendships set status = 'accepted', accepted_at = now()
     where user_a = least(me, target) and user_b = greatest(me, target) and status = 'pending' and requester = target;
  else
    delete from public.friendships
     where user_a = least(me, target) and user_b = greatest(me, target) and status = 'pending' and requester = target;
  end if;
  return public.relation_of(target);
end $$;

-- anulează cererea trimisă / șterge prietenul
create or replace function public.friend_remove(target uuid) returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user();
begin
  delete from public.friendships where user_a = least(me, target) and user_b = greatest(me, target);
  return public.relation_of(target);
end $$;

create or replace function public.friend_requests() returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); out jsonb;
begin
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar, 'at', f.created_at)
                            order by f.created_at desc), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out
    from public.friendships f join public.profiles pr on pr.id = f.requester
   where f.status = 'pending' and f.requester <> me and (f.user_a = me or f.user_b = me)
     and not public.is_blocked_pair(me, f.requester);
  return out;
end $$;

create or replace function public.friends_of(p_user uuid, p_limit int default 100) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); out jsonb;
begin
  if public.is_blocked_pair(me, p_user) then return '[]'::jsonb; end if;
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar)), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out
    from (select case when f.user_a = p_user then f.user_b else f.user_a end as fid
            from public.friendships f
           where f.status = 'accepted' and (f.user_a = p_user or f.user_b = p_user)
           limit least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 100), 1), 200)) x
    join public.profiles pr on pr.id = x.fid
   where not public.is_blocked_pair(me, pr.id);
  return out;
end $$;

-- ============ 7) block / raportare ============
create or replace function public.block_user(target uuid) returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user();
begin
  if target is null or target = me then raise exception 'invalid' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  insert into public.blocks (blocker, blocked) values (me, target) on conflict do nothing;
  delete from public.friendships where user_a = least(me, target) and user_b = greatest(me, target);
  return public.relation_of(target);
end $$;

create or replace function public.unblock_user(target uuid) returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user();
begin
  delete from public.blocks where blocker = me and blocked = target;
  return public.relation_of(target);
end $$;

create or replace function public.my_blocks() returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); out jsonb;
begin
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar) order by b.created_at desc), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out from public.blocks b join public.profiles pr on pr.id = b.blocked where b.blocker = me;
  return out;
end $$;

create or replace function public.report_submit(p_type text, p_id text, p_reason text, p_details text default null) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); tu uuid := null;
begin
  if p_type not in ('user','post','comment','story','message') then raise exception 'invalid type' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_reason not in ('spam','harassment','hate','nudity','violence','scam','impersonation','self_harm','other') then raise exception 'invalid reason' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_id is null or char_length(p_id) = 0 or char_length(p_id) > 64 then raise exception 'invalid target' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if (select count(*) from public.reports where reporter = me and created_at > now() - interval '1 day') >= 30 then
    raise exception 'too many reports' using errcode = '54000';
  end if;
  begin
    if p_type = 'user' then tu := p_id::uuid;
    elsif p_type = 'post' then select author into tu from public.posts where id = p_id::uuid;
    elsif p_type = 'comment' then select author into tu from public.comments where id = p_id::uuid;
    elsif p_type = 'story' then select author into tu from public.stories where id = p_id::uuid;
    end if;
  exception when invalid_text_representation then
    raise exception 'invalid target' using errcode = '22023';
  end;
  insert into public.reports (reporter, target_type, target_id, target_user, reason, details)
  values (me, p_type, p_id, tu, p_reason, left(p_details, 500));
  return true;
end $$;

-- ============ 8) profil ============
create or replace function public.profile_view(p_user uuid) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); pr record; rel text; st jsonb;
begin
  select id, username, handle, bio, avatar, cover, created_at into pr from public.profiles where id = p_user;
  if not found then return null; end if;
  rel := public.relation_of(p_user);
  if rel = 'blocked_me' then return jsonb_build_object('id', p_user, 'relation', rel); end if;
  select jsonb_build_object('count', count(*),
           'seen', coalesce(bool_and(exists (select 1 from public.story_views v where v.story_id = s.id and v.viewer = me)), true))
    into st from public.stories s
   where s.author = p_user and s.expires_at > now() and (p_user = me or public.are_friends(me, p_user));
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'bio', pr.bio, 'avatar', pr.avatar, 'cover', pr.cover,
    'joined', pr.created_at, 'relation', rel,
    'posts', (select count(*) from public.posts p where p.author = p_user and public.can_view_post(p.author, p.audience)),
    'friends', (select count(*) from public.friendships f where f.status = 'accepted' and (f.user_a = p_user or f.user_b = p_user)),
    'mutual', case when p_user = me then 0 else (
        select count(*) from public.friendships f1 join public.friendships f2
          on (case when f1.user_a = me then f1.user_b else f1.user_a end) = (case when f2.user_a = p_user then f2.user_b else f2.user_a end)
         where f1.status = 'accepted' and f2.status = 'accepted'
           and (f1.user_a = me or f1.user_b = me) and (f2.user_a = p_user or f2.user_b = p_user)) end,
    'story', st);
end $$;

create or replace function public.profile_posts(p_user uuid, p_before timestamptz default null, p_limit int default 15) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); out jsonb;
begin
  p_limit := least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 15), 1), 30);
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(public.post_to_json(x.r) order by x.ca desc, x.pid desc), '[]'::jsonb) into out from (
    select p as r, p.created_at as ca, p.id as pid from public.posts p
     where p.author = p_user and (p_before is null or p.created_at < p_before) and public.can_view_post(p.author, p.audience)
     order by p.created_at desc, p.id desc limit p_limit) x;
  return out;
end $$;

-- ============ 9) feed ============
create or replace function public.feed_page(p_scope text default 'all', p_before timestamptz default null, p_limit int default 15) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); out jsonb;
begin
  p_limit := least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 15), 1), 30);
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(public.post_to_json(x.r) order by x.ca desc, x.pid desc), '[]'::jsonb) into out from (
