    select p as r, p.created_at as ca, p.id as pid from public.posts p
     where (p_before is null or p.created_at < p_before)
       and not public.is_blocked_pair(me, p.author)
       and ( p.author = me
             or public.are_friends(me, p.author)
             or (coalesce(p_scope, 'all') = 'all' and p.audience = 'public') )
     order by p.created_at desc, p.id desc limit p_limit) x;
  return out;
end $$;

create or replace function public.saved_posts(p_before timestamptz default null, p_limit int default 20) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); out jsonb;
begin
  p_limit := least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 20), 1), 40);
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg((public.post_to_json(x.r) || jsonb_build_object('saved_at', x.sa)) order by x.sa desc), '[]'::jsonb) into out from (
    select p as r, s.created_at as sa from public.saves s join public.posts p on p.id = s.post_id
     where s.user_id = me and (p_before is null or s.created_at < p_before) and public.can_view_post(p.author, p.audience)
     order by s.created_at desc limit p_limit) x;
  return out;
end $$;

-- ============ 10) postări: creare / ștergere / like / save / comentarii ============
create or replace function public.post_add(p_body text, p_media jsonb default '[]'::jsonb, p_audience text default 'public',
                                           p_shared_from uuid default null, p_poll jsonb default null) returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare
  me uuid := public.require_user(); pid uuid; m jsonb; o jsonb; i int := 0; ncorrect int := 0; nopt int;
  q_multiple boolean; q_quiz boolean; mins int; src public.posts;
begin
  p_body := coalesce(p_body, ''); p_media := coalesce(p_media, '[]'::jsonb);
  if p_audience not in ('public', 'friends') then raise exception 'invalid audience' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if jsonb_typeof(p_media) <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(p_media) > 10 then raise exception 'invalid media' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  for m in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_media) loop
    if coalesce(m->>'type', '') not in ('image', 'video') or position('/media/' || me::text || '/' in coalesce(m->>'url', '')) = 0 then
      raise exception 'invalid media item' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
  end loop;
  if p_shared_from is not null then
    select * into src from public.posts where id = p_shared_from;
    if not found or not public.can_view_post(src.author, src.audience) then raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
    if src.shared_from is not null then p_shared_from := src.shared_from; end if;
  end if;
  if char_length(btrim(p_body)) = 0 and jsonb_array_length(p_media) = 0 and p_poll is null and p_shared_from is null then
    raise exception 'empty post' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if (select count(*) from public.posts where author = me and created_at > now() - interval '1 hour') >= 30 then
    raise exception 'too many posts' using errcode = '54000';
  end if;

  insert into public.posts (author, body, media, audience, shared_from)
  values (me, p_body, p_media, p_audience, p_shared_from) returning id into pid;

  if p_poll is not null then
    nopt := coalesce(jsonb_array_length(p_poll->'options'), 0);
    if nopt < 2 or nopt > 10 then raise exception 'poll needs 2-10 options' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    q_multiple := coalesce((p_poll->>'multiple')::boolean, false);
    q_quiz := coalesce((p_poll->>'quiz')::boolean, false);
    mins := nullif(p_poll->>'closes_in_minutes', '')::int;
    if mins is not null and (mins < 1 or mins > 60 * 24 * 30) then raise exception 'invalid deadline' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    insert into public.polls (post_id, question, multiple, anonymous, quiz, explanation, allow_add, allow_change, results, closes_at)
    values (pid, left(btrim(coalesce(p_poll->>'question', '')), 200), q_multiple,
            coalesce((p_poll->>'anonymous')::boolean, false), q_quiz, left(p_poll->>'explanation', 300),
            coalesce((p_poll->>'allow_add')::boolean, false),
            case when q_quiz then false else coalesce((p_poll->>'allow_change')::boolean, true) end,
            coalesce(nullif(p_poll->>'results', ''), 'always'),
            case when mins is null then null else now() + make_interval(mins => mins) end);
    for o in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_poll->'options') loop
      insert into public.poll_options (post_id, idx, label, correct, added_by)
      values (pid, i, left(btrim(coalesce(o->>'label', '')), 100), coalesce((o->>'correct')::boolean, false), me);
      if coalesce((o->>'correct')::boolean, false) then ncorrect := ncorrect + 1; end if;
      i := i + 1;
    end loop;
    if q_quiz and (ncorrect < 1 or (not q_multiple and ncorrect <> 1)) then raise exception 'quiz needs correct answer' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  end if;
  select * into src from public.posts where id = pid;
  return public.post_to_json(src);
end $$;

create or replace function public.post_delete(p_id uuid) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user();
begin
  delete from public.posts where id = p_id and author = me;
  return found;
end $$;

create or replace function public.post_edit(p_id uuid, p_body text) returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); r public.posts;
begin
  if char_length(coalesce(p_body, '')) > 3000 then raise exception 'too long' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  update public.posts set body = coalesce(p_body, ''), edited_at = now() where id = p_id and author = me returning * into r;
  if not found then raise exception 'forbidden' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return public.post_to_json(r);
end $$;

create or replace function public.post_like(p_id uuid, p_on boolean) returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); po public.posts;
begin
  select * into po from public.posts where id = p_id;
  if not found or not public.can_view_post(po.author, po.audience) then raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  if p_on then insert into public.post_likes (post_id, user_id) values (p_id, me) on conflict do nothing;
  else delete from public.post_likes where post_id = p_id and user_id = me; end if;
  return jsonb_build_object('liked', p_on, 'likes', (select count(*) from public.post_likes where post_id = p_id));
end $$;

create or replace function public.post_save(p_id uuid, p_on boolean) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); po public.posts;
begin
  select * into po from public.posts where id = p_id;
  if not found or not public.can_view_post(po.author, po.audience) then raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  if p_on then insert into public.saves (user_id, post_id) values (me, p_id) on conflict do nothing;
  else delete from public.saves where user_id = me and post_id = p_id; end if;
  return p_on;
end $$;

create or replace function public.post_likers(p_id uuid) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); po public.posts; out jsonb;
begin
  select * into po from public.posts where id = p_id;
  if not found or not public.can_view_post(po.author, po.audience) then return '[]'::jsonb; end if;
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar)), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out from (select user_id from public.post_likes where post_id = p_id order by created_at desc limit 100) l
    join public.profiles pr on pr.id = l.user_id where not public.is_blocked_pair(me, pr.id);
  return out;
end $$;

create or replace function public.comments_list(p_post uuid) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); po public.posts; out jsonb;
begin
  select * into po from public.posts where id = p_post;
  if not found or not public.can_view_post(po.author, po.audience) then return '[]'::jsonb; end if;
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
      'id', c.id, 'parent', c.parent_id, 'body', c.body, 'created_at', c.created_at,
      'author', jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar),
      'likes', (select count(*) from public.comment_likes cl where cl.comment_id = c.id),
      'liked', exists (select 1 from public.comment_likes cl where cl.comment_id = c.id and cl.user_id = me),
      'mine', c.author = me, 'can_delete', (c.author = me or po.author = me)) order by c.created_at), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out
    from public.comments c join public.profiles pr on pr.id = c.author
   where c.post_id = p_post and not public.is_blocked_pair(me, c.author);
  return out;
end $$;

create or replace function public.comment_add(p_post uuid, p_body text, p_parent uuid default null) returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); po public.posts; cid uuid; par public.comments; pr record;
begin
  select * into po from public.posts where id = p_post;
  if not found or not public.can_view_post(po.author, po.audience) then raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  p_body := btrim(coalesce(p_body, ''));
  if char_length(p_body) < 1 or char_length(p_body) > 1000 then raise exception 'invalid comment' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_parent is not null then
    select * into par from public.comments where id = p_parent and post_id = p_post;
    if not found then raise exception 'invalid parent' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    if par.parent_id is not null then p_parent := par.parent_id; end if;   -- un singur nivel de răspunsuri
  end if;
  if (select count(*) from public.comments where author = me and created_at > now() - interval '1 minute') >= 20 then
    raise exception 'too many comments' using errcode = '54000';
  end if;
  insert into public.comments (post_id, author, parent_id, body) values (p_post, me, p_parent, p_body) returning id into cid;
  select id, username, handle, avatar into pr from public.profiles where id = me;
  return jsonb_build_object('id', cid, 'parent', p_parent, 'body', p_body, 'created_at', now(),
    'author', jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar),
    'likes', 0, 'liked', false, 'mine', true, 'can_delete', true);
end $$;

create or replace function public.comment_delete(p_id uuid) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user();
begin
  delete from public.comments c
   where c.id = p_id and (c.author = me or exists (select 1 from public.posts p where p.id = c.post_id and p.author = me));
  return found;
end $$;

create or replace function public.comment_like(p_id uuid, p_on boolean) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); c public.comments; po public.posts;
begin
  select * into c from public.comments where id = p_id;
  if not found then raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  select * into po from public.posts where id = c.post_id;
  if not public.can_view_post(po.author, po.audience) then raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  if p_on then insert into public.comment_likes (comment_id, user_id) values (p_id, me) on conflict do nothing;
  else delete from public.comment_likes where comment_id = p_id and user_id = me; end if;
  return p_on;
end $$;

-- ============ 11) sondaje ============
create or replace function public.poll_vote(p_post uuid, p_options bigint[]) returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); pl public.polls; po public.posts; n int; had boolean;
begin
  select * into po from public.posts where id = p_post;
  if not found or not public.can_view_post(po.author, po.audience) then raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  select * into pl from public.polls where post_id = p_post;
  if not found then raise exception 'not a poll' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if pl.closes_at is not null and pl.closes_at <= now() then raise exception 'poll closed' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  p_options := array(select distinct x from unnest(coalesce(p_options, '{}'::bigint[])) x);
  select count(*) into n from public.poll_options where post_id = p_post and id = any(p_options);
  if n <> coalesce(array_length(p_options, 1), 0) then raise exception 'invalid option' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if not pl.multiple and n > 1 then raise exception 'single choice' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  had := exists (select 1 from public.poll_votes where post_id = p_post and user_id = me);
  if had and not pl.allow_change then raise exception 'vote locked' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  delete from public.poll_votes where post_id = p_post and user_id = me;
  if n > 0 then
    insert into public.poll_votes (option_id, user_id, post_id)
    select id, me, p_post from public.poll_options where post_id = p_post and id = any(p_options);
  end if;
  return public.post_to_json(po)->'poll';
end $$;

create or replace function public.poll_add_option(p_post uuid, p_label text) returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); pl public.polls; po public.posts; nx int;
begin
  select * into po from public.posts where id = p_post;
  if not found or not public.can_view_post(po.author, po.audience) then raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  select * into pl from public.polls where post_id = p_post;
  if not found or not pl.allow_add then raise exception 'forbidden' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if pl.closes_at is not null and pl.closes_at <= now() then raise exception 'poll closed' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  p_label := btrim(coalesce(p_label, ''));
  if char_length(p_label) < 1 or char_length(p_label) > 100 then raise exception 'invalid label' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  select coalesce(max(idx), -1) + 1 into nx from public.poll_options where post_id = p_post;
  if nx >= 10 then raise exception 'too many options' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  insert into public.poll_options (post_id, idx, label, added_by) values (p_post, nx, p_label, me);
  return public.post_to_json(po)->'poll';
end $$;

create or replace function public.poll_voters(p_post uuid, p_option bigint) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); pl public.polls; po public.posts; out jsonb; voted boolean; closed boolean;
begin
  select * into po from public.posts where id = p_post;
  if not found or not public.can_view_post(po.author, po.audience) then return '[]'::jsonb; end if;
  select * into pl from public.polls where post_id = p_post;
  if not found or pl.anonymous then return '[]'::jsonb; end if;
  closed := pl.closes_at is not null and pl.closes_at <= now();
  voted := exists (select 1 from public.poll_votes where post_id = p_post and user_id = me);
  if not (pl.results = 'always' or (pl.results = 'after_vote' and (voted or po.author = me)) or (pl.results = 'after_close' and closed)) then
    return '[]'::jsonb;
  end if;
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar)), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out from (select user_id from public.poll_votes where option_id = p_option and post_id = p_post order by created_at limit 100) v
    join public.profiles pr on pr.id = v.user_id where not public.is_blocked_pair(me, pr.id);
  return out;
end $$;

-- ============ 12) stories ============
create or replace function public.story_add(p_kind text, p_media text, p_body text, p_bg text, p_meta jsonb default '{}'::jsonb) returns jsonb
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); sid uuid;
begin
  if p_kind not in ('image', 'video', 'text') then raise exception 'invalid kind' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_kind in ('image', 'video') and position('/media/' || me::text || '/' in coalesce(p_media, '')) = 0 then
    raise exception 'invalid media' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_kind = 'text' and char_length(btrim(coalesce(p_body, ''))) = 0 then raise exception 'empty story' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if (select count(*) from public.stories where author = me and created_at > now() - interval '1 day') >= 50 then
    raise exception 'too many stories' using errcode = '54000';
  end if;
  insert into public.stories (author, kind, media_url, body, bg, meta)
  values (me, p_kind, case when p_kind = 'text' then null else p_media end, left(p_body, 300), left(p_bg, 40), coalesce(p_meta, '{}'::jsonb))
  returning id into sid;
  return jsonb_build_object('id', sid);
end $$;

create or replace function public.story_delete(p_id uuid) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user();
begin
  delete from public.stories where id = p_id and author = me;
  return found;
end $$;

create or replace function public.story_rail() returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); out jsonb;
begin
  with s as (
    select st.* from public.stories st
     where st.expires_at > now()
       and (st.author = me or (public.are_friends(me, st.author) and not public.is_blocked_pair(me, st.author)))
  ), g as (
    select s.author, count(*) as cnt, max(s.created_at) as latest,
           bool_and(exists (select 1 from public.story_views v where v.story_id = s.id and v.viewer = me)) as seen
      from s group by s.author
  )
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'user', jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar),
           'own', g.author = me, 'seen', g.seen, 'count', g.cnt, 'latest', g.latest)
           order by (g.author = me) desc, g.seen asc, g.latest desc), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out from g join public.profiles pr on pr.id = g.author;
  return out;
end $$;

create or replace function public.stories_of(p_user uuid) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); out jsonb;
begin
  if p_user <> me and (public.is_blocked_pair(me, p_user) or not public.are_friends(me, p_user)) then return '[]'::jsonb; end if;
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
      'id', s.id, 'kind', s.kind, 'media_url', s.media_url, 'body', s.body, 'bg', s.bg, 'meta', s.meta,
      'created_at', s.created_at, 'expires_at', s.expires_at,
      'seen', exists (select 1 from public.story_views v where v.story_id = s.id and v.viewer = me),
      'liked', exists (select 1 from public.story_likes l where l.story_id = s.id and l.user_id = me),
      'views', case when s.author = me then (select count(*) from public.story_views v where v.story_id = s.id) else null end,
      'likes', case when s.author = me then (select count(*) from public.story_likes l where l.story_id = s.id) else null end)
      order by s.created_at), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out from public.stories s where s.author = p_user and s.expires_at > now();
  return out;
end $$;

create or replace function public.story_view(p_id uuid) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); s public.stories;
begin
  select * into s from public.stories where id = p_id and expires_at > now();
  if not found or s.author = me then return false; end if;
  if public.is_blocked_pair(me, s.author) or not public.are_friends(me, s.author) then return false; end if;
  insert into public.story_views (story_id, viewer) values (p_id, me) on conflict do nothing;
  return true;
end $$;

create or replace function public.story_viewers(p_id uuid) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); out jsonb;
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from public.stories where id = p_id and author = me) then return '[]'::jsonb; end if;
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', pr.id, 'name', pr.username, 'handle', pr.handle, 'avatar', pr.avatar,
           'at', v.viewed_at, 'liked', exists (select 1 from public.story_likes l where l.story_id = p_id and l.user_id = v.viewer))
           order by v.viewed_at desc), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out from public.story_views v join public.profiles pr on pr.id = v.viewer where v.story_id = p_id;
  return out;
end $$;

create or replace function public.story_like(p_id uuid, p_on boolean) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); s public.stories;
begin
  select * into s from public.stories where id = p_id and expires_at > now();
  if not found or public.is_blocked_pair(me, s.author) or (s.author <> me and not public.are_friends(me, s.author)) then
    raise exception 'not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  if p_on then insert into public.story_likes (story_id, user_id) values (p_id, me) on conflict do nothing;
  else delete from public.story_likes where story_id = p_id and user_id = me; end if;
  return p_on;
end $$;

-- ============ 13) căutare ============
create or replace function public.search_users(q text, lim int default 20) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = public as $$
declare me uuid := public.require_user(); pat text; out jsonb;
begin
  q := btrim(coalesce(q, ''));
  if char_length(q) < 2 then return '[]'::jsonb; end if;
  q := left(q, 40);
  pat := '%' || replace(replace(replace(lower(ltrim(q, '@')), '\', '\\'), '%', '\%'), '_', '\_') || '%';
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', x.id, 'name', x.username, 'handle', x.handle, 'avatar', x.avatar,
           'relation', public.relation_of(x.id)) order by x.rank, x.username), '[]'::jsonb)
    into out from (
      select pr.id, pr.username, pr.handle, pr.avatar,
             case when lower(pr.handle) = lower(ltrim(q, '@')) then 0 when lower(pr.handle) like pat then 1 else 2 end as rank
        from public.profiles pr
       where pr.id <> me and (lower(pr.handle) like pat or lower(pr.username) like pat)
         and not public.is_blocked_pair(me, pr.id)
       order by rank, pr.username limit least(greatest(coalesce(lim, 20), 1), 40)) x;
  return out;
end $$;

-- ============ 14) stocare media (bucket public, doar în folderul propriu) ============
insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit, allowed_mime_types)
values ('media', 'media', true, 52428800,
        array['image/jpeg','image/png','image/webp','image/gif','video/mp4','video/webm','video/quicktime'])
on conflict (id) do update set public = excluded.public, file_size_limit = excluded.file_size_limit,
                               allowed_mime_types = excluded.allowed_mime_types;

drop policy if exists media_insert_own on storage.objects;
create policy media_insert_own on storage.objects for insert to authenticated
  with check (bucket_id = 'media' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
drop policy if exists media_delete_own on storage.objects;
create policy media_delete_own on storage.objects for delete to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'media' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);

-- ============ 15) permisiuni: doar utilizatorii autentificați pot apela funcțiile publice; ajutătoarele rămân interne ============
do $$
declare r record;
begin
  for r in
    select p.oid::regprocedure as sig, p.proname as name
      from pg_proc p join pg_namespace n on n.oid = p.pronamespace
     where n.nspname = 'public'
       and p.proname = any (array[
         'relation_of','friend_request','friend_respond','friend_remove','friend_requests','friends_of',
         'block_user','unblock_user','my_blocks','report_submit','profile_view','profile_posts','feed_page','saved_posts',
         'post_add','post_delete','post_edit','post_like','post_save','post_likers','comments_list','comment_add','comment_delete','comment_like',
         'poll_vote','poll_add_option','poll_voters','story_add','story_delete','story_rail','stories_of','story_view','story_viewers','story_like','search_users'])
  loop
    execute format('revoke all on function %s from public, anon', r.sig);
    execute format('grant execute on function %s to authenticated', r.sig);
  end loop;
  for r in
    select p.oid::regprocedure as sig
      from pg_proc p join pg_namespace n on n.oid = p.pronamespace
     where n.nspname = 'public'
       and p.proname = any (array['is_blocked_pair','are_friends','can_view_post','require_user','post_to_json'])
  loop
    execute format('revoke all on function %s from public, anon, authenticated', r.sig);
  end loop;
end $$;
=== FILE: app/src/main/assets/social/chat.js ===
/* =====================================================================
   HiBlue · Social · Chat (update0.0.11)
   Lista de conversații (fixate, necitite, mute, arhivă) + conversație tip Messenger: bule grupate, separatoare de zi,
   răspuns (glisare dreapta), reacții, fixare mesaje, editare, ștergere (pentru mine / pentru toți), poze/video,
   postări distribuite, „Văzut”, căutare în conversație, redirecționare, bulă în afara aplicației (Android).
   Livrare mesaje: verificare periodică (nu push). Mesajele NU sunt criptate end-to-end.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  var S = window.Social, e2 = esc;
  var QUICK = ["❤️", "😂", "😮", "😢", "👍", "🔥"];

  /* ---------- funcții pure (testate) ---------- */
  var pad = function (n) { return (n < 10 ? "0" : "") + n; };
  S.clock = function (iso) { var d = new Date(iso); return isFinite(d) ? pad(d.getHours()) + ":" + pad(d.getMinutes()) : ""; };
  S.dayLabel = function (iso, now) {
    var d = new Date(iso), n = new Date(now || Date.now());
    if (!isFinite(d)) return "";
    var sd = function (x) { return new Date(x.getFullYear(), x.getMonth(), x.getDate()).getTime(); };
    var diff = Math.round((sd(n) - sd(d)) / 86400000);
    if (diff === 0) return "Azi"; if (diff === 1) return "Ieri";
    var M = ["ian", "feb", "mar", "apr", "mai", "iun", "iul", "aug", "sep", "oct", "nov", "dec"];
    return d.getDate() + " " + M[d.getMonth()] + (d.getFullYear() !== n.getFullYear() ? " " + d.getFullYear() : "");
  };
  /* mesaje (crescător) -> elemente cu separatoare de zi și marcaje de grup (first/last) */
  S.groupMessages = function (msgs, now) {
    var out = [], prev = null, GAP = 5 * 60 * 1000;
    msgs.forEach(function (m, i) {
      var day = S.dayLabel(m.created_at, now);
      if (!prev || S.dayLabel(prev.created_at, now) !== day) out.push({ sep: day });
      var next = msgs[i + 1];
      var sameP = prev && prev.sender === m.sender && S.dayLabel(prev.created_at, now) === day && (new Date(m.created_at) - new Date(prev.created_at)) < GAP;
      var sameN = next && next.sender === m.sender && S.dayLabel(next.created_at, now) === day && (new Date(next.created_at) - new Date(m.created_at)) < GAP;
      out.push({ m: m, first: !sameP, last: !sameN });
      prev = m;
    });
    return out;
  };
  /* îmbină mesaje noi în lista existentă (după id), păstrând ordinea crescătoare; elimină mesajele temporare înlocuite */
  S.mergeMessages = function (list, incoming) {
    var by = {}; list.forEach(function (m) { by[m.id] = m; });
    incoming.forEach(function (m) { by[m.id] = m; });
    return Object.keys(by).map(function (k) { return by[k]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return new Date(a.created_at) - new Date(b.created_at) || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1); });
  };
  S.previewText = function (last) {
    if (!last) return "";
    var p = last.mine ? "Tu: " : "";
    if (last.deleted) return p + "Mesaj șters";
    if (last.kind === "image") return p + "📷 Poză";
    if (last.kind === "video") return p + "🎬 Video";
    if (last.kind === "post") return p + "↗ Postare distribuită";
    return p + (last.body || "");
  };
  S.sortChats = function (a) {
    return a.slice().sort(function (x, y) {
      return (y.pinned ? 1 : 0) - (x.pinned ? 1 : 0) || new Date(y.last_at) - new Date(x.last_at);
    });
  };

  /* ---------- lista de conversații (camera Chat) ---------- */
  var chats = [], listEl = null, filter = "", showArch = false, listTimer = 0, loadedAt = 0;
  function rowHTML(c) {
    var o = c.other, l = c.last || {}, un = c.unread || 0;
    return '<div class="ch-r' + (un ? " un" : "") + '" data-c="' + e2(c.id) + '">' + S.av(o, 54, { nav: false }) +
      '<div class="ch-m"><div class="ch-t"><b>' + e2(o.name || o.handle || "") + '</b><small>' + (l.at ? S.ago(l.at) : "") + '</small></div>' +
      '<div class="ch-p"><span>' + e2(S.previewText(l)) + '</span>' + (c.muted ? '<i class="mu">' + I("belloff") + '</i>' : "") + (c.pinned ? '<i class="pn">' + I("pin") + '</i>' : "") + (un ? '<b class="bd">' + (un > 99 ? "99+" : un) + '</b>' : "") + '</div></div></div>';
  }
  function paintList() {
    if (!listEl) return;
    var f = filter.toLowerCase(), act = chats.filter(function (c) { return !c.archived; }), arch = chats.filter(function (c) { return c.archived; });
    var match = function (c) { return !f || (c.other.name || "").toLowerCase().indexOf(f) >= 0 || (c.other.handle || "").toLowerCase().indexOf(f) >= 0; };
    var rows = (showArch ? arch : act).filter(match);
    var box = listEl.querySelector("#chL");
    box.innerHTML = rows.length ? S.sortChats(rows).map(rowHTML).join("") :
      '<div class="fd-empty"><span>' + I("chat") + '</span><h3>' + (f ? "Niciun rezultat" : showArch ? "Nicio conversație arhivată" : "Încă nu ai conversații") + '</h3><p>' + (f ? "" : "Apasă pe creion pentru un mesaj nou sau scrie cuiva din profilul lui.") + '</p></div>';
    var a = listEl.querySelector("#chA"); a.hidden = showArch ? false : !arch.length; a.textContent = showArch ? "← Conversații" : "Arhivate (" + arch.length + ")";
    var un = chats.reduce(function (s, c) { return s + (c.muted || c.archived ? 0 : (c.unread || 0)); }, 0);
    S.emit("chat-unread", un);
  }
  function loadList() {
    return S.api("chat_list").then(function (r) {
      loadedAt = Date.now();
      if (r.ok) { chats = r.data || []; paintList(); }
      else if (listEl) listEl.querySelector("#chL").innerHTML = '<p class="note">' + e2(r.err) + '</p>';
    });
  }
  function chatMenu(c) {
    var o = c.other;
    S.menu([
      { ic: "pin", t: c.pinned ? "Anulează fixarea" : "Fixează în partea de sus", fn: function () { setField(c, "pinned", !c.pinned); } },
      { ic: c.muted ? "bell" : "belloff", t: c.muted ? "Activează notificările" : "Dezactivează notificările", fn: function () { setField(c, "muted", !c.muted); } },
      { ic: "book", t: c.archived ? "Scoate din arhivă" : "Arhivează", fn: function () { setField(c, "archived", !c.archived); } },
      { ic: "user", t: "Vezi profilul", fn: function () { S.openProfile(o.id); } },
      { ic: "trash", t: "Șterge conversația", s: "Doar pentru tine", danger: true, fn: function () {
        ask("Ștergi conversația?", "Istoricul dispare doar la tine. Reapare dacă primești un mesaj nou.", "Șterge").then(function (ok) {
          if (ok) S.api("chat_delete_conv", { p_conv: c.id }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); chats = chats.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== c.id; }); paintList(); }); }); } }
    ], o.name || "Conversație");
  }
  function setField(c, field, on) {
    S.api("chat_set", { p_conv: c.id, p_field: field, p_on: on }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); c[field] = on; paintList(); });
  }

  Rooms.chat = {
    mount: function (el) {
      listEl = el; filter = ""; showArch = false;
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="chl"><div class="chl-h"><h2>Mesaje</h2><button class="ib" data-k="new" aria-label="Mesaj nou">' + I("edit") + '</button></div>' +
        '<div class="chl-s">' + I("search") + '<input id="chQ" placeholder="Caută în conversații" maxlength="40" autocomplete="off"></div>' +
        '<button class="lnk chl-a" id="chA" data-k="arch" hidden></button><div id="chL"><div class="sk"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div></div></div>';
      el.addEventListener("input", function (e) { if (e.target.id === "chQ") { filter = e.target.value.trim(); paintList(); } });
      var pressT = 0, pressed = null, longDone = false;
      el.addEventListener("touchstart", function (e) { var r = e.target.closest(".ch-r"); if (!r) return; pressed = r; longDone = false; clearTimeout(pressT); pressT = setTimeout(function () { longDone = true; var c = chats.filter(function (x) { return x.id === r.dataset.c; })[0]; if (c) { hap(1); chatMenu(c); } }, 480); }, { passive: true });
      var cancel = function () { clearTimeout(pressT); };
      el.addEventListener("touchmove", cancel, { passive: true }); el.addEventListener("touchend", cancel, { passive: true });
      el.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        var b = e.target.closest("[data-k]"), r = e.target.closest(".ch-r");
        if (b && b.dataset.k === "new") return S.pickFriends("Mesaj nou").then(function (a) { if (a && a[0]) openWith(a[0].id); });
        if (b && b.dataset.k === "arch") { showArch = !showArch; paintList(); return; }
        if (r) { if (longDone) { longDone = false; return; } var c = chats.filter(function (x) { return x.id === r.dataset.c; })[0]; if (c) openConv(c.id, c.other); }
      });
      loadList(); clearInterval(listTimer); listTimer = setInterval(function () { if (listEl && listEl.isConnected && !document.hidden) loadList(); }, 8000);
    },
    show: function () { if (Date.now() - loadedAt > 3000) loadList(); },
    hide: function () { }
  };
  S.on("reset", function () { chats = []; listEl = null; clearInterval(listTimer); closeConv(); stopBubblePoll(); });

  /* ---------- deschidere conversații ---------- */
  function openWith(userId) {
    return S.api("dm_open", { target: userId }).then(function (r) {
      if (!r.ok) { toast(r.err); return; }
      openConv(r.data.id, r.data.other);
    });
  }
  S.chatOpen = function (userId) {
    if (!userId) return;
    if (!S.isMe(userId)) { try { go("chat"); } catch (e) { } openWith(userId); }
  };
  S.chatOpenById = function (convId) {
    try { go("chat"); } catch (e) { }
    return S.api("chat_list").then(function (r) {
      var c = r.ok ? (r.data || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === convId; })[0] : null;
      if (c) openConv(c.id, c.other); else if (S.bubble && S.bubble.conv === convId) openConv(convId, S.bubble.other);
    });
  };
  S.shareToChat = function (post) {
    S.pickFriends("Trimite postarea către").then(function (a) {
      if (!a || !a[0]) return;
      S.api("dm_open", { target: a[0].id }).then(function (r) {
        if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err);
        S.api("chat_send", { p_conv: r.data.id, p_body: "", p_kind: "post", p_media: [], p_reply: null, p_post: post.id }).then(function (x) { toast(x.ok ? "Trimis lui " + (a[0].name || "prieten") : x.err); });
      });
    });
  };

  /* ---------- conversația ---------- */
  var cv = null;
  function closeConv() { if (cv) cv.close(); }

  function openConv(convId, other) {
    if (cv) cv.close();
    var st = { id: convId, other: other, msgs: [], pins: [], otherRead: null, reply: null, edit: null, more: true, loading: false, muted: false, pinned: false, timer: 0, closed: false, showTime: {} };
    var w = document.createElement("div"); w.className = "cv";
    w.innerHTML = '<div class="cv-h"><button class="ib" data-k="back" aria-label="Înapoi">' + I("back") + '</button><button class="cv-w" data-k="prof">' + S.av(other, 38, { nav: false }) + '<span><b>' + e2(other.name || other.handle || "") + '</b><small>@' + e2(other.handle || "") + '</small></span></button><button class="ib" data-k="menu" aria-label="Mai multe">' + I("dots") + '</button></div>' +
      '<button class="cv-pin" hidden data-k="pins"></button><div class="cv-b" id="cvB"><div class="sk"><i></i><i></i></div></div>' +
      '<div class="cv-rp" hidden></div><div class="cv-in"><button class="ib" data-k="att" aria-label="Atașează">' + I("image") + '</button><div class="cv-ta"><textarea id="cvT" rows="1" maxlength="4000" placeholder="Mesaj…"></textarea></div><button class="ib snd" data-k="send" aria-label="Trimite">' + I("share") + '</button></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(w);
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { requestAnimationFrame(function () { w.classList.add("on"); }); });
    var B = w.querySelector("#cvB"), T = w.querySelector("#cvT"), RP = w.querySelector(".cv-rp"), PN = w.querySelector(".cv-pin");
    var offBack = S.addBack(function () { if (!w.isConnected) return false; close(); return true; });
    var offNav = S.on("navigate", function () { close(); });
    function close() {
      if (st.closed) return; st.closed = true; clearInterval(st.timer); offBack(); offNav(); cv = null;
      S.api("chat_read", { p_conv: convId }); w.classList.remove("on"); setTimeout(function () { w.remove(); }, 220); loadList();
    }
    cv = { close: close, id: convId };

    function msgHTML(it) {
      if (it.sep) return '<div class="ds"><span>' + e2(it.sep) + '</span></div>';
      var m = it.m, cls = "mg " + (m.mine ? "mine" : "their") + (it.first ? " first" : "") + (it.last ? " last" : "") + (m.pending ? " pend" : "") + (m.failed ? " fail" : "");
      var body = "";
      if (m.deleted) body = '<span class="del">' + I("ban") + 'Mesaj șters</span>';
      else {
        if (m.reply) body += '<div class="qt"><b>' + (m.reply.sender === S.me().id ? "Tu" : e2(other.name || "")) + '</b><span>' + (m.reply.deleted ? "Mesaj șters" : e2(m.reply.kind === "text" ? m.reply.body : m.reply.kind === "image" ? "📷 Poză" : m.reply.kind === "video" ? "🎬 Video" : "↗ Postare")) + '</span></div>';
        if (m.kind === "image" || m.kind === "video") body += '<div class="mm' + ((m.media || []).length > 1 ? " multi" : "") + '">' + (m.media || []).slice(0, 4).map(function (x, i) { return x.type === "video" ? '<div class="mi" data-lb="' + i + '"><video src="' + e2(x.url) + '" ' + (x.poster ? 'poster="' + e2(x.poster) + '"' : "") + ' playsinline preload="metadata"></video><span class="vi">' + I("play") + '</span></div>' : '<div class="mi" data-lb="' + i + '"><img src="' + e2(x.url) + '" loading="lazy" alt="" draggable="false"></div>'; }).join("") + '</div>';
        if (m.kind === "post" && m.ref) body += '<button class="rf" data-prof="' + e2(m.ref.author_id) + '">' + (m.ref.thumb ? '<img src="' + e2(m.ref.thumb) + '" alt="">' : '<span class="rf-i">' + I(m.ref.poll ? "pollI" : "feed") + '</span>') + '<span class="rf-t"><b>' + e2(m.ref.author || "") + '</b><small>' + e2(m.ref.poll && !m.ref.text ? "Sondaj" : (m.ref.text || "Postare")) + '</small></span></button>';
        if (m.body) body += '<div class="tx">' + S.rich(m.body) + '</div>';
      }
      var rx = (m.reactions || []).length ? '<div class="rx">' + m.reactions.map(function (r) { return '<span class="' + (r.mine ? "me" : "") + '" data-rx="' + e2(r.emoji) + '">' + e2(r.emoji) + (r.n > 1 ? "<i>" + r.n + "</i>" : "") + '</span>'; }).join("") + '</div>' : "";
      var meta = '<div class="mt' + (st.showTime[m.id] || m.failed ? " on" : "") + '">' + S.clock(m.created_at) + (m.edited_at ? " · editat" : "") + (m.failed ? " · netrimis, atinge pentru reîncercare" : "") + '</div>';
      return '<div class="' + cls + '" data-id="' + e2(m.id) + '"><div class="bb' + (m.pinned ? " pnd" : "") + '">' + (m.pinned ? '<i class="pi">' + I("pin") + '</i>' : "") + body + '</div>' + rx + meta + (m.__status ? '<div class="sn">' + m.__status + '</div>' : "") + '</div>';
    }
    function paint(keepBottom) {
      var near = B.scrollHeight - B.scrollTop - B.clientHeight < 140, prevH = B.scrollHeight, prevT = B.scrollTop;
      var items = S.groupMessages(st.msgs), lastMine = -1;
      st.msgs.forEach(function (m, i) { if (m.mine && !m.pending && !m.failed) lastMine = i; });
      st.msgs.forEach(function (m, i) { m.__status = (i === lastMine) ? (st.otherRead && new Date(st.otherRead) >= new Date(m.created_at) ? "Văzut" : "Trimis") : ""; });
      B.innerHTML = (st.more && st.msgs.length ? '<button class="lnk cv-old" data-k="old">Mesaje mai vechi</button>' : "") + (items.length ? items.map(msgHTML).join("") : '<p class="note">Spune salut 👋</p>');
      if (keepBottom || near) B.scrollTop = B.scrollHeight; else if (st.keepOffset) { B.scrollTop = prevT + (B.scrollHeight - prevH); st.keepOffset = false; }
      var p = st.pins[0];
      PN.hidden = !p; if (p) PN.innerHTML = I("pin") + '<span>' + e2(p.body || (p.kind === "image" ? "📷 Poză" : p.kind === "video" ? "🎬 Video" : "Postare")) + '</span><small>' + st.pins.length + '</small>';
    }
    function apply(d, initial) {
      var inc = (d.messages || []).slice().reverse();
      var hadIds = {}; st.msgs.forEach(function (m) { hadIds[m.id] = 1; });
      var fresh = inc.filter(function (m) { return !hadIds[m.id] && !m.mine; });
      st.msgs = S.mergeMessages(st.msgs.filter(function (m) { return !m.pending || !inc.some(function (x) { return x.mine && x.body === m.body && x.kind === m.kind && Math.abs(new Date(x.created_at) - new Date(m.created_at)) < 60000; }); }), inc);
      st.pins = d.pins || []; st.otherRead = d.other_read_at; st.pinned = d.pinned; st.muted = d.muted;
      if (initial) st.more = inc.length >= 40;
      paint(initial || fresh.length > 0);
      if (fresh.length) { S.api("chat_read", { p_conv: convId }); }
      if (d.blocked) w.querySelector(".cv-in").classList.add("off");
    }
    function load() {
      return S.api("chat_messages", { p_conv: convId, p_before: null, p_limit: 40 }).then(function (r) {
        if (st.closed) return;
        if (!r.ok) { B.innerHTML = '<p class="note">' + e2(r.err) + '</p>'; return; }
        apply(r.data, st.msgs.length === 0); if (st.msgs.length === 0) st.more = false;
      });
    }
    function older() {
      if (st.loading || !st.more || !st.msgs.length) return; st.loading = true;
      S.api("chat_messages", { p_conv: convId, p_before: st.msgs[0].created_at, p_limit: 40 }).then(function (r) {
        st.loading = false; if (!r.ok || st.closed) return;
        var inc = (r.data.messages || []).slice().reverse(); if (inc.length < 40) st.more = false;
        st.msgs = S.mergeMessages(st.msgs, inc); st.keepOffset = true; paint(false);
      });
    }
    B.addEventListener("scroll", function () { if (B.scrollTop < 60) older(); }, { passive: true });

    /* trimitere */
    function setBar() {
      var t = st.edit ? "Editezi mesajul" : st.reply ? "Răspunzi: " + (st.reply.body || (st.reply.kind === "image" ? "Poză" : st.reply.kind === "video" ? "Video" : "Postare")) : "";
      RP.hidden = !t; RP.innerHTML = t ? '<span>' + e2(t.slice(0, 70)) + '</span><button class="ib sm" data-k="xbar" aria-label="Anulează">' + I("close") + '</button>' : "";
    }
    function resize() { T.style.height = "auto"; T.style.height = Math.min(T.scrollHeight, 120) + "px"; }
    T.addEventListener("input", resize);
    function send() {
      var txt = T.value.trim(); if (!txt) return;
      if (st.edit) {
        var em = st.edit; st.edit = null; setBar(); T.value = ""; resize();
        S.api("chat_edit", { p_msg: em.id, p_body: txt }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); st.msgs = S.mergeMessages(st.msgs, [r.data]); paint(false); }); return;
      }
      var tmp = { id: "tmp" + S.rand(), conv: convId, sender: S.me().id, mine: true, kind: "text", body: txt, media: [], reply: st.reply ? { id: st.reply.id, sender: st.reply.sender, kind: st.reply.kind, body: st.reply.body, deleted: false } : null, created_at: new Date().toISOString(), pending: true, reactions: [] };
      var replyId = st.reply ? st.reply.id : null; st.reply = null; setBar(); T.value = ""; resize();
      st.msgs.push(tmp); paint(true); doSend(tmp, replyId);
    }
    function doSend(tmp, replyId) {
      tmp.pending = true; tmp.failed = false;
      S.api("chat_send", { p_conv: convId, p_body: tmp.body, p_kind: "text", p_media: [], p_reply: replyId, p_post: null }).then(function (r) {
        st.msgs = st.msgs.filter(function (m) { return m.id !== tmp.id; });
        if (!r.ok) { tmp.pending = false; tmp.failed = true; tmp._replyId = replyId; st.msgs.push(tmp); toast(r.err); }
        else st.msgs = S.mergeMessages(st.msgs, [r.data]);
        paint(true);
      });
    }
    function attach() {
      S.pick("image/*,video/mp4,video/webm,video/quicktime", true).then(function (fs) {
        if (!fs.length) return; toast("Se trimite…");
        var jobs = fs.slice(0, 10).map(function (f) {
          if (f.type.indexOf("video") === 0) { if (f.size > S.MAX_VIDEO) { toast("Videoclip prea mare (max 45 MB)"); return Promise.resolve(null); } return S.upload(f).then(function (u) { return { type: "video", url: u.url }; }); }
          return S.compress(f, 1600, 0.82).then(function (c) { return S.upload(c.blob).then(function (u) { return { type: "image", url: u.url, w: c.w, h: c.h }; }); });
        });
        Promise.all(jobs.map(function (j) { return j.catch(function (er) { toast(er.message || "Încărcare eșuată"); return null; }); })).then(function (up) {
          up = up.filter(Boolean); if (!up.length) return;
          var kind = up.every(function (x) { return x.type === "video"; }) ? "video" : "image";
          S.api("chat_send", { p_conv: convId, p_body: "", p_kind: kind, p_media: up, p_reply: null, p_post: null }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); st.msgs = S.mergeMessages(st.msgs, [r.data]); paint(true); });
        });
      });
    }

    /* acțiuni pe mesaj */
    function find(id) { return st.msgs.filter(function (m) { return m.id === id; })[0]; }
    function react(m, emoji) {
      var mine = (m.reactions || []).filter(function (r) { return r.mine; })[0], on = !(mine && mine.emoji === emoji);
      S.api("chat_react", { p_msg: m.id, p_emoji: emoji, p_on: on }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); st.msgs = S.mergeMessages(st.msgs, [r.data]); paint(false); });
    }
    function forward(m) {
      S.pickFriends("Redirecționează către").then(function (a) {
        if (!a || !a[0]) return;
        S.api("dm_open", { target: a[0].id }).then(function (d) {
          if (!d.ok) return toast(d.err);
          var args = m.kind === "post" && m.ref ? { p_conv: d.data.id, p_body: "", p_kind: "post", p_media: [], p_reply: null, p_post: m.ref.post_id } :
            m.kind === "text" ? { p_conv: d.data.id, p_body: m.body, p_kind: "text", p_media: [], p_reply: null, p_post: null } : null;
          if (!args) return toast("Doar textul și postările se pot redirecționa");
          S.api("chat_send", args).then(function (x) { toast(x.ok ? "Redirecționat" : x.err); });
        });
      });
    }
    function msgMenu(m) {
      if (m.pending || String(m.id).indexOf("tmp") === 0) return;
      var sh = S.sheet('<div class="qr">' + QUICK.map(function (e) { return '<button data-e="' + e + '">' + e + '</button>'; }).join("") + '</div><div class="qm"></div>', { cls: "menu" });
      sh.body.querySelector(".qr").addEventListener("click", function (ev) { var b = ev.target.closest("[data-e]"); if (!b) return; sh.close(); react(m, b.dataset.e); });
      var items = [
        !m.deleted ? { ic: "chat", t: "Răspunde", fn: function () { st.reply = m; st.edit = null; setBar(); T.focus(); } } : null,
        !m.deleted && m.body ? { ic: "copy", t: "Copiază", fn: function () { S.copy(m.body); } } : null,
        !m.deleted ? { ic: "pin", t: m.pinned ? "Anulează fixarea" : "Fixează mesajul", fn: function () { S.api("chat_pin_msg", { p_conv: convId, p_msg: m.id, p_on: !m.pinned }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); load(); }); } } : null,
        !m.deleted && (m.kind === "text" || m.kind === "post") ? { ic: "share", t: "Redirecționează", fn: function () { forward(m); } } : null,
        m.mine && m.kind === "text" && !m.deleted ? { ic: "edit", t: "Editează", fn: function () { st.edit = m; st.reply = null; T.value = m.body; resize(); setBar(); T.focus(); } } : null,
        { ic: "trash", t: "Șterge pentru mine", fn: function () { S.api("chat_delete", { p_msg: m.id, p_all: false }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); st.msgs = st.msgs.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== m.id; }); paint(false); }); } },
        m.mine && !m.deleted ? { ic: "trash", t: "Șterge pentru toți", danger: true, fn: function () { ask("Ștergi pentru toți?", "Mesajul dispare și la celălalt.", "Șterge").then(function (ok) { if (ok) S.api("chat_delete", { p_msg: m.id, p_all: true }).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) return toast(r.err); load(); }); }); } } : null,
        !m.mine && !m.deleted ? { ic: "flag", t: "Raportează mesajul", danger: true, fn: function () { S.report("message", m.id, other); } } : null
      ].filter(Boolean);
      var qm = sh.body.querySelector(".qm");
      qm.innerHTML = items.map(function (it, i) { return '<button class="mn' + (it.danger ? " danger" : "") + '" data-i="' + i + '"><span class="mi">' + I(it.ic) + '</span><span class="mt"><b>' + e2(it.t) + '</b></span></button>'; }).join("");
      qm.addEventListener("click", function (ev) { var b = ev.target.closest(".mn"); if (!b) return; var it = items[+b.dataset.i]; sh.close(); setTimeout(it.fn, 60); });
    }

    /* gesturi: apăsare lungă = meniu, glisare dreapta = răspuns, atingere = oră */
    var sx = 0, sy = 0, pt = 0, longD = false, sw = null;
    B.addEventListener("touchstart", function (e) {
      var g = e.target.closest(".mg"); if (!g) return; sw = g; sx = e.touches[0].clientX; sy = e.touches[0].clientY; longD = false; clearTimeout(pt);
      pt = setTimeout(function () { longD = true; var m = find(g.dataset.id); if (m) { hap(1); msgMenu(m); } }, 450);
    }, { passive: true });
    B.addEventListener("touchmove", function (e) { var dx = e.touches[0].clientX - sx, dy = e.touches[0].clientY - sy; if (Math.abs(dx) > 10 || Math.abs(dy) > 10) clearTimeout(pt); if (sw && dx > 0 && Math.abs(dy) < 24) sw.style.transform = "translateX(" + Math.min(70, dx) + "px)"; }, { passive: true });
    B.addEventListener("touchend", function (e) {
      clearTimeout(pt); if (!sw) return; var dx = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - sx; sw.style.transform = "";
      if (dx > 64 && !longD) { var m = find(sw.dataset.id); if (m && !m.deleted && !m.pending) { st.reply = m; st.edit = null; setBar(); T.focus(); hap(0); } }
      sw = null;
    }, { passive: true });
    B.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      if (longD) { longD = false; return; }
      var k = e.target.closest("[data-k]"); if (k && k.dataset.k === "old") return older();
      var lb = e.target.closest("[data-lb]"), g = e.target.closest(".mg");
      if (lb && g) { var m = find(g.dataset.id); if (m) S.openLightbox(m.media, +lb.dataset.lb); return; }
      var rx = e.target.closest("[data-rx]"); if (rx && g) { var mm = find(g.dataset.id); if (mm) react(mm, rx.dataset.rx); return; }
      if (e.target.closest("[data-prof]")) return;
      if (g) { var m2 = find(g.dataset.id); if (!m2) return; if (m2.failed) { st.msgs = st.msgs.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== m2.id; }); st.msgs.push(m2); return doSend(m2, m2._replyId || null); } st.showTime[m2.id] = !st.showTime[m2.id]; paint(false); }
    });

    function pinsSheet() {
      var sh = S.sheet(st.pins.map(function (p) { return '<button class="mn" data-id="' + e2(p.id) + '"><span class="mi">' + I("pin") + '</span><span class="mt"><b>' + e2(p.body || (p.kind === "image" ? "📷 Poză" : p.kind === "video" ? "🎬 Video" : "Postare")) + '</b><small>' + S.ago(p.created_at) + '</small></span></button>'; }).join("") || '<p class="note">Niciun mesaj fixat.</p>', { title: "Mesaje fixate", cls: "menu" });

    }
    function searchSheet() {

        });
      }, 300);
      sh.body.querySelector("#csQ").addEventListener("input", run);
      sh.body.addEventListener("click", function (e) { var b = e.target.closest("[data-id]"); if (!b) return; sh.close(); var el = B.querySelector('[data-id="' + b.dataset.id + '"]'); if (el) { el.scrollIntoView({ block: "center" }); el.classList.add("hl"); setTimeout(function () { el.classList.remove("hl"); }, 1500); } else toast("Mesajul e mai vechi: derulează în sus"); });

    function headMenu() {

"Conversație fixată" : "Fixare anulată"); }); } },
        { ic: st.muted ? "bell" : "belloff", t: st.muted ?       ], other.name || "Conversație");
    }

    w.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest("[data-k]"); if (!b) return;
      var k = b.dataset.k;
      if (k === "back") return close();
      if (k === "prof") { S.emit("navigate"); return S.openProfile(other.id); }
      if (k === "menu") return headMenu();

    });
    T.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) { if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey && !e.isComposing) { e.preventDefault(); send(); } });
    load();
